# S8.8 — Storey previews, the any-program preset, and the preset panel Implementation Plan

> **Status:** Done (controller, D-108; pending the owner's reading) · **Date:** 2026-10-02 · **Roadmap:** [S8.8](2026-09-30-implementation-roadmap.md) (revision stage after S8.7, before S6) · **Decisions:** D-017, D-024, D-026, D-056, D-058, D-061, D-062, D-063, D-064, D-065, D-068, D-091, D-095, D-098, D-106, D-107, D-108; [ADR-014](../decisions/ADR-014-program-types-and-department-zoning.md) (the any-program preset, Task 8), [ADR-015](../decisions/ADR-015-plans-per-storey.md) (the storey preview height, Task 8), [ADR-006](../decisions/ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md)
>
> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Implement the three requests of D-108 as the revision stage S8.8. (1) An *any-program preset*: any preset, built-in or general, can be cast to a preset without a space type, which every preset input of every plan generator accepts; the zones keep the space type of the generator's layout and take the preset's program, a preset that keeps its space type must still match its input (`PresetSpaceTypeMismatch`), and the plan's provenance records every input given an any-program preset, so no program assumption changes silently (GLOBAL.md scientific rule 3). In Grasshopper the cast is the floating parameter *Any Program Preset* (AnyP) in panel *1 Program*. (2) The eighteen built-in preset components move to a panel of their own, *1 Program Presets*. (3) The floors simplified from a storey plan are previewed at the storey's height, as the plan is: storey plans record their base elevation, from which the plan, *Transform Plan*, and simplifier previews draw a storey; plans and floors that are not storeys stay at 0, and buildings are unchanged. Data, Breps, *Convert2BEM* outputs, and every existing snapshot and icon stay unchanged; no component or GUID changes, and one parameter is added. S8.8 ends with version 0.8.7 and the tag `v0.8.7`. After the stage gate the controller verifies a fresh clone of the tag and then asks the owner before pushing `main` and `v0.8.7` (D-108); S6 (Convert2IDF) follows.

**Architecture:** `Lod.Core.Programs.ProgramPreset.SpaceType` becomes `SpaceType?`, with `IsAnyProgram` and `AsAnyProgram()`, which returns the same name, the same `ZoneProgram` object, and the same WWR without a space type; presets are still created typed. `Lod.Core.Plans.PlanBuilder` (the shared helper of `PlanGenerator` and `MultiStoreyPlanGenerator`, S8.7) accepts an any-program preset on every slot, records `Presets` from the slots' space types (unchanged for typed presets), and adds `AnyProgramPreset.{Input}` = the preset's name for each slot given one (`PlanPresets.AnyProgramPresetKeyPrefix`). `MultiStoreyPlanGenerator` records `StoreyElevation`, the sum of the floor heights below a storey, in each storey's provenance, and the new static class `StoreyProvenance` reads it, following operations of exactly one input (*Transform Plan*, every simplifier). In `Lod.Grasshopper`, `PreviewGoo` gains a virtual `StoreyElevation` (0) that `PlanGoo` and `FloorGoo` take from `StoreyProvenance`, and the multi-storey `Output` of `TypologyGeneratorComponent` no longer raises the plans itself; `AnyProgramPresetGoo` and `AnyProgramPresetParameter` carry the cast, the generators' preset inputs (`ProgramPresetParameter`) take it through `PreferredCast`, and every preset input has one shared description (`ComponentSupport.PresetInputDescription`); `ComponentCategories.ProgramPresets` is the panel of `DefaultPresetComponent`. `scripts/make_icons.py` draws one new icon.

**Tech Stack:** C# 12 (`LangVersion` 12.0); .NET SDK ≥ 8 (`global.json` pins 8.0.100 with `rollForward: latestMajor`); `netstandard2.0` core libraries; Clipper2 2.0.0; RhinoCommon and Grasshopper 8.19.25132.1001 (plugin targets `net7.0`); xUnit 2.9.3, xunit.runner.visualstudio 3.1.5, Microsoft.NET.Test.Sdk 17.14.1 (tests target `net8.0`); Windows PowerShell 5.1 for `scripts/verify.ps1`; Python 3.10 or later with Pillow for `scripts/make_icons.py` (the icons in this plan were drawn with Pillow 12.3.0).

## Global Constraints

- **Gate:** S8.7 is complete on `main` (tag `v0.8.6`, D-107), and D-108 is recorded (`3d9ec0b`); this plan is committed to `main` before Task 1 ("docs(plans): add the s8.8 storey previews, any-program preset, and preset panel plan"); it is the only file of that commit. ADR-014 and ADR-015 are accepted. Before Task 1 the working tree is clean (`git status --short` prints nothing).
- Rhino 8 (8.19 or later) only (D-003); stages exchange Grasshopper objects, not JSON (D-017).
- `Lod.Core` and `Lod.Generators` have no RhinoCommon or Grasshopper reference; their tests run with plain `dotnet test`.
- Nullable reference types on; warnings are errors; `.editorconfig` rules are enforced in the build; every public member of `Lod.Core` and `Lod.Generators` has XML docs (CS1591 is an error).
- Generation is deterministic; outputs keep their order. The provenance parameters of a plan stay ordered by key, so `AnyProgramPreset.{Input}` entries come first and `StoreyElevation` follows `StoreyCount`.
- **Explicit semantics (AGENTS.md, D-061).** The absence of a space type is explicit data (`SpaceType` is `null`), never inferred from a name; a zone's space type always comes from the generator's layout (`PlannedZone.SpaceType`), its program from its slot's preset.
- **No silent program change (GLOBAL.md scientific rule 3).** An any-program preset is only ever the user's explicit cast, and every slot given one is recorded in the plan's provenance as `AnyProgramPreset.{Input}`, which every floor and building made from the plan carries in its provenance inputs. A typed preset on another type's input is still the error `PresetSpaceTypeMismatch`, with its message unchanged.
- Numerical tolerances come only from `ToleranceSettings` (ADR-004); S8.8 adds none and changes none, and `ToleranceSettings.cs` is not touched.
- **Existing outputs do not change.** The twenty-five existing snapshots stay byte-identical: plans of typed presets record no new key, and the stepped-band snapshot has no provenance. Every existing test keeps passing; no test is removed. Three existing tests that list a stepped band's or a multi-storey plan's exact provenance gain `StoreyElevation` (Task 4): an added expectation, no check weakened. No existing icon PNG changes.
- **Previews only (D-062, D-108).** The storey elevation moves only the previews of plans and floors: the data stay at elevation 0, and the Breps of *Convert2BEM*, the building previews, and every data output are unchanged.
- **Never weaken a check (AGENTS.md rules 11, 12).** Tasks 1, 2, and 4 start from tests that fail the way each task says. The integration tests of Task 3 pass at once, because Task 2 already accepts the presets; it says to stop if a case fails. No tolerance is widened, no validation check is relaxed, and no test is weakened or removed.
- **No backward compatibility (D-064).** `ProgramPreset.SpaceType` becomes nullable (a public API change; every repository use compiles unchanged). One parameter is added with a new GUID: *Any Program Preset* `24bc6a12-ad06-4c25-bc9d-4e8318b8744c`. No component is added or removed, and no existing component, parameter, input, output, or GUID changes; the eighteen default preset components only change their panel.
- Grasshopper components and parameters are thin adaptors (AGENTS.md): the cast, the slot check, the provenance, and the storey elevation live in `Lod.Core`; Grasshopper reads them.
- Research equivalence rules are not changed (GLOBAL.md scientific rule 7): S8.8 changes no simplifier, aggregator, validation rule, or equivalence rule, so the research documentation needs no edit; ADR-014 and ADR-015 record the choices.
- **Links.** No commit contains a relative link to a file that a later commit creates.
- **Code fences.** Every Markdown file this plan touches keeps balanced code fences: every opening fence line carries a language, every closing fence line is bare, and no file ends inside a block. Text this plan appends to a file is the content of a code block, never the fence lines around it; Tasks 9 and 11 run a check that prints each file's state.
- No simulation inside the pipeline (D-016). No CI (D-005): `scripts/verify.ps1` passes locally before every merge to `main`.
- Commits: `type(scope): summary` in lower case, no agent, model, or tool attribution and no `Co-Authored-By` trailers (D-001, D-002); author identity per D-050.
- **Pushes (D-108).** Slices are rebased onto local `main` and nothing is pushed while the stage runs. After the close-out (Task 11) and its tag, the controller verifies a fresh clone of `v0.8.7` and then asks the owner; `main` and `v0.8.7` are pushed only after the owner says so (Task 12), never with force.
- `.gh` example definitions are made and saved by a person in Rhino 8; agents never write or check them (D-058). S8.8 adds none.
- S8.8 changes these earlier files: `ProgramPreset.cs` (S1, last changed in S4.1), `PlanPresets.cs` (S4.1), `PlanGeneratorTests.cs` (S4.1, last changed in S8.1), `PlanBuilder.cs`, `PlanGenerator.cs` (S2), `MultiStoreyPlanGenerator.cs`, `MultiStoreyPlanGeneratorTests.cs`, `SteppedBandGeneratorTests.cs`, `TypologyGeneratorComponents.cs` (S8.1), and `scripts/make_icons.py` (S4.1) (all last changed in S8.7), `BemGoo.cs` (S2, last changed in S8.4), `ComponentSupport.cs` and `BemParameters.cs` (S2, last changed in S4.1), `PipelineComponents.cs` (S2, last changed in S8.1), `PresetComponents.cs` (S4.1, last changed in S8.4), `ComponentCategories.cs` (S0), and `Directory.Build.props` (S0, last changed in S8.7); and the documents ADR-014 (S8.4), ADR-015, `pipeline.md`, `domain-model.md`, `program-presets.md`, `docs/development/grasshopper-smoke-test.md`, AGENTS.md, README.md, and the roadmap (all last changed in S8.7), and the decision log (last changed by D-108).

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Core.Tests.Programs;

public sealed class AnyProgramPresetTests
{
    public static IEnumerable<object[]> ExamplePresets() =>
        new[] { ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit, ExampleResidentialPresets.Corridor, ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair }
            .Concat(ExampleNonResidentialPresets.SpaceTypes.Select(ExampleNonResidentialPresets.PresetOf))
            .Select(p => new object[] { p });

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(ExamplePresets))]
    public void TheCastHasNoSpaceTypeAndKeepsEveryOtherValue(ProgramPreset typed)
    {
        SpaceType? spaceType = typed.SpaceType;

        ProgramPreset any = typed.AsAnyProgram();

        Assert.Null(any.SpaceType);
        Assert.True(any.IsAnyProgram);
        Assert.Equal(typed.Name, any.Name);
        Assert.Equal(typed.WindowToWallRatio, any.WindowToWallRatio);
        Assert.Same(typed.Program, any.Program);
        Assert.Equal(typed.Program.IsConditioned, any.Program.IsConditioned);
        Assert.Same(typed.Program.Thermostat, any.Program.Thermostat);
        Assert.Equal(typed.Program.Loads, any.Program.Loads);
        Assert.Equal(typed.Program.Loads.Select(l => l.Schedule), any.Program.Loads.Select(l => l.Schedule));
        Assert.False(typed.IsAnyProgram);
        Assert.Equal(spaceType, typed.SpaceType);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ACustomPresetKeepsItsValuesToo()
    {
        PresetValues values = ExampleResidentialPresets.CorridorValues with { Name = "Custom", WindowToWallRatio = 0.45, Conditioned = false };
        ProgramPreset typed = values.ToPreset().Value;

        ProgramPreset any = typed.AsAnyProgram();

        Assert.Equal("Custom", any.Name);
        Assert.Equal(0.45, any.WindowToWallRatio);
        Assert.False(any.Program.IsConditioned);
        Assert.Same(typed.Program, any.Program);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CastingTwiceGivesTheSamePreset()
    {
        ProgramPreset any = ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit.AsAnyProgram();

        Assert.Same(any, any.AsAnyProgram());
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ATypedPresetIsNotAnyProgram()
    {
        Assert.False(ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit.IsAnyProgram);
        Assert.Equal(SpaceType.DwellingUnit, ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit.SpaceType);
    }
}
```

- [x] **Step 4: Implement — replace `src/Lod.Core/Programs/ProgramPreset.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Lod.Core.Common;

namespace Lod.Core.Programs;

/// <summary>
/// A program preset for one space type (D-024): its zone program and window-to-wall ratio (D-026). An any-program preset
/// (<see cref="AsAnyProgram"/>) has no space type and fits every preset input of a plan generator (D-108, ADR-014).
/// </summary>
public sealed class ProgramPreset
{
    private ProgramPreset(string name, SpaceType? spaceType, ZoneProgram program, double windowToWallRatio)
    {
        Name = name;
        SpaceType = spaceType;
        Program = program;
        WindowToWallRatio = windowToWallRatio;
    }

    /// <summary>Preset name; descriptive only (reports, provenance, messages), never used for matching (D-061).</summary>
    public string Name { get; }

    /// <summary>The space type this preset applies to; <c>null</c> for an any-program preset, which applies to the space type of any input it is given.</summary>
    public SpaceType? SpaceType { get; }

    /// <summary>Whether the preset has no space type (<see cref="AsAnyProgram"/>).</summary>
    public bool IsAnyProgram => SpaceType is null;

    /// <summary>Loads and setpoints.</summary>
    public ZoneProgram Program { get; }

    /// <summary>Window-to-wall ratio in [0, 1) used by the plan generator to size the window on every exterior wall of the space (D-026, D-039).</summary>
    public double WindowToWallRatio { get; }

    /// <summary>Creates a preset.</summary>
    /// <param name="name">Non-empty name.</param>
    /// <param name="spaceType">Space type.</param>
    /// <param name="program">Loads and setpoints.</param>
    /// <param name="windowToWallRatio">WWR in [0, 1).</param>
    /// <returns>The preset or validation errors.</returns>
    public static Result<ProgramPreset> Create(string name, SpaceType spaceType, ZoneProgram program, double windowToWallRatio)
    {
        var diagnostics = new List<Diagnostic>();
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(name))
        {
            diagnostics.Add(Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.EmptyName, "A program preset needs a name."));
        }

        if (double.IsNaN(windowToWallRatio) || windowToWallRatio < 0 || windowToWallRatio >= 1)
        {
            diagnostics.Add(Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.WindowToWallRatio, $"WWR must be in [0, 1), got {windowToWallRatio}.", name));
        }

        return Result.FromDiagnostics(() => new ProgramPreset(name, spaceType, program, windowToWallRatio), diagnostics);
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// The same preset without a space type (D-108, ADR-014): the name, the program (loads, schedules, conditioning, setpoints), and the WWR are kept
    /// unchanged. A plan generator accepts it on every preset input, and its zones keep the space type of the generator's layout.
    /// </summary>
    /// <returns>The any-program preset; this preset when it already is one.</returns>
    public ProgramPreset AsAnyProgram() => IsAnyProgram ? this : new ProgramPreset(Name, null, Program, WindowToWallRatio);
}
```

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Core.Tests.Plans;

public sealed class PlanGeneratorTests
{
    private readonly TwoZoneGenerator _generator = new();

    [Fact]
    public void EachZoneGetsThePresetOfItsSlot()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = _generator.Generate(new TwoZoneParameters(3.0), Presets()).Value;

        Assert.Same(ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit.Program, Assert.Single(plan.Zones, z => z.SpaceType == SpaceType.DwellingUnit).Program);
        Assert.Same(ExampleResidentialPresets.Corridor.Program, Assert.Single(plan.Zones, z => z.SpaceType == SpaceType.Corridor).Program);
        Assert.Contains(plan.Provenance.Parameters, p => p.Key == "Presets" && p.Value == "DwellingUnit:Example Dwelling Unit;Corridor:Example Corridor");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryPresetOfTheWrongSpaceTypeIsAnError()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new TwoZoneParameters(3.0), Presets(ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair, ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair));

        Diagnostic[] mismatches = result.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.PresetSpaceTypeMismatch).ToArray();
        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "Unit", "Hall" }, mismatches.Select(d => d.Subject));
        Assert.Equal(
            "Input Unit needs a DwellingUnit preset, got 'Example Stair' (Stair).",
            mismatches[0].Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AnAnyProgramPresetFitsEverySlotAndItsZonesKeepTheLayoutSpaceType()
    {
        ProgramPreset unit = ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair.AsAnyProgram();
        ProgramPreset hall = ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit.AsAnyProgram();

        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new TwoZoneParameters(3.0), Presets(unit, hall));

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess);
        Zone u1 = Assert.Single(result.Value.Zones, z => z.Id.Value == "U1");
        Zone c1 = Assert.Single(result.Value.Zones, z => z.Id.Value == "C1");
        Assert.Equal(SpaceType.DwellingUnit, u1.SpaceType);
        Assert.Equal(SpaceType.Corridor, c1.SpaceType);
        Assert.Same(ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair.Program, u1.Program);
        Assert.Same(ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit.Program, c1.Program);
        WallSurface[] outdoor = result.Value.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors).ToArray();
        Assert.All(outdoor.Where(w => w.Zone.Value == "U1"), w => Assert.Equal(0.1 * w.Area, Assert.Single(w.Windows).Area, 9));
        Assert.All(outdoor.Where(w => w.Zone.Value == "C1"), w => Assert.Equal(0.3 * w.Area, Assert.Single(w.Windows).Area, 9));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ProvenanceRecordsEverySlotThatReceivedAnAnyProgramPreset()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = _generator.Generate(new TwoZoneParameters(3.0), Presets(hall: ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair.AsAnyProgram())).Value;

        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { "AnyProgramPreset.Hall=Example Stair", "FloorHeight=3", "OrientationDegrees=0", "Presets=DwellingUnit:Example Dwelling Unit;Corridor:Example Stair" },
            plan.Provenance.Parameters.Select(p => $"{p.Key}={p.Value}"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PlansOfTypedPresetsRecordNoAnyProgramPreset()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = _generator.Generate(new TwoZoneParameters(3.0), Presets()).Value;

        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { "FloorHeight=3", "OrientationDegrees=0", "Presets=DwellingUnit:Example Dwelling Unit;Corridor:Example Corridor" },
            plan.Provenance.Parameters.Select(p => $"{p.Key}={p.Value}"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ATypedPresetBesideAnAnyProgramPresetMustStillMatchItsSlot()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new TwoZoneParameters(3.0), Presets(ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair, ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair.AsAnyProgram()));

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Diagnostic mismatch = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.PresetSpaceTypeMismatch, mismatch.Code);
        Assert.Equal("Unit", mismatch.Subject);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ANullPresetIsAMissingPreset()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new TwoZoneParameters(3.0), new TwoZonePresets(ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit, null!));

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Assert.Contains(result.Diagnostics, d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.MissingPreset && d.Subject == "Hall");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ALayoutSpaceTypeWithoutASlotIsAMissingPreset()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new TwoZoneParameters(3.0), new UnitOnlyPresets(ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit));

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Assert.Contains(result.Diagnostics, d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.MissingPreset && d.Subject == "C1");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PresetErrorsAreReportedWithParameterErrors()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new TwoZoneParameters(-1.0), Presets(ExampleResidentialPresets.Corridor));

        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, DiagnosticCodes.PresetSpaceTypeMismatch },
            result.Diagnostics.Select(d => d.Code));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AWallShorterThanTheMinimumGetsNoWindowAndAnInfo()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new TwoZoneParameters(3.0, HallWidth: 0.5), Presets());

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess);
        WallSurface[] outdoor = result.Value.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors).ToArray();
        WallSurface[] shortWalls = outdoor.Where(w => w.Length < 1.0).ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(2, shortWalls.Length);
        Assert.All(shortWalls, w => Assert.Empty(w.Windows));
        Assert.All(outdoor.Except(shortWalls), w => Assert.Single(w.Windows));
        Diagnostic[] omitted = result.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.WindowOmitted).ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(shortWalls.Select(w => w.Id.Value), omitted.Select(d => d.Subject));
        Assert.All(omitted, d => Assert.Equal(DiagnosticSeverity.Info, d.Severity));
        Assert.Equal(
            "Outdoor wall C1/W1 is 0.5 m long, below the 1 m minimum for a window (ADR-011); it has no window (0.3 m² of glazing not placed).",
            omitted[0].Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AWallAtTheMinimumKeepsItsWindow()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new TwoZoneParameters(3.0, HallWidth: 1.0), Presets());

        Assert.Empty(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.All(result.Value.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors), w => Assert.Single(w.Windows));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AWindowBelowTheMinimumSizeIsOmitted()
    {
        // The corridor's WWR 0.2 gives a 1.789 m wide window on each 4 m wall, below a 2 m minimum width; the unit's WWR 0.3 gives 2.191 m.
        var generator = new TwoZoneGenerator(new GeometryLimits(1.0, 2.0, 0.3));

        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = generator.Generate(new TwoZoneParameters(3.0), Presets());

        WallSurface[] outdoor = result.Value.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors).ToArray();
        Assert.All(outdoor.Where(w => w.Zone.Value == "C1"), w => Assert.Empty(w.Windows));
        Assert.All(outdoor.Where(w => w.Zone.Value == "U1"), w => Assert.Single(w.Windows));
        Diagnostic[] omitted = result.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.WindowOmitted).ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(3, omitted.Length);
        Assert.All(omitted, d => Assert.StartsWith("C1/", d.Subject));
        Assert.Equal(
            "The window of outdoor wall C1/W1 would be 1.789 m × 1.342 m, below the 2 m × 0.3 m minimum (ADR-011); the wall has no window (2.4 m² of glazing not placed).",
            omitted[0].Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OnlyNonDefaultLimitsAreRecordedInProvenance()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan standard = _generator.Generate(new TwoZoneParameters(3.0), Presets()).Value;
        IGeneratedPlan custom = new TwoZoneGenerator(new GeometryLimits(1.5, 0.3, 0.25)).Generate(new TwoZoneParameters(3.0), Presets()).Value;

        Assert.DoesNotContain(standard.Provenance.Parameters, p => p.Key.StartsWith("Minimum"));
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { "MinimumWindowHeight=0.25", "MinimumWindowWallLength=1.5", "MinimumWindowWidth=0.3" },
            custom.Provenance.Parameters.Where(p => p.Key.StartsWith("Minimum")).Select(p => $"{p.Key}={p.Value}"));
    }

    private static TwoZonePresets Presets(ProgramPreset? unit = null, ProgramPreset? hall = null) =>
        new(unit ?? ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit, hall ?? ExampleResidentialPresets.Corridor);

    private sealed record TwoZoneParameters(double FloorHeight, double HallWidth = 4.0)
        : PlanParameters(FloorHeight, 0.0);

    private record TwoZonePresets(ProgramPreset Unit, ProgramPreset Hall) : PlanPresets
    {
        public override IReadOnlyList<PresetSlot> Slots => new[]
        {
            new PresetSlot(nameof(Unit), SpaceType.DwellingUnit, Unit),
            new PresetSlot(nameof(Hall), SpaceType.Corridor, Hall),
        };
    }

    private sealed record UnitOnlyPresets(ProgramPreset Unit) : TwoZonePresets(Unit, Unit)
    {
        public override IReadOnlyList<PresetSlot> Slots => new[] { new PresetSlot(nameof(Unit), SpaceType.DwellingUnit, Unit) };
    }

    /// <summary>A plan 4 m deep: one 4 m dwelling unit <c>U1</c> west of one corridor <c>C1</c>, 4 m wide unless set otherwise.</summary>
    private sealed class TwoZoneGenerator : PlanGenerator<TwoZoneParameters, TwoZonePresets>
    {
        public TwoZoneGenerator()
            : base(ToleranceSettings.Default)
        {
        }

        public TwoZoneGenerator(GeometryLimits limits)
            : base(ToleranceSettings.Default, limits)
        {
        }

        public override string Name => nameof(TwoZoneGenerator);

        protected override IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(TwoZoneParameters parameters) => Enumerable.Empty<Diagnostic>();

        protected override PlanLayout Layout(TwoZoneParameters parameters) => new(
            Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, 0.0, 4.0 + parameters.HallWidth, 4.0),
            new[]
            {
                new PlannedZone(new ZoneId("U1"), "Unit", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, 0.0, 4.0, 4.0)),
                new PlannedZone(new ZoneId("C1"), "Corridor", SpaceType.Corridor, Polygon2.Rectangle(4.0, 0.0, 4.0 + parameters.HallWidth, 4.0)),
            });

        protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(TwoZoneParameters parameters) => Enumerable.Empty<KeyValuePair<string, string>>();
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Core.Tests.Plans;

/// <summary>The base of generators that return one plan per storey (D-095, ADR-015).</summary>
public sealed class MultiStoreyPlanGeneratorTests
{
    private readonly BandGenerator _generator = new();

    [Fact]
    public void EveryStoreyIsOnePlanBottomUp()
    {
        IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan> plans = _generator.Generate(new BandParameters(3), Presets()).Value;

        Assert.Equal(3, plans.Count);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { 32.0, 24.0, 16.0 }, plans.Select(p => p.Footprint.Area));
        Assert.All(plans, p => Assert.Equal(new[] { "U1", "C1" }, p.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value)));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { 16.0, 12.0, 8.0 }, plans.Select(p => p.Zones[0].FloorArea));
        Assert.All(plans, p => Assert.Equal(3.0, p.FloorHeight));
        Assert.All(plans, p => Assert.Equal(12.5, p.OrientationDegrees));
        Assert.All(plans, p => Assert.All(p.Zones, z => Assert.Equal(0.0, z.Parts[0].Elevation)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ProvenanceRecordsTheGeneratorAndTheStoreyIndex()
    {
        IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan> plans = _generator.Generate(new BandParameters(2), Presets()).Value;

        Assert.All(plans, p => Assert.Equal(nameof(BandGenerator), p.Provenance.Operation));
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { "FloorHeight=3", "HallWidth=4", "OrientationDegrees=12.5", "Presets=DwellingUnit:Example Dwelling Unit;Corridor:Example Corridor", "Storey=0", "Storeys=2" },
            plans[0].Provenance.Parameters.Select(p => $"{p.Key}={p.Value}"));
        Assert.Equal("1", plans[1].Provenance.Parameters.Single(p => p.Key == "Storey").Value);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryStoreyRecordsTheSlotsThatReceivedAnAnyProgramPreset()
    {
        var presets = new BandPresets(ExampleResidentialPresets.Corridor.AsAnyProgram(), ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair.AsAnyProgram());

        IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan> plans = _generator.Generate(new BandParameters(2), presets).Value;

        Assert.All(plans, p => Assert.Equal(new[] { SpaceType.DwellingUnit, SpaceType.Corridor }, p.Zones.Select(z => z.SpaceType)));
        Assert.All(plans, p => Assert.Same(ExampleResidentialPresets.Corridor.Program, p.Zones[0].Program));
        Assert.All(
            plans,
            p => Assert.Equal(
                new[] { "AnyProgramPreset.Hall=Example Stair", "AnyProgramPreset.Unit=Example Corridor" },
                p.Provenance.Parameters.Where(q => q.Key.StartsWith("AnyProgramPreset.")).Select(q => $"{q.Key}={q.Value}")));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AStoreyIsBuiltExactlyAsAPlanGeneratorBuildsItsPlan()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan storey = _generator.Generate(new BandParameters(2), Presets()).Value[0];
        IGeneratedPlan plan = new GroundStoreyGenerator().Generate(new BandParameters(2), Presets()).Value;

        Assert.Equal(TextReport.Describe(plan), TextReport.Describe(storey));
        Assert.Equal(
            plan.Provenance.Parameters.Select(p => $"{p.Key}={p.Value}").Append("Storey=0").OrderBy(p => p, System.StringComparer.Ordinal),
            storey.Provenance.Parameters.Select(p => $"{p.Key}={p.Value}"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ParameterAndPresetErrorsAreReportedOnceBeforeAnyStoreyIsLaidOut()
    {
        var generator = new BandGenerator();

        Result<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>> result = generator.Generate(new BandParameters(0, FloorHeight: -1.0), Presets(ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair));

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, DiagnosticCodes.PresetSpaceTypeMismatch },
            result.Diagnostics.Select(d => d.Code));
        Assert.StartsWith("FloorHeight must be positive", result.Diagnostics[0].Message);
        Assert.Equal("Storeys must be at least 1, got 0.", result.Diagnostics[1].Message);
        Assert.Equal(0, generator.Layouts);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ADiagnosticOfOneStoreyNamesItsStorey()
    {
        // A 0.5 m hall: the hall's south and north walls of every storey are below ADR-011's 1 m minimum and get no window.
        Result<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>> result = _generator.Generate(new BandParameters(2, HallWidth: 0.5), Presets());

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess);
        Diagnostic[] omitted = result.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.WindowOmitted).ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(4, omitted.Length);
        Assert.All(omitted, d => Assert.Equal(DiagnosticSeverity.Info, d.Severity));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "Storey 0: ", "Storey 0: ", "Storey 1: ", "Storey 1: " }, omitted.Select(d => d.Message.Substring(0, 10)));
        IGeneratedPlan[] plans = result.Value.ToArray();
        Assert.All(omitted.Take(2), d => Assert.Contains(plans[0].Surfaces, s => s.Id.Value == d.Subject));
        Assert.Equal(
            "Storey 1: Outdoor wall C1/W1 is 0.5 m long, below the 1 m minimum for a window (ADR-011); it has no window (0.3 m² of glazing not placed).",
            omitted[2].Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AStoreyWhoseZonesDoNotTileFailsTheGenerationAndIsNamed()
    {
        Result<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>> result = _generator.Generate(new BandParameters(3, OverlapStorey: 1), Presets());

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Assert.NotEmpty(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.All(result.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error), d => Assert.StartsWith("Storey 1: ", d.Message));
    }

    private static BandPresets Presets(ProgramPreset? unit = null) =>
        new(unit ?? ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit, ExampleResidentialPresets.Corridor);

    /// <param name="Storeys">Number of storeys, at least 1.</param>
    /// <param name="FloorHeight">Floor-to-floor height, m.</param>
    /// <param name="HallWidth">Width of the hall along x, m.</param>
    /// <param name="OverlapStorey">A storey whose hall overlaps its unit by 0.5 m, or −1 for none.</param>
    private sealed record BandParameters(int Storeys, double FloorHeight = 3.0, double HallWidth = 4.0, int OverlapStorey = -1)
        : PlanParameters(FloorHeight, 12.5);

    private sealed record BandPresets(ProgramPreset Unit, ProgramPreset Hall) : PlanPresets
    {
        public override IReadOnlyList<PresetSlot> Slots => new[]
        {
            new PresetSlot(nameof(Unit), SpaceType.DwellingUnit, Unit),
            new PresetSlot(nameof(Hall), SpaceType.Corridor, Hall),
        };
    }

    private static PlanLayout Storey(BandParameters parameters, int k)
    {
        double south = k;
        double hallWest = k == parameters.OverlapStorey ? 3.5 : 4.0;
        return new PlanLayout(
            Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, south, 4.0 + parameters.HallWidth, 4.0),
            new[]
            {
                new PlannedZone(new ZoneId("U1"), "Unit", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, south, 4.0, 4.0)),
                new PlannedZone(new ZoneId("C1"), "Hall", SpaceType.Corridor, Polygon2.Rectangle(hallWest, south, 4.0 + parameters.HallWidth, 4.0)),
            });
    }

    private static IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Described(BandParameters parameters) => new[]
    {
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(BandParameters.Storeys), parameters.Storeys.ToString(System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(BandParameters.HallWidth), Provenance.Format(parameters.HallWidth)),
    };

    /// <summary>A band of a 4 m unit <c>U1</c> and a hall <c>C1</c> east of it, 4 m deep on storey 0 and 1 m shallower on every storey above.</summary>
    private sealed class BandGenerator : MultiStoreyPlanGenerator<BandParameters, BandPresets>
    {
        public BandGenerator()
            : base(ToleranceSettings.Default)
        {
        }

        public int Layouts { get; private set; }

        public override string Name => nameof(BandGenerator);

        protected override IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(BandParameters parameters)
        {
            if (parameters.Storeys < 1)
            {
                yield return Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, $"Storeys must be at least 1, got {parameters.Storeys}.");
            }
        }

        protected override IReadOnlyList<PlanLayout> Layout(BandParameters parameters)
        {
            Layouts++;
            return Enumerable.Range(0, parameters.Storeys).Select(k => Storey(parameters, k)).ToArray();
        }

        protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(BandParameters parameters) => Described(parameters);
    }

    /// <summary>Storey 0 of <see cref="BandGenerator"/> as a plan generator of one plan.</summary>
    private sealed class GroundStoreyGenerator : PlanGenerator<BandParameters, BandPresets>
    {
        public GroundStoreyGenerator()
            : base(ToleranceSettings.Default)
        {
        }

        public override string Name => nameof(BandGenerator);

        protected override IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(BandParameters parameters) => Enumerable.Empty<Diagnostic>();

        protected override PlanLayout Layout(BandParameters parameters) => Storey(parameters, 0);

        protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(BandParameters parameters) => Described(parameters);
    }
}
```

```csharp
namespace Lod.Generators.Tests;

/// <summary>Any-program presets (D-108) on every preset input of every plan generator, with its example presets and default parameters.</summary>
public sealed class AnyProgramPresetTests
{
    /// <summary>Generates a family's default plans (one, or one per storey) with each example preset replaced by the mapped preset.</summary>
    public delegate Result<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>> Run(Func<ProgramPreset, ProgramPreset> map);

    private static readonly Dictionary<string, Run> Families = new()
    {
        ["Linear"] = m => One(new LinearPlanGenerator(ToleranceSettings.Default).Generate(
            LinearPlanGeneratorTests.Canonical,
            new LinearPlanPresets(m(LinearPlanGeneratorTests.Examples.DwellingUnit), m(LinearPlanGeneratorTests.Examples.Corridor), m(LinearPlanGeneratorTests.Examples.Stair)))),

    private static readonly Dictionary<string, PlanPresets> ExampleRecords = new()
    {
        ["Linear"] = LinearPlanGeneratorTests.Examples,
        ["StairBayBar"] = StairBayBarGeneratorTests.Examples,
        ["StairPair"] = StairPairGeneratorTests.Examples,
        ["GalleryBar"] = GalleryBarGeneratorTests.Examples,
        ["TerraceRow"] = TerraceRowGeneratorTests.Examples,
        ["PointPlate"] = PointPlateGeneratorTests.Examples,
        ["WingedBand"] = WingedBandGeneratorTests.Examples,
        ["OpenCourt"] = OpenCourtGeneratorTests.Examples,
        ["EnclosedCourt"] = EnclosedCourtGeneratorTests.Examples,
        ["OfficePlate"] = OfficePlateGeneratorTests.Examples,
        ["Cafeteria"] = CafeteriaGeneratorTests.Examples,
        ["RadialLobes"] = RadialLobesGeneratorTests.Examples,
        ["BranchingMall"] = BranchingMallGeneratorTests.Examples,
        ["CareHub"] = CareHubGeneratorTests.Examples,
        ["FoyerHalls"] = FoyerHallsGeneratorTests.Examples,
        ["OperatingSuite"] = OperatingSuiteGeneratorTests.Examples,
        ["CourtCluster"] = CourtClusterGeneratorTests.Examples,
        ["SteppedBand"] = SteppedBandGeneratorTests.Examples,
    };

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> FamilyNames() => Families.Keys.Select(k => new object[] { k });

    [Fact]
    public void EveryPlanGeneratorIsCovered()
    {
        Assert.Equal(18, Families.Count);
        Assert.Equal(Families.Keys, ExampleRecords.Keys);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(FamilyNames))]
    public void CastingEveryExamplePresetChangesOnlyTheProvenance(string family)
    {
        IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan> typed = Families[family](p => p).Value;

        IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan> cast = Families[family](p => p.AsAnyProgram()).Value;

        Assert.Equal(typed.Select(TextReport.Describe), cast.Select(TextReport.Describe));
        IEnumerable<string> recorded = ExampleRecords[family].Slots.Select(s => $"{PlanPresets.AnyProgramPresetKeyPrefix}{s.Input}={s.Preset.Name}");
        for (int k = 0; k < typed.Count; k++)
        {
            Assert.Equal(
                typed[k].Provenance.Parameters.Select(p => $"{p.Key}={p.Value}").Concat(recorded).OrderBy(p => p, StringComparer.Ordinal),
                cast[k].Provenance.Parameters.Select(p => $"{p.Key}={p.Value}"));
            Assert.DoesNotContain(typed[k].Provenance.Parameters, p => p.Key.StartsWith(PlanPresets.AnyProgramPresetKeyPrefix, StringComparison.Ordinal));
        }
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(FamilyNames))]
    public void AnotherProgramOnEveryInputKeepsTheLayoutsSpaceTypes(string family)
    {
        ProgramPreset kitchen = ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Kitchen.AsAnyProgram();
        IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan> typed = Families[family](p => p).Value;

        Result<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>> result = Families[family](_ => kitchen);

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, string.Join("\n", result.Diagnostics));
        Assert.Equal(typed.Count, result.Value.Count);
        for (int k = 0; k < typed.Count; k++)
        {
            Assert.Equal(typed[k].Zones.Select(z => (z.Id, z.SpaceType)), result.Value[k].Zones.Select(z => (z.Id, z.SpaceType)));
            Assert.All(result.Value[k].Zones, z => Assert.Same(kitchen.Program, z.Program));
            Assert.All(
                ExampleRecords[family].Slots,
                s => Assert.Contains(result.Value[k].Provenance.Parameters, p => p.Key == PlanPresets.AnyProgramPresetKeyPrefix + s.Input && p.Value == "Example Kitchen"));
        }
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(FamilyNames))]
    public void ATypedPresetOfAnotherSpaceTypeIsStillRejectedOnEveryInput(string family)
    {
        ProgramPreset mechanical = (ExampleResidentialPresets.StairValues with { Name = "Plant", SpaceType = SpaceType.Mechanical }).ToPreset().Value;

        Result<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>> result = Families[family](_ => mechanical);

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Assert.Equal(ExampleRecords[family].Slots.Select(s => s.Input), result.Diagnostics.Select(d => d.Subject));
        Assert.All(result.Diagnostics, d => Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.PresetSpaceTypeMismatch, d.Code));
    }

    private static Result<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>> One(Result<IGeneratedPlan> result) => result.IsSuccess
        ? Result.Success<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>>(new[] { result.Value }, result.Diagnostics)
        : Result.Failure<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>>(result.Diagnostics);
}
```

- [x] **Step 5: Implement — replace `src/Lod.Core/Plans/PlanPresets.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Lod.Core.Programs;

namespace Lod.Core.Plans;

/// <summary>One preset input of a plan generator (D-061): the space type it feeds and the preset given for it.</summary>
/// <param name="Input">Name of the input, used in messages.</param>
/// <param name="SpaceType">The space type this input feeds; the preset must have it, unless it is an any-program preset (D-108).</param>
/// <param name="Preset">The preset given for the input.</param>
public sealed record PresetSlot(string Input, SpaceType SpaceType, ProgramPreset Preset);

/// <summary>
/// The program presets of a plan generator, one per space type it places (D-061). Each typology defines a record with one property per space type
/// and lists them as <see cref="Slots"/>.
/// </summary>
public abstract record PlanPresets
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Prefix of the provenance parameter <c>AnyProgramPreset.{Input}</c>, whose value is the preset's name, that a plan records for each slot given an
    /// any-program preset (<see cref="ProgramPreset.AsAnyProgram"/>, D-108).
    /// </summary>
    public const string AnyProgramPresetKeyPrefix = "AnyProgramPreset.";

    /// <summary>The preset inputs in a fixed order; the order is kept in provenance.</summary>
    public abstract IReadOnlyList<PresetSlot> Slots { get; }
}
```

- [x] **Step 6: Implement — replace `src/Lod.Core/Plans/PlanBuilder.cs` (full file)**

The slot check accepts an any-program preset, `Presets` uses the slot's space type, and `DescribeAnyProgramPresets` adds the keys; nothing else changes.

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Layout;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;

namespace Lod.Core.Plans;

/// <summary>
/// The work shared by <see cref="PlanGenerator{TParameters, TPresets}"/> and <see cref="MultiStoreyPlanGenerator{TParameters, TPresets}"/>: the
/// checks of the common parameters and of every preset slot (D-061), and building one plan from a layout: zones with their presets, surfaces,
/// centred windows within the minimum wall and window geometry (D-026, ADR-011), and provenance.
/// </summary>
internal static class PlanBuilder
{
    /// <summary>Checks <c>FloorHeight</c> and <c>OrientationDegrees</c>, then adds the family's errors and the preset slot errors, in that order.</summary>
    /// <param name="parameters">Parameters.</param>
    /// <param name="familyErrors">Errors of the family's own parameters.</param>
    /// <param name="presets">Presets.</param>
    /// <returns>Every error, empty when the inputs are valid.</returns>
    public static List<Diagnostic> CheckInputs(PlanParameters parameters, IEnumerable<Diagnostic> familyErrors, PlanPresets presets)
    {
        var diagnostics = new List<Diagnostic>();
        if (!IsPositive(parameters.FloorHeight))
        {
            diagnostics.Add(Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, $"FloorHeight must be positive, got {parameters.FloorHeight}."));
        }

        if (double.IsNaN(parameters.OrientationDegrees) || double.IsInfinity(parameters.OrientationDegrees))
        {
            diagnostics.Add(Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, $"OrientationDegrees must be finite, got {parameters.OrientationDegrees}."));
        }

        diagnostics.AddRange(familyErrors);
        diagnostics.AddRange(CheckSlots(presets.Slots));
        return diagnostics;
    }

    /// <summary>Builds one plan from a layout of valid parameters.</summary>
    /// <param name="name">Operation name recorded in provenance.</param>
    /// <param name="layout">Footprint and zones.</param>
    /// <param name="parameters">Validated parameters.</param>
    /// <param name="presets">Checked presets.</param>
    /// <param name="described">The family's parameters for provenance, and any further keys of the caller.</param>
    /// <param name="limits">Minimum wall and window geometry (ADR-011); recorded in provenance when not the default.</param>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    /// <returns>The plan, or errors.</returns>
    public static Result<IGeneratedPlan> Build(
        string name,
        PlanLayout layout,
        PlanParameters parameters,
        PlanPresets presets,
        IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> described,
        GeometryLimits limits,
        ToleranceSettings tolerances)
    {
        var diagnostics = new List<Diagnostic>();
        var zones = new List<Zone>();
        foreach (PlannedZone planned in layout.Zones)
        {
            ProgramPreset? preset = Find(presets, planned.SpaceType);
            if (preset is null)
            {
                diagnostics.Add(Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.MissingPreset, $"No program preset for space type {planned.SpaceType}.", planned.Id.Value));
                continue;
            }

            zones.Add(new Zone(planned.Id, planned.Name, planned.SpaceType, new[] { new ZonePart(planned.Footprint, 0.0, parameters.FloorHeight) }, preset.Program, Enumerable.Empty<ZoneId>()));
        }

        if (diagnostics.Count > 0)
        {
            return Result.Failure<IGeneratedPlan>(diagnostics);
        }

        Result<IReadOnlyList<Surface>> surfaces = new LayoutSurfaceBuilder(tolerances)
            .Build(layout.Footprint, layout.Zones.Select(z => new LayoutZone(z.Id, z.Footprint)).ToArray(), parameters.FloorHeight);
        if (!surfaces.IsSuccess)
        {
            return Result.Failure<IGeneratedPlan>(surfaces.Diagnostics);
        }

        Dictionary<ZoneId, double> ratios = layout.Zones.ToDictionary(z => z.Id, z => Find(presets, z.SpaceType)!.WindowToWallRatio);
        Result<IReadOnlyList<Surface>> withWindows = LayoutMeasures.PlaceWindowsByRatio(surfaces.Value, id => ratios[id], limits, tolerances);
        IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> recorded = described
            .Concat(new[]
            {
                new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(PlanParameters.FloorHeight), Provenance.Format(parameters.FloorHeight)),
                new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(PlanParameters.OrientationDegrees), Provenance.Format(parameters.OrientationDegrees)),
                new KeyValuePair<string, string>("Presets", string.Join(";", presets.Slots.Select(s => $"{s.SpaceType}:{s.Preset.Name}"))),
            })
            .Concat(DescribeAnyProgramPresets(presets))
            .Concat(DescribeLimits(limits));
        var plan = new GeneratedPlan(layout.Footprint, parameters.FloorHeight, parameters.OrientationDegrees, zones, withWindows.Value, Provenance.Of(name, recorded));
        return Result.Success<IGeneratedPlan>(plan, withWindows.Diagnostics);
    }

    /// <summary>Whether a value is finite and positive.</summary>
    /// <param name="value">The value.</param>
    /// <returns><c>true</c> when positive and finite.</returns>
    public static bool IsPositive(double value) => value > 0 && !double.IsInfinity(value);

    private static IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> DescribeLimits(GeometryLimits limits) => limits == GeometryLimits.Default
        ? Enumerable.Empty<KeyValuePair<string, string>>()
        : new[]
        {
            new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(GeometryLimits.MinimumWindowWallLength), Provenance.Format(limits.MinimumWindowWallLength)),
            new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(GeometryLimits.MinimumWindowWidth), Provenance.Format(limits.MinimumWindowWidth)),
            new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(GeometryLimits.MinimumWindowHeight), Provenance.Format(limits.MinimumWindowHeight)),
        };

    /// <summary>
    /// One entry <c>AnyProgramPreset.{Input}</c> = the preset's name for each slot given an any-program preset, so that a program applied to another
    /// space type than its own is never silent (GLOBAL.md scientific rule 3); none when every preset is typed, which keeps those plans unchanged.
    /// </summary>
    private static IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> DescribeAnyProgramPresets(PlanPresets presets) => presets.Slots
        .Where(s => s.Preset.IsAnyProgram)
        .Select(s => new KeyValuePair<string, string>(PlanPresets.AnyProgramPresetKeyPrefix + s.Input, s.Preset.Name));

    private static IEnumerable<Diagnostic> CheckSlots(IEnumerable<PresetSlot> slots)
    {
        foreach (PresetSlot slot in slots)
        {
            if (slot.Preset is null)
            {
                yield return Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.MissingPreset, $"Input {slot.Input} needs a {slot.SpaceType} preset, got none.", slot.Input);
            }
            else if (!slot.Preset.IsAnyProgram && slot.Preset.SpaceType != slot.SpaceType)
            {
                yield return Diagnostic.Error(
                    DiagnosticCodes.PresetSpaceTypeMismatch,
                    $"Input {slot.Input} needs a {slot.SpaceType} preset, got '{slot.Preset.Name}' ({slot.Preset.SpaceType}).",
                    slot.Input);
            }
        }
    }

    private static ProgramPreset? Find(PlanPresets presets, SpaceType spaceType) =>
        presets.Slots.FirstOrDefault(s => s.SpaceType == spaceType)?.Preset;
}
```

Only the documentation of the `presets` parameter of `Generate` changes in each.

`src/Lod.Core/Plans/PlanGenerator.cs`:

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;

namespace Lod.Core.Plans;

/// <summary>Parameters every plan generator needs. Typology-specific parameters extend this record (spec §6).</summary>
/// <param name="FloorHeight">Floor-to-floor height, m.</param>
/// <param name="OrientationDegrees">Clockwise rotation of plan north from true north, degrees.</param>
public abstract record PlanParameters(double FloorHeight, double OrientationDegrees);

/// <summary>A zone proposed by a generator's layout step.</summary>
/// <param name="Id">Zone ID.</param>
/// <param name="Name">Display name.</param>
/// <param name="SpaceType">Space type; selects the program preset.</param>
/// <param name="Footprint">Zone polygon.</param>
public sealed record PlannedZone(ZoneId Id, string Name, SpaceType SpaceType, Polygon2 Footprint);

/// <summary>The geometric output of a generator's layout step.</summary>
/// <param name="Footprint">Plan boundary.</param>
/// <param name="Zones">Zones tiling the footprint.</param>
public sealed record PlanLayout(Polygon2 Footprint, IReadOnlyList<PlannedZone> Zones);

/// <summary>
/// Base class of plan generators (spec §5). Subclasses only validate their parameters and lay out zones; this class assigns program presets,
/// builds surfaces, places centered windows from each preset's WWR within the minimum wall and window geometry (ADR-011), and records provenance.
/// Generation is deterministic.
/// </summary>
/// <typeparam name="TParameters">The generator's parameter record.</typeparam>
/// <typeparam name="TPresets">The generator's preset record, one preset per space type it places (D-061).</typeparam>
public abstract class PlanGenerator<TParameters, TPresets>
    where TParameters : PlanParameters
    where TPresets : PlanPresets
{
    /// <summary>Initialises the generator with the default minimum wall and window geometry (ADR-011).</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    protected PlanGenerator(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
        : this(tolerances, GeometryLimits.Default)
    {
    }

    /// <summary>Initialises the generator.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    /// <param name="limits">Minimum wall and window geometry (ADR-011); recorded in provenance when not the default.</param>
    protected PlanGenerator(ToleranceSettings tolerances, GeometryLimits limits)
    {
        Tolerances = tolerances;
        Limits = limits;
    }

    /// <summary>Operation name recorded in provenance.</summary>
    public abstract string Name { get; }

    /// <summary>Tolerances.</summary>
    protected ToleranceSettings Tolerances { get; }

    /// <summary>Minimum wall and window geometry (ADR-011).</summary>
    protected GeometryLimits Limits { get; }

    /// <summary>Generates a detailed plan.</summary>
    /// <param name="parameters">Generator parameters.</param>
    /// <param name="presets">Program presets, one per slot; each must have its slot's space type or be an any-program preset (D-108).</param>
    /// <returns>The plan, or errors.</returns>
    public Result<IGeneratedPlan> Generate(TParameters parameters, TPresets presets)
    {
        List<Diagnostic> errors = PlanBuilder.CheckInputs(parameters, Validate(parameters), presets);
        if (errors.Count > 0)
        {
            return Result.Failure<IGeneratedPlan>(errors);
        }

        return PlanBuilder.Build(Name, Layout(parameters), parameters, presets, Describe(parameters), Limits, Tolerances);
    }

    /// <summary>Checks typology-specific parameters.</summary>
    /// <param name="parameters">Parameters.</param>
    /// <returns>Errors, if any.</returns>
    protected abstract IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(TParameters parameters);

    /// <summary>Lays out zones for valid parameters.</summary>
    /// <param name="parameters">Validated parameters.</param>
    /// <returns>Footprint and zones.</returns>
    protected abstract PlanLayout Layout(TParameters parameters);

    /// <summary>Typology-specific parameters for provenance, as invariant-culture text.</summary>
    /// <param name="parameters">Parameters.</param>
    /// <returns>Key-value pairs.</returns>
    protected abstract IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(TParameters parameters);

    /// <summary>Whether a value is finite and positive.</summary>
    /// <param name="value">The value.</param>
    /// <returns><c>true</c> when positive and finite.</returns>
    protected static bool IsPositive(double value) => PlanBuilder.IsPositive(value);
}
```

`src/Lod.Core/Plans/MultiStoreyPlanGenerator.cs`:

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Model;

namespace Lod.Core.Plans;

/// <summary>
/// Base class of plan generators whose plan differs per storey (D-095, ADR-015). The subclass validates its parameters once and lays out every
/// storey; this class checks the common parameters and the presets once, then builds every storey exactly as
/// <see cref="PlanGenerator{TParameters, TPresets}"/> builds its plan (presets, surfaces, centred windows within the minimum wall and window geometry
/// of ADR-011, provenance) and returns the plans bottom-up. Each plan records its storey index as the provenance parameter <c>Storey</c>, from 0 at
/// the bottom. The floor aggregators stack the plans as floor entries with multiplier 1 (ADR-015). Generation is deterministic.
/// </summary>
/// <typeparam name="TParameters">The generator's parameter record.</typeparam>
/// <typeparam name="TPresets">The generator's preset record, one preset per space type it places (D-061).</typeparam>
public abstract class MultiStoreyPlanGenerator<TParameters, TPresets>
    where TParameters : PlanParameters
    where TPresets : PlanPresets
{
    /// <summary>Provenance key of the storey index.</summary>
    public const string StoreyKey = "Storey";

    /// <summary>Initialises the generator with the default minimum wall and window geometry (ADR-011).</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    protected MultiStoreyPlanGenerator(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
        : this(tolerances, GeometryLimits.Default)
    {
    }

    /// <summary>Initialises the generator.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    /// <param name="limits">Minimum wall and window geometry (ADR-011); recorded in provenance when not the default.</param>
    protected MultiStoreyPlanGenerator(ToleranceSettings tolerances, GeometryLimits limits)
    {
        Tolerances = tolerances;
        Limits = limits;
    }

    /// <summary>Operation name recorded in provenance.</summary>
    public abstract string Name { get; }

    /// <summary>Tolerances.</summary>
    protected ToleranceSettings Tolerances { get; }

    /// <summary>Minimum wall and window geometry (ADR-011).</summary>
    protected GeometryLimits Limits { get; }

    /// <summary>Generates the detailed plan of every storey.</summary>
    /// <param name="parameters">Generator parameters.</param>
    /// <param name="presets">Program presets, one per slot; each must have its slot's space type or be an any-program preset (D-108).</param>
    /// <returns>One plan per storey, bottom-up, or errors. A diagnostic of one storey has a message starting with <c>Storey {k}: </c>.</returns>
    public Result<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>> Generate(TParameters parameters, TPresets presets)
    {
        List<Diagnostic> errors = PlanBuilder.CheckInputs(parameters, Validate(parameters), presets);
        if (errors.Count > 0)
        {
            return Result.Failure<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>>(errors);
        }

        var plans = new List<IGeneratedPlan>();
        var diagnostics = new List<Diagnostic>();
        IReadOnlyList<PlanLayout> layouts = Layout(parameters);
        for (int k = 0; k < layouts.Count; k++)
        {
            IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> described = Describe(parameters)
                .Append(new KeyValuePair<string, string>(StoreyKey, k.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)));
            Result<IGeneratedPlan> storey = PlanBuilder.Build(Name, layouts[k], parameters, presets, described, Limits, Tolerances);
            string prefix = $"Storey {k}: ";
            diagnostics.AddRange(storey.Diagnostics.Select(d => d with { Message = prefix + d.Message }));
            if (storey.IsSuccess)
            {
                plans.Add(storey.Value);
            }
        }

        return diagnostics.Any(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error)
            ? Result.Failure<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>>(diagnostics)
            : Result.Success<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>>(plans.AsReadOnly(), diagnostics);
    }

    /// <summary>Checks typology-specific parameters.</summary>
    /// <param name="parameters">Parameters.</param>
    /// <returns>Errors, if any.</returns>
    protected abstract IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(TParameters parameters);

    /// <summary>Lays out every storey for valid parameters.</summary>
    /// <param name="parameters">Validated parameters.</param>
    /// <returns>Footprint and zones of every storey, bottom-up; at least one.</returns>
    protected abstract IReadOnlyList<PlanLayout> Layout(TParameters parameters);

    /// <summary>Typology-specific parameters for provenance, as invariant-culture text; the same for every storey.</summary>
    /// <param name="parameters">Parameters.</param>
    /// <returns>Key-value pairs.</returns>
    protected abstract IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(TParameters parameters);

    /// <summary>Whether a value is finite and positive.</summary>
    /// <param name="value">The value.</param>
    /// <returns><c>true</c> when positive and finite.</returns>
    protected static bool IsPositive(double value) => PlanBuilder.IsPositive(value);
}
```

- Produces: 33 tests. The linear plan with the office program on its corridor and the kitchen program on its stair keeps the zones' space types and takes the presets' programs (1); it validates with every simplifier and aggregator (16); the stepped band with the care-bedroom program on its dwellings and the service program on its stair validates with every simplifier and aggregator, every storey through the simplifier and the storeys stacked with multiplier 1 (16); in each case every floor and the building validate, the aggregation gives no warning, and the building's provenance names every `AnyProgramPreset.*` key. - [x] **Step 1: Write the integration tests — create `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/AnyProgramPresetTests.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Buildings;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Core.Validation;
using Lod.Generators.Linear;
using Lod.Generators.SteppedBand;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Integration.Tests;

/// <summary>
/// Plans built with any-program presets (D-108) through every simplifier and aggregator: the zones keep the layout's space types, their programs
/// come from the presets, every floor and building validates, and the use of each any-program preset stays in the provenance of the building.
/// </summary>
public sealed class AnyProgramPresetTests
{
    private static readonly string[] Simplifiers = { "NoSimplification", "SemanticMerge", "PerimeterCore", "SingleZonePerFloor" };
    private static readonly string[] Aggregators = { "Stack", "StackedFloorZoneMultiplier", "SingleZonePerFloorType", "SingleZoneBuilding" };

    /// <summary>The linear plan with the example dwelling preset, the office program on the corridor, and the kitchen program on the stair.</summary>
    private static readonly LinearPlanPresets LinearPresets = new(
        ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit,
        ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Office.AsAnyProgram(),
        ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Kitchen.AsAnyProgram());

    /// <summary>The stepped band with the care bedroom program on the dwellings, the example corridor, and the service program on the stair.</summary>
    private static readonly SteppedBandPresets BandPresets = new(
        ExampleNonResidentialPresets.CareBedroom.AsAnyProgram(),
        ExampleResidentialPresets.Corridor,
        ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Service.AsAnyProgram());

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> Matrix() =>
        from simplifier in Simplifiers
        from aggregator in Aggregators
        select new object[] { simplifier, aggregator };

    [Fact]
    public void TheZonesOfTheLinearPlanKeepTheirSpaceTypesAndTakeThePresetsPrograms()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = new LinearPlanGenerator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Generate(Pipeline.Canonical, LinearPresets).Value;
        IGeneratedPlan typed = Pipeline.Plan();

        Assert.Equal(typed.Zones.Select(z => (z.Id, z.SpaceType)), plan.Zones.Select(z => (z.Id, z.SpaceType)));
        Assert.Same(ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Office.Program, plan.Zones.Single(z => z.SpaceType == SpaceType.Corridor).Program);
        Assert.Same(ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Kitchen.Program, plan.Zones.Single(z => z.SpaceType == SpaceType.Stair).Program);
        Assert.All(plan.Zones.Where(z => z.SpaceType == SpaceType.DwellingUnit), z => Assert.Same(ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit.Program, z.Program));
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(Matrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationOfTheLinearPlanValidates(string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = new LinearPlanGenerator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Generate(Pipeline.Canonical, LinearPresets).Value;

        AssertValidates(new[] { plan }, simplifier, aggregator, new[] { 3 }, "AnyProgramPreset.Corridor=Example Office", "AnyProgramPreset.Stair=Example Kitchen");
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(Matrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationOfTheSteppedBandValidates(string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan> plans = new SteppedBandGenerator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Generate(SteppedBandParameters.Default, BandPresets).Value;

        AssertValidates(plans, simplifier, aggregator, plans.Select(_ => 1).ToArray(), "AnyProgramPreset.DwellingUnit=Example Care Bedroom", "AnyProgramPreset.Stair=Example Service");
    }

    private static void AssertValidates(IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan> plans, string simplifier, string aggregator, int[] multipliers, params string[] recorded)
    {
        var entries = new List<FloorEntry>();
        for (int k = 0; k < plans.Count; k++)
        {
            Result<IFloor> floor = Pipeline.Simplifier(simplifier).Simplify(plans[k]);
            Assert.True(floor.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", floor.Diagnostics));
            ValidationReport floorReport = new FloorValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(floor.Value);
            Assert.True(floorReport.Passed, floorReport.Describe());
            entries.Add(new FloorEntry(floor.Value, multipliers[k]));
        }

        Result<IGeneratedBuilding> building = Pipeline.Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(entries);
        Assert.True(building.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", building.Diagnostics));
        Assert.DoesNotContain(building.Diagnostics, d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Warning);
        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(building.Value);
        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
        string provenance = building.Value.Provenance.Describe();
        Assert.All(recorded, r => Assert.Contains(r, provenance));
    }
}
```

- Produces: `public static class StoreyProvenance` (`Lod.Core.Plans`) with `public const string ElevationKey = "StoreyElevation"` and `public static double ElevationOf(Provenance)`: the value of `StoreyElevation` in the provenance or, while an operation has exactly one input, in its input's (invariant culture); 0 when none is recorded, and for an operation of several inputs such as an aggregated building. `MultiStoreyPlanGenerator.Generate` records on storey `k` the provenance parameter `StoreyElevation` = the sum of the floor heights below it (`Provenance.Format`: `0`, `3`, `6` for the default stepped band); the plans stay at elevation 0.
```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Buildings;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Core.Simplification;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Core.Tests.Plans;

/// <summary>The storey elevation that the previews read from a plan or floor of a multi-storey generator (D-108, ADR-015).</summary>
public sealed class StoreyProvenanceTests
{
    private readonly TwoStoreyBand _generator = new();

    [Fact]
    public void AStoreyPlanGivesItsRecordedElevation()
    {
        Assert.Equal(6.2, StoreyProvenance.ElevationOf(Storey(6.2)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void APlanThatIsNotAStoreyIsAtZero()
    {
        Assert.Equal(0.0, StoreyProvenance.ElevationOf(Provenance.Of("LinearPlanGenerator", Enumerable.Empty<KeyValuePair<string, string>>())));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TheElevationIsFollowedThroughOperationsOfOneInput()
    {
        Provenance moved = Provenance.Of(PlanTransform.Operation, Enumerable.Empty<KeyValuePair<string, string>>(), Storey(6.0));
        Provenance simplified = Provenance.Of("SemanticMerge", Enumerable.Empty<KeyValuePair<string, string>>(), moved);

        Assert.Equal(6.0, StoreyProvenance.ElevationOf(moved));
        Assert.Equal(6.0, StoreyProvenance.ElevationOf(simplified));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AnOperationOfSeveralInputsIsAtZero()
    {
        Provenance building = Provenance.Of("Stack", Enumerable.Empty<KeyValuePair<string, string>>(), Storey(0.0), Storey(3.0));

        Assert.Equal(0.0, StoreyProvenance.ElevationOf(building));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EverySimplifierKeepsTheElevationOfItsStoreyOnAMovedPlan()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan storey = _generator.Generate(new BandParameters(), Presets).Value[1];
        IPlanSimplifier[] simplifiers =
        {
            new NoSimplification(),
            new SemanticMerge(ToleranceSettings.Default),
            new PerimeterCore(ToleranceSettings.Default, 1.0),
            new SingleZonePerFloor(ToleranceSettings.Default),
        };

        IGeneratedPlan moved = PlanTransform.Apply(storey, 30.0, 5.0, -2.0).Value;

        Assert.Equal(3.0, StoreyProvenance.ElevationOf(storey.Provenance));
        Assert.Equal(3.0, StoreyProvenance.ElevationOf(moved.Provenance));
        Assert.All(simplifiers, s => Assert.Equal(3.0, StoreyProvenance.ElevationOf(s.Simplify(moved).Value.Provenance)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ABuildingOfStoreysIsAtZero()
    {
        FloorEntry[] entries = _generator.Generate(new BandParameters(), Presets).Value
            .Select(p => new FloorEntry(new NoSimplification().Simplify(p).Value, 1))
            .ToArray();

        IGeneratedBuilding building = new Stack(ToleranceSettings.Default).Aggregate(entries).Value;

        Assert.Equal(0.0, StoreyProvenance.ElevationOf(building.Provenance));
    }

    private static BandPresets Presets => new(ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit, ExampleResidentialPresets.Corridor);

    private static Provenance Storey(double elevation) => Provenance.Of(
        "Band",
        new[]
        {
            new KeyValuePair<string, string>("Storey", "2"),
            new KeyValuePair<string, string>(StoreyProvenance.ElevationKey, Provenance.Format(elevation)),
        });

    private sealed record BandParameters() : PlanParameters(3.0, 0.0);

    private sealed record BandPresets(ProgramPreset Unit, ProgramPreset Hall) : PlanPresets
    {
        public override IReadOnlyList<PresetSlot> Slots => new[]
        {
            new PresetSlot(nameof(Unit), SpaceType.DwellingUnit, Unit),
            new PresetSlot(nameof(Hall), SpaceType.Corridor, Hall),
        };
    }

    /// <summary>Two equal storeys of an 8 m unit <c>U1</c> west of a 4 m hall <c>C1</c>, 8 m deep.</summary>
    private sealed class TwoStoreyBand : MultiStoreyPlanGenerator<BandParameters, BandPresets>
    {
        public TwoStoreyBand()
            : base(ToleranceSettings.Default)
        {
        }

        public override string Name => nameof(TwoStoreyBand);

        protected override IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(BandParameters parameters) => Enumerable.Empty<Diagnostic>();

        protected override IReadOnlyList<PlanLayout> Layout(BandParameters parameters) => Enumerable.Range(0, 2)
            .Select(_ => new PlanLayout(
                Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, 0.0, 12.0, 8.0),
                new[]
                {
                    new PlannedZone(new ZoneId("U1"), "Unit", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, 0.0, 8.0, 8.0)),
                    new PlannedZone(new ZoneId("C1"), "Hall", SpaceType.Corridor, Polygon2.Rectangle(8.0, 0.0, 12.0, 8.0)),
                }))
            .ToArray();

        protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(BandParameters parameters) => Enumerable.Empty<KeyValuePair<string, string>>();
    }
}
```
