```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Core.Tests.Plans;

/// <summary>The base of generators that return one plan per storey (D-095, ADR-015).</summary>
public sealed class MultiStoreyPlanGeneratorTests
{
    private readonly BandGenerator _generator = new();

    [Fact]
    public void EveryStoreyIsOnePlanBottomUp()
    {
        IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan> plans = _generator.Generate(new BandParameters(3), Presets()).Value;

        Assert.Equal(3, plans.Count);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { 32.0, 24.0, 16.0 }, plans.Select(p => p.Footprint.Area));
        Assert.All(plans, p => Assert.Equal(new[] { "U1", "C1" }, p.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value)));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { 16.0, 12.0, 8.0 }, plans.Select(p => p.Zones[0].FloorArea));
        Assert.All(plans, p => Assert.Equal(3.0, p.FloorHeight));
        Assert.All(plans, p => Assert.Equal(12.5, p.OrientationDegrees));
        Assert.All(plans, p => Assert.All(p.Zones, z => Assert.Equal(0.0, z.Parts[0].Elevation)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ProvenanceRecordsTheGeneratorAndTheStoreyIndex()
    {
        IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan> plans = _generator.Generate(new BandParameters(2), Presets()).Value;

        Assert.All(plans, p => Assert.Equal(nameof(BandGenerator), p.Provenance.Operation));
        Assert.Equal(
            new[]
            {
                "FloorHeight=3", "HallWidth=4", "OrientationDegrees=12.5", "Presets=DwellingUnit:Example Dwelling Unit;Corridor:Example Corridor", "Storey=0",
                "StoreyElevation=0", "Storeys=2",
            },
            plans[0].Provenance.Parameters.Select(p => $"{p.Key}={p.Value}"));
        Assert.Equal("1", plans[1].Provenance.Parameters.Single(p => p.Key == "Storey").Value);
        Assert.Equal("3", plans[1].Provenance.Parameters.Single(p => p.Key == "StoreyElevation").Value);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryStoreyRecordsItsElevationTheSumOfTheFloorHeightsBelowIt()
    {
        IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan> plans = _generator.Generate(new BandParameters(4, FloorHeight: 3.1), Presets()).Value;

        Assert.Equal(new[] { 0.0, 3.1, 6.2, 9.3 }, plans.Select(p => StoreyProvenance.ElevationOf(p.Provenance)));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "0", "3.1", "6.2", "9.3" }, plans.Select(p => p.Provenance.Parameters.Single(q => q.Key == StoreyProvenance.ElevationKey).Value));
        Assert.All(plans, p => Assert.All(p.Zones, z => Assert.Equal(0.0, z.Parts[0].Elevation)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryStoreyRecordsTheSlotsThatReceivedAnAnyProgramPreset()
    {
        var presets = new BandPresets(ExampleResidentialPresets.Corridor.AsAnyProgram(), ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair.AsAnyProgram());

        IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan> plans = _generator.Generate(new BandParameters(2), presets).Value;

        Assert.All(plans, p => Assert.Equal(new[] { SpaceType.DwellingUnit, SpaceType.Corridor }, p.Zones.Select(z => z.SpaceType)));
        Assert.All(plans, p => Assert.Same(ExampleResidentialPresets.Corridor.Program, p.Zones[0].Program));
        Assert.All(
            plans,
            p => Assert.Equal(
                new[] { "AnyProgramPreset.Hall=Example Stair", "AnyProgramPreset.Unit=Example Corridor" },
                p.Provenance.Parameters.Where(q => q.Key.StartsWith("AnyProgramPreset.")).Select(q => $"{q.Key}={q.Value}")));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AStoreyIsBuiltExactlyAsAPlanGeneratorBuildsItsPlan()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan storey = _generator.Generate(new BandParameters(2), Presets()).Value[0];
        IGeneratedPlan plan = new GroundStoreyGenerator().Generate(new BandParameters(2), Presets()).Value;

        Assert.Equal(TextReport.Describe(plan), TextReport.Describe(storey));
        Assert.Equal(
            plan.Provenance.Parameters.Select(p => $"{p.Key}={p.Value}").Append("Storey=0").Append("StoreyElevation=0").OrderBy(p => p, System.StringComparer.Ordinal),
            storey.Provenance.Parameters.Select(p => $"{p.Key}={p.Value}"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ParameterAndPresetErrorsAreReportedOnceBeforeAnyStoreyIsLaidOut()
    {
        var generator = new BandGenerator();

        Result<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>> result = generator.Generate(new BandParameters(0, FloorHeight: -1.0), Presets(ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair));

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, DiagnosticCodes.PresetSpaceTypeMismatch },
            result.Diagnostics.Select(d => d.Code));
        Assert.StartsWith("FloorHeight must be positive", result.Diagnostics[0].Message);
        Assert.Equal("Storeys must be at least 1, got 0.", result.Diagnostics[1].Message);
        Assert.Equal(0, generator.Layouts);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ADiagnosticOfOneStoreyNamesItsStorey()
    {
        // A 0.5 m hall: the hall's south and north walls of every storey are below ADR-011's 1 m minimum and get no window.
        Result<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>> result = _generator.Generate(new BandParameters(2, HallWidth: 0.5), Presets());

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess);
        Diagnostic[] omitted = result.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.WindowOmitted).ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(4, omitted.Length);
        Assert.All(omitted, d => Assert.Equal(DiagnosticSeverity.Info, d.Severity));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "Storey 0: ", "Storey 0: ", "Storey 1: ", "Storey 1: " }, omitted.Select(d => d.Message.Substring(0, 10)));
        IGeneratedPlan[] plans = result.Value.ToArray();
        Assert.All(omitted.Take(2), d => Assert.Contains(plans[0].Surfaces, s => s.Id.Value == d.Subject));
        Assert.Equal(
            "Storey 1: Outdoor wall C1/W1 is 0.5 m long, below the 1 m minimum for a window (ADR-011); it has no window (0.3 m² of glazing not placed).",
            omitted[2].Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AStoreyWhoseZonesDoNotTileFailsTheGenerationAndIsNamed()
    {
        Result<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>> result = _generator.Generate(new BandParameters(3, OverlapStorey: 1), Presets());

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Assert.NotEmpty(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.All(result.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error), d => Assert.StartsWith("Storey 1: ", d.Message));
    }

    private static BandPresets Presets(ProgramPreset? unit = null) =>
        new(unit ?? ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit, ExampleResidentialPresets.Corridor);

    /// <param name="Storeys">Number of storeys, at least 1.</param>
    /// <param name="FloorHeight">Floor-to-floor height, m.</param>
    /// <param name="HallWidth">Width of the hall along x, m.</param>
    /// <param name="OverlapStorey">A storey whose hall overlaps its unit by 0.5 m, or −1 for none.</param>
    private sealed record BandParameters(int Storeys, double FloorHeight = 3.0, double HallWidth = 4.0, int OverlapStorey = -1)
        : PlanParameters(FloorHeight, 12.5);

    private sealed record BandPresets(ProgramPreset Unit, ProgramPreset Hall) : PlanPresets
    {
        public override IReadOnlyList<PresetSlot> Slots => new[]
        {
            new PresetSlot(nameof(Unit), SpaceType.DwellingUnit, Unit),
            new PresetSlot(nameof(Hall), SpaceType.Corridor, Hall),
        };
    }

    private static PlanLayout Storey(BandParameters parameters, int k)
    {
        double south = k;
        double hallWest = k == parameters.OverlapStorey ? 3.5 : 4.0;
        return new PlanLayout(
            Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, south, 4.0 + parameters.HallWidth, 4.0),
            new[]
            {
                new PlannedZone(new ZoneId("U1"), "Unit", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, south, 4.0, 4.0)),
                new PlannedZone(new ZoneId("C1"), "Hall", SpaceType.Corridor, Polygon2.Rectangle(hallWest, south, 4.0 + parameters.HallWidth, 4.0)),
            });
    }

    private static IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Described(BandParameters parameters) => new[]
    {
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(BandParameters.Storeys), parameters.Storeys.ToString(System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(BandParameters.HallWidth), Provenance.Format(parameters.HallWidth)),
    };

    /// <summary>A band of a 4 m unit <c>U1</c> and a hall <c>C1</c> east of it, 4 m deep on storey 0 and 1 m shallower on every storey above.</summary>
    private sealed class BandGenerator : MultiStoreyPlanGenerator<BandParameters, BandPresets>
    {
        public BandGenerator()
            : base(ToleranceSettings.Default)
        {
        }

        public int Layouts { get; private set; }

        public override string Name => nameof(BandGenerator);

        protected override IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(BandParameters parameters)
        {
            if (parameters.Storeys < 1)
            {
                yield return Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, $"Storeys must be at least 1, got {parameters.Storeys}.");
            }
        }

        protected override IReadOnlyList<PlanLayout> Layout(BandParameters parameters)
        {
            Layouts++;
            return Enumerable.Range(0, parameters.Storeys).Select(k => Storey(parameters, k)).ToArray();
        }

        protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(BandParameters parameters) => Described(parameters);
    }

    /// <summary>Storey 0 of <see cref="BandGenerator"/> as a plan generator of one plan.</summary>
    private sealed class GroundStoreyGenerator : PlanGenerator<BandParameters, BandPresets>
    {
        public GroundStoreyGenerator()
            : base(ToleranceSettings.Default)
        {
        }

        public override string Name => nameof(BandGenerator);

        protected override IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(BandParameters parameters) => Enumerable.Empty<Diagnostic>();

        protected override PlanLayout Layout(BandParameters parameters) => Storey(parameters, 0);

        protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(BandParameters parameters) => Described(parameters);
    }
}
```

```csharp
        var ops = new PolygonOps(ToleranceSettings.Default);

                .Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors && Orientation.Bin(w.PlanAzimuth) == OrientationBin.South)
                .ToArray();

            Assert.All(terraceFacing, w => Assert.True(w.Start.Y == 3.0 * k && w.End.Y == 3.0 * k));

    {

    [Fact]
    public void PresetsHaveOneSlotPerSpaceTypeAndAreCheckedOnce()
    {
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { ("DwellingUnit", SpaceType.DwellingUnit), ("Corridor", SpaceType.Corridor), ("Stair", SpaceType.Stair) },
            Examples.Slots.Select(s => (s.Input, s.SpaceType)));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { Examples.DwellingUnit, Examples.Corridor, Examples.Stair }, Examples.Slots.Select(s => s.Preset));
        var swapped = new SteppedBandPresets(ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair, ExampleResidentialPresets.Corridor, ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit);

        Result<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>> result = _generator.Generate(SteppedBandParameters.Default, swapped);

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "DwellingUnit", "Stair" }, result.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.PresetSpaceTypeMismatch).Select(d => d.Subject));
    }

    public void GenerationIsDeterministic()
    {
        Assert.Equal(Describe(Generate(SteppedBandParameters.Default)), Describe(Generate(SteppedBandParameters.Default)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ProvenanceRecordsParametersPresetsAndTheStorey()
    {
        IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan> plans = Generate(SteppedBandParameters.Default);

        Assert.All(plans, p => Assert.Equal(nameof(SteppedBandGenerator), p.Provenance.Operation));
        Assert.Equal(
            new[]
            {
                "BaseDepth=14", "CorridorWidth=2", "FloorHeight=3", "Length=36", "OrientationDegrees=0",
                "Presets=DwellingUnit:Example Dwelling Unit;Corridor:Example Corridor;Stair:Example Stair", "StairWidth=4", "StepDepth=3", "Storey=0",
                "StoreyCount=3", "StoreyElevation=0", "UnitWidth=6",
            },
            plans[0].Provenance.Parameters.Select(p => $"{p.Key}={p.Value}"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "0", "1", "2" }, plans.Select(p => p.Provenance.Parameters.Single(q => q.Key == "Storey").Value));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "0", "3", "6" }, plans.Select(p => p.Provenance.Parameters.Single(q => q.Key == "StoreyElevation").Value));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalStoreysSnapshot()
    {
        Snapshot.Match(Describe(Generate(SteppedBandParameters.Default)), "stepped-band-canonical");
    }

    /// <summary>The text report of every storey, bottom-up, each after a line <c>storey k</c>.</summary>
    private static string Describe(IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan> plans)
    {
        var builder = new StringBuilder();
        for (int k = 0; k < plans.Count; k++)
        {
            builder.Append("storey ").Append(k).Append('\n').Append(TextReport.Describe(plans[k]));
        }

        return builder.ToString();
    }

    private static void AssertRectangle(double minX, double minY, double maxX, double maxY, Point2[] actual)
    {
        Point2[] expected = Polygon2.Rectangle(minX, minY, maxX, maxY).Outer.ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(expected.Length, actual.Length);
        for (int i = 0; i < expected.Length; i++)
        {
            Assert.Equal(expected[i].X, actual[i].X, 9);
            Assert.Equal(expected[i].Y, actual[i].Y, 9);
        }
    }

    private static Zone Zone(IGeneratedPlan plan, string id) => plan.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == id);

    private static Point2[] Outer(IGeneratedPlan plan, string zone) => Zone(plan, zone).Parts[0].Footprint.Outer.ToArray();

    private IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan> Generate(SteppedBandParameters parameters) => _generator.Generate(parameters, Examples).Value;
}
```

- [x] **Step 6: Implement — create `src/Lod.Core/Plans/StoreyProvenance.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Model;

namespace Lod.Core.Plans;

/// <summary>
/// The base elevation of a storey of a <see cref="MultiStoreyPlanGenerator{TParameters, TPresets}"/> (ADR-015, D-108): the sum of the floor heights
/// below it, recorded in the storey plan's provenance as <see cref="ElevationKey"/>. The plan itself stays at elevation 0; only the Grasshopper
/// previews of the plan and of what is made from it alone use the elevation, so a storey is drawn where the floor aggregators place it.
/// </summary>
public static class StoreyProvenance
{
    /// <summary>Provenance key of a storey's base elevation, m, as invariant-culture text.</summary>
    public const string ElevationKey = "StoreyElevation";

    /// <summary>
    /// The storey elevation an object descends from: the value of <see cref="ElevationKey"/> in its provenance or, while an operation has exactly one
    /// input (<see cref="PlanTransform"/>, a plan simplifier), in its input's. An object of several inputs (an aggregated building) or one that does not
    /// descend from a storey plan is at 0.
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="provenance">The provenance of a plan, floor, or building.</param>
    /// <returns>The elevation, m; 0 when none is recorded.</returns>
    public static double ElevationOf(Provenance provenance)
    {
        Provenance? current = provenance;
        while (current is not null)
        {
            KeyValuePair<string, string> recorded = current.Parameters.FirstOrDefault(p => p.Key == ElevationKey);
            if (recorded.Key is not null)
            {
                return double.Parse(recorded.Value, NumberStyles.Float, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
            }

            current = current.Inputs.Count == 1 ? current.Inputs[0] : null;
        }

        return 0.0;
    }
}
```

- [x] **Step 7: Implement — replace `src/Lod.Core/Plans/MultiStoreyPlanGenerator.cs` (full file)**

`Generate` appends `StoreyElevation` after `Storey` and adds the floor height after each storey; the class summary names the key; nothing else changes.

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Model;

namespace Lod.Core.Plans;

/// <summary>
/// Base class of plan generators whose plan differs per storey (D-095, ADR-015). The subclass validates its parameters once and lays out every
/// storey; this class checks the common parameters and the presets once, then builds every storey exactly as
/// <see cref="PlanGenerator{TParameters, TPresets}"/> builds its plan (presets, surfaces, centred windows within the minimum wall and window geometry
/// of ADR-011, provenance) and returns the plans bottom-up. Each plan records its storey index as the provenance parameter <c>Storey</c>, from 0 at
/// the bottom, and its base elevation, the sum of the floor heights below it, as <see cref="StoreyProvenance.ElevationKey"/>, which only previews read
/// (D-108); every plan lies at elevation 0. The floor aggregators stack the plans as floor entries with multiplier 1 (ADR-015). Generation is
/// deterministic.
/// </summary>
/// <typeparam name="TParameters">The generator's parameter record.</typeparam>
/// <typeparam name="TPresets">The generator's preset record, one preset per space type it places (D-061).</typeparam>
public abstract class MultiStoreyPlanGenerator<TParameters, TPresets>
    where TParameters : PlanParameters
    where TPresets : PlanPresets
{
    /// <summary>Provenance key of the storey index.</summary>
    public const string StoreyKey = "Storey";

    /// <summary>Initialises the generator with the default minimum wall and window geometry (ADR-011).</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    protected MultiStoreyPlanGenerator(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
        : this(tolerances, GeometryLimits.Default)
    {
    }

    /// <summary>Initialises the generator.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    /// <param name="limits">Minimum wall and window geometry (ADR-011); recorded in provenance when not the default.</param>
    protected MultiStoreyPlanGenerator(ToleranceSettings tolerances, GeometryLimits limits)
    {
        Tolerances = tolerances;
        Limits = limits;
    }

    /// <summary>Operation name recorded in provenance.</summary>
    public abstract string Name { get; }

    /// <summary>Tolerances.</summary>
    protected ToleranceSettings Tolerances { get; }

    /// <summary>Minimum wall and window geometry (ADR-011).</summary>
    protected GeometryLimits Limits { get; }

    /// <summary>Generates the detailed plan of every storey.</summary>
    /// <param name="parameters">Generator parameters.</param>
    /// <param name="presets">Program presets, one per slot; each must have its slot's space type or be an any-program preset (D-108).</param>
    /// <returns>One plan per storey, bottom-up, or errors. A diagnostic of one storey has a message starting with <c>Storey {k}: </c>.</returns>
    public Result<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>> Generate(TParameters parameters, TPresets presets)
    {
        List<Diagnostic> errors = PlanBuilder.CheckInputs(parameters, Validate(parameters), presets);
        if (errors.Count > 0)
        {
            return Result.Failure<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>>(errors);
        }

        var plans = new List<IGeneratedPlan>();
        var diagnostics = new List<Diagnostic>();
        IReadOnlyList<PlanLayout> layouts = Layout(parameters);
        double elevation = 0.0;
        for (int k = 0; k < layouts.Count; k++)
        {
            IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> described = Describe(parameters)
                .Append(new KeyValuePair<string, string>(StoreyKey, k.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)))
                .Append(new KeyValuePair<string, string>(StoreyProvenance.ElevationKey, Provenance.Format(elevation)));
            elevation += parameters.FloorHeight;
            Result<IGeneratedPlan> storey = PlanBuilder.Build(Name, layouts[k], parameters, presets, described, Limits, Tolerances);
            string prefix = $"Storey {k}: ";
            diagnostics.AddRange(storey.Diagnostics.Select(d => d with { Message = prefix + d.Message }));
            if (storey.IsSuccess)
            {
                plans.Add(storey.Value);
            }
        }

        return diagnostics.Any(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error)
            ? Result.Failure<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>>(diagnostics)
            : Result.Success<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>>(plans.AsReadOnly(), diagnostics);
    }

    /// <summary>Checks typology-specific parameters.</summary>
    /// <param name="parameters">Parameters.</param>
    /// <returns>Errors, if any.</returns>
    protected abstract IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(TParameters parameters);

    /// <summary>Lays out every storey for valid parameters.</summary>
    /// <param name="parameters">Validated parameters.</param>
    /// <returns>Footprint and zones of every storey, bottom-up; at least one.</returns>
    protected abstract IReadOnlyList<PlanLayout> Layout(TParameters parameters);

    /// <summary>Typology-specific parameters for provenance, as invariant-culture text; the same for every storey.</summary>
    /// <param name="parameters">Parameters.</param>
    /// <returns>Key-value pairs.</returns>
    protected abstract IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(TParameters parameters);

    /// <summary>Whether a value is finite and positive.</summary>
    /// <param name="value">The value.</param>
    /// <returns><c>true</c> when positive and finite.</returns>
    protected static bool IsPositive(double value) => PlanBuilder.IsPositive(value);
}
```

### Task 5: Every storey plan and its floor previewed at the storey's elevation

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/Lod.Grasshopper/Goo/BemGoo.cs` (S2 file, last changed in S8.4), `src/Lod.Grasshopper/Components/TypologyGeneratorComponents.cs` (S8.1 file, last changed in S8.7)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `StoreyProvenance.ElevationOf` (Task 4); `PreviewGoo<T>` (S2–S8.4: `PreviewOffset`, the Brep preview built once and moved), `PlanGoo`, `FloorGoo`, `RhinoGeometry.UnitScale`; the multi-storey `Output` of `TypologyGeneratorComponent` (S8.7), `ComponentSupport.Unwrap`, `ComponentSupport.PreviewLocation`.
- Produces: `PreviewGoo<T>` gains `protected virtual double StoreyElevation => 0.0`, and its preview is moved by `PreviewOffset + (0, 0, StoreyElevation × RhinoGeometry.UnitScale)`; `PlanGoo` and `FloorGoo` override it with `StoreyProvenance.ElevationOf(Value.Provenance)`; `BuildingGoo` keeps 0. The multi-storey `Output` sets each storey's `PlanGoo` with the *Preview Location* only, so the elevation is not added twice and the generator's previews are where S8.7 drew them. *Transform Plan* and the four simplifier components need no change: their goo takes the height from the data, plus their own *Preview Location*. `PreviewOffset` still means the *Preview Location* only. Data, Breps, and *Convert2BEM* outputs are unchanged.

- [x] **Step 1: Replace `src/Lod.Grasshopper/Goo/BemGoo.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using Grasshopper.Kernel;

using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Core.Schedules;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Convert;
using Rhino.Display;
using Rhino.Geometry;
using Surface = Lod.Core.Model.Surface;

namespace Lod.Grasshopper.Goo;

/// <summary>Grasshopper wrapper of an immutable pipeline object. Values are never modified, so duplicates share the value.</summary>
/// <typeparam name="T">Wrapped type.</typeparam>
public abstract class BemGoo<T> : GH_Goo<T>
    where T : class
{
    protected BemGoo(T value)
        : base(value)
    {
    }

    public override bool IsValid => Value is not null;

    public override string TypeDescription => $"BEMGen {TypeName}";
}

{

        : base(value)
    {
    }

    public override string TypeName => "Schedule";

    public override IGH_Goo Duplicate() => new ScheduleGoo(Value);

    public override string ToString() => $"Schedule {Value.Name} ({Value.Kind})";
}

        : base(value)
    {
    }

    public override string TypeName => "Load";

    public override IGH_Goo Duplicate() => new LoadGoo(Value);

    public override string ToString() => $"Load {Value.Type} {Value.Value} {Value.Basis}";
}

public sealed class ProgramPresetGoo : BemGoo<ProgramPreset>
{

        : base(value)
    {
    }

    public override string TypeName => "Program Preset";

    public override IGH_Goo Duplicate() => new ProgramPresetGoo(Value);

    public override string ToString() => $"Preset {Value.Name} ({Value.SpaceType}, WWR {Value.WindowToWallRatio})";
}

/// <summary>
/// Wrapper with viewport preview: zones coloured by space type (a zone with several parts as one merged volume, D-071), storeys that are represented
/// but not modelled in transparent grey (D-071, D-075), windows in blue. The preview is raised to <see cref="StoreyElevation"/> and moved by
/// <see cref="PreviewOffset"/> (D-062, D-108); the wrapped value keeps its true coordinates.
/// </summary>
/// <typeparam name="T">Wrapped type.</typeparam>
public abstract class PreviewGoo<T> : BemGoo<T>, IGH_PreviewData
    where T : class
{
    private static readonly Color WindowColour = Color.FromArgb(70, 140, 220);
    private static readonly Color UnmodelledColour = Color.FromArgb(150, 150, 150);
    private List<(Brep Brep, DisplayMaterial Material)>? _preview;

    protected PreviewGoo(T value, Vector3d previewOffset)
        : base(value)
    {
        PreviewOffset = previewOffset;
    }

    /// <summary>Translation of the viewport preview only, in model units (D-062).</summary>
    public Vector3d PreviewOffset { get; }

    public BoundingBox ClippingBox
    {
        get
        {
            BoundingBox box = BoundingBox.Empty;
            foreach ((Brep brep, DisplayMaterial _) in Preview)
            {
                box.Union(brep.GetBoundingBox(false));
            }

            return box;
        }
    }

    private List<(Brep Brep, DisplayMaterial Material)> Preview => _preview ??= BuildPreview();

    public void DrawViewportMeshes(GH_PreviewMeshArgs args)
    {
        foreach ((Brep brep, DisplayMaterial material) in Preview)
        {
            args.Pipeline.DrawBrepShaded(brep, material);
        }
    }

    public void DrawViewportWires(GH_PreviewWireArgs args)
    {
        foreach ((Brep brep, DisplayMaterial _) in Preview)
        {
            args.Pipeline.DrawBrepWires(brep, args.Color);
        }
    }

    protected abstract IEnumerable<Zone> Zones { get; }

    /// <summary>
    /// The base elevation, m, of the storey the value is or comes from (<see cref="StoreyProvenance"/>, ADR-015), at which its preview is drawn below
    /// the Preview Location; 0 unless a plan or floor says so.
    /// </summary>
    protected virtual double StoreyElevation => 0.0;

    protected abstract IEnumerable<Surface> Surfaces { get; }

    /// <summary>Parts of storeys that are represented but not modelled, drawn transparent grey; none unless a building says so.</summary>
    protected virtual IEnumerable<ZonePart> UnmodelledParts => Enumerable.Empty<ZonePart>();

    private List<(Brep, DisplayMaterial)> BuildPreview()
    {
        var items = new List<(Brep, DisplayMaterial)>();
        foreach (Zone zone in Zones)
        {
            var material = new DisplayMaterial(SpaceTypeColour(zone.SpaceType), 0.6);
            IEnumerable<Brep> volumes = zone.Parts.Count > 1 ? RhinoGeometry.MergedZoneBreps(zone) : RhinoGeometry.ZoneBreps(zone);
            items.AddRange(volumes.Select(b => (b, material)));
        }

        var unmodelled = new DisplayMaterial(UnmodelledColour, 0.85);
        items.AddRange(RhinoGeometry.PartBreps(UnmodelledParts).Select(b => (b, unmodelled)));

        var glass = new DisplayMaterial(WindowColour, 0.2);
        foreach (WallSurface wall in Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>())
        {
            items.AddRange(RhinoGeometry.WindowBreps(wall).Select(b => (b, glass)));
        }

        Vector3d offset = PreviewOffset + new Vector3d(0.0, 0.0, StoreyElevation * RhinoGeometry.UnitScale);
        if (!offset.IsZero)
        {
            Transform move = Transform.Translation(offset);
            foreach ((Brep brep, DisplayMaterial _) in items)
            {
                brep.Transform(move);
            }
        }

        return items;
    }

    private static Color SpaceTypeColour(SpaceType type) => type switch
    {
        SpaceType.DwellingUnit => Color.FromArgb(240, 200, 120),
        SpaceType.Corridor => Color.FromArgb(180, 180, 180),
        SpaceType.Stair => Color.FromArgb(200, 120, 120),
        SpaceType.Core => Color.FromArgb(160, 120, 200),
        SpaceType.Mixed => Color.FromArgb(120, 190, 160),
        SpaceType.Office => Color.FromArgb(130, 170, 220),
        SpaceType.Retail => Color.FromArgb(230, 150, 170),
        SpaceType.Mall => Color.FromArgb(210, 210, 160),
        SpaceType.Kitchen => Color.FromArgb(220, 130, 90),
        SpaceType.Dining => Color.FromArgb(240, 220, 150),
        SpaceType.OperatingTheatre => Color.FromArgb(110, 190, 200),
        SpaceType.CleanCorridor => Color.FromArgb(200, 230, 230),
        SpaceType.DirtyCorridor => Color.FromArgb(160, 140, 120),
        SpaceType.ClinicalSupport => Color.FromArgb(150, 200, 180),
        SpaceType.CareBedroom => Color.FromArgb(230, 180, 200),
        SpaceType.CareCommunal => Color.FromArgb(200, 160, 220),
        SpaceType.ActivityHall => Color.FromArgb(170, 210, 120),
        _ => Color.FromArgb(150, 170, 200),
    };
}

    protected override IEnumerable<Zone> Zones => Value.Zones;

    protected override IEnumerable<Surface> Surfaces => Value.Surfaces;

    /// <param name="value">The building.</param>
    /// <param name="previewOffset">Preview Location (D-062).</param>
    /// <param name="unmodelled">Parts of the storeys the building represents but does not model, from the aggregator (preview only).</param>
    public BuildingGoo(IGeneratedBuilding value, Vector3d previewOffset = default, IReadOnlyList<ZonePart>? unmodelled = null)

    public override string ToString() => $"Building ({Value.Zones.Count} zones, {Value.Provenance.Operation})";
}
```

- [x] **Step 2: Replace `src/Lod.Grasshopper/Components/TypologyGeneratorComponents.cs` (full file)**

The multi-storey `Output` passes the *Preview Location* only; `using System.Linq;` is added and `using Lod.Grasshopper.Convert;` removed; nothing else changes.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using Grasshopper.Kernel;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.BranchingMall;
using Lod.Generators.Cafeteria;
using Lod.Generators.CareHub;
using Lod.Generators.CourtCluster;
using Lod.Generators.EnclosedCourt;
using Lod.Generators.FoyerHalls;
using Lod.Generators.GalleryBar;
using Lod.Generators.OfficePlate;
using Lod.Generators.OpenCourt;
using Lod.Generators.OperatingSuite;
using Lod.Generators.PointPlate;
using Lod.Generators.RadialLobes;
using Lod.Generators.StairBayBar;
using Lod.Generators.StairPair;
using Lod.Generators.SteppedBand;
using Lod.Generators.TerraceRow;
using Lod.Generators.WingedBand;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Goo;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Parameters;
using Rhino.Geometry;

namespace Lod.Grasshopper.Components;

/// <summary>
/// Shared inputs and output of the typology plan generator components (ADR-006): number inputs with the family's defaults, one required preset input
/// per space type (D-061, D-065), the Preview Location last (D-062), and one Plan output: one plan, or, for a family whose plan differs per storey,
/// the list of storey plans bottom-up (ADR-015).
/// </summary>
public abstract class TypologyGeneratorComponent : GH_Component
{
    protected const string FloorHeightDescription = "Floor-to-floor height, m.";

    protected const string OrientationDescription = "Clockwise rotation of plan north from true north, degrees.";

    protected const string Metres = " All lengths in metres regardless of model units.";

    protected TypologyGeneratorComponent(string name, string nickname, string description)
        : base(name, nickname, description + Metres, ComponentCategories.Category, ComponentCategories.Generate)
    {
    }

    protected override Bitmap? Icon => IconLibrary.For(this);

    /// <summary>Whether the Plan output is the list of storey plans of a multi-storey family (ADR-015) rather than one plan.</summary>
    protected virtual bool PlanPerStorey => false;

    protected override void RegisterOutputParams(GH_OutputParamManager pManager)
    {
        if (PlanPerStorey)
        {
            pManager.AddParameter(
                new PlanParameter(),
                "Plan",
                "Plan",
                "The detailed plan of every storey, bottom-up (ADR-015). Wire it through a plan simplifier into a floor aggregator's Floors, with Multipliers unconnected (one storey each).",
                GH_ParamAccess.list);
        }
        else
        {
            pManager.AddParameter(new PlanParameter(), "Plan", "Plan", "The detailed plan.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Adds the required preset input of one space type.</summary>
    protected static void AddPreset(GH_InputParamManager pManager, string name, string nickname, SpaceType spaceType, string role) =>
        pManager.AddParameter(new ProgramPresetParameter(), name, nickname, $"Program preset of {role}; space type {spaceType}.", GH_ParamAccess.item);

    /// <summary>Reads <paramref name="count"/> number inputs from <paramref name="first"/>; <c>false</c> when one is missing.</summary>
    protected static bool TryNumbers(IGH_DataAccess DA, int first, int count, out double[] values)
    {
        values = new double[count];
        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
        {
            if (!DA.GetData(first + i, ref values[i]))
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    /// <summary>Reads a preset input; <c>null</c> when it is unconnected, which Grasshopper reports as a missing input (D-065).</summary>
    protected static ProgramPreset? Preset(IGH_DataAccess DA, int index)
    {
        ProgramPresetGoo? goo = null;
        return DA.GetData(index, ref goo) && goo is not null ? goo.Value : null;
    }

    /// <summary>Reports the generator's diagnostics and outputs the plan with this component's preview location.</summary>
    protected void Output(IGH_DataAccess DA, Result<IGeneratedPlan> result)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan? plan = ComponentSupport.Unwrap(this, result);
        if (plan is not null)
        {
            DA.SetData(0, new PlanGoo(plan, ComponentSupport.PreviewLocation(this, DA)));
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Reports the generator's diagnostics and outputs the storey plans, bottom-up, with this component's preview location; each storey's preview is
    /// drawn at the elevation recorded in its plan (<see cref="Lod.Core.Plans.StoreyProvenance"/>), while the plans keep elevation 0 (ADR-015, D-108).
    /// </summary>
    protected void Output(IGH_DataAccess DA, Result<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>> result)
    {
        IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>? plans = ComponentSupport.Unwrap(this, result);
        if (plans is not null)
        {
            Vector3d location = ComponentSupport.PreviewLocation(this, DA);
            DA.SetDataList(0, plans.Select(p => new PlanGoo(p, location)));
        }
    }
}

public sealed class StairBayBarComponent : TypologyGeneratorComponent
{
    public StairBayBarComponent()
        : base(
            "Stair Bay Bar",
            "SBar",
            "Repeated stair bays in a linear bar (HDHM-TYP-001, ADR-006): bays of dwelling | stair | dwelling through the full depth, each stair serving the two dwellings beside it; no corridor. One zone per dwelling unit.")
    {
    }

    public override Guid ComponentGuid => new("911c3204-42bb-4023-bdb7-2931ea1caad3");

    protected override void RegisterInputParams(GH_InputParamManager pManager)
    {
        StairBayBarParameters d = StairBayBarParameters.Default;
        pManager.AddIntegerParameter("Bay Count", "BC", "Number of stair bays along the bar; at least 2.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.BayCount);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Unit Width", "UW", "Width of each dwelling along the bar, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.UnitWidth);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Depth", "D", "Bar depth, which every dwelling and stair spans, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.Depth);

        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Orientation", "O", OrientationDescription, GH_ParamAccess.item, d.OrientationDegrees);

        pManager.AddParameter(ComponentSupport.PreviewLocationParameter());
    }

    protected override void SolveInstance(IGH_DataAccess DA)
    {
        int wings = 0;
        if (!DA.GetData(0, ref wings)
            || !TryNumbers(DA, 1, 6, out double[] v)
            || Preset(DA, 7) is not { } careBedroom
            || Preset(DA, 8) is not { } careCommunal
            || Preset(DA, 9) is not { } corridor)
        {
            return;
        }

        var parameters = new CareHubParameters(wings, v[0], v[1], v[2], v[3], v[4], v[5]);
        Output(DA, new CareHubGenerator(ToleranceSettings.Default).Generate(parameters, new CareHubPresets(careBedroom, careCommunal, corridor)));
    }
}
```

## Slice C — `feature/any-program-preset-parameter`

### Task 6: *Any Program Preset* parameter, accepted by every preset input, and its icon

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/Lod.Grasshopper/Parameters/BemParameters.cs` (S2 file, last changed in S4.1), `src/Lod.Grasshopper/Goo/BemGoo.cs` (Task 5), `src/Lod.Grasshopper/Components/ComponentSupport.cs` (S2 file, last changed in S4.1), `src/Lod.Grasshopper/Components/PipelineComponents.cs` (S2 file, last changed in S8.1), `src/Lod.Grasshopper/Components/TypologyGeneratorComponents.cs` (Task 5), `scripts/make_icons.py` (S4.1 file, last changed in S8.7)
- Create (generated): `src/Lod.Grasshopper/Icons/AnyProgramPresetParameter.png`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ProgramPreset.AsAnyProgram()` and `SpaceType?` (Task 1); `BemGoo<T>`, `ProgramPresetGoo`, `BemParameter<TGoo>` (S2–S4.1, a `GH_Param<TGoo>` in panel *0 Info*, hidden), `ProgramPresetParameter` (GUID `56578999-825c-4926-afdd-ce897b53221a`, the type of every generator's preset input), `GH_Param<T>.PreferredCast`, `GH_ObjectWrapper`, `ComponentCategories.Program`; `TypologyGeneratorComponent.AddPreset` (S8.1) and the three preset inputs of `LinearPlanGeneratorComponent` (S4.1); `IconLibrary.For`.
- Produces: `AnyProgramPresetGoo : BemGoo<ProgramPreset>`, whose constructor always stores `value.AsAnyProgram()`, `TypeName` "Any Program Preset", `ToString` `Preset {Name} (any program, no space type, WWR {ratio})` (`NoSpaceType`), and `CastTo` to `ProgramPresetGoo` and `ProgramPreset`; `ProgramPresetGoo.ToString` prints the same text for a preset without a space type. `AnyProgramPresetParameter : BemParameter<AnyProgramPresetGoo>`: *Any Program Preset*, nickname AnyP, GUID `24bc6a12-ad06-4c25-bc9d-4e8318b8744c`, panel *1 Program*, primary, item access, a floating parameter without persistent data; its `PreferredCast` turns a `ProgramPresetGoo` with a value, a plain `ProgramPreset`, or a `GH_ObjectWrapper` around one (a script output) into an `AnyProgramPresetGoo`, and leaves anything else to Grasshopper's own conversion, which fails for it. `ProgramPresetParameter.PreferredCast` turns an `AnyProgramPresetGoo` into a `ProgramPresetGoo` of its any-program value, so every preset input of every generator takes it; its description names any-program presets. `BemParameter` gains a constructor with a panel argument; the six existing parameters stay in *0 Info*. `ComponentSupport.PresetInputDescription(role, spaceType)` = `Program preset of {role}; space type {spaceType}.` plus the one shared sentence ` An Any Program Preset (D-108) is accepted too: its zones keep this space type and take the preset's program, and the plan's provenance records it.`, used by `AddPreset` (17 generators) and the three preset inputs of the *Linear Plan Generator*. The motif `small_any_preset` (the small preset tag with a wildcard asterisk where its hole was, in the Program accent, inside the parameter hexagon), mapped after `ProgramPresetParameter`: 61 icons. No component, input, output, or GUID changes.

The parameter's `PreferredCast` is taken from the final state of the verified reference (`d9c3059`, which added the `GH_ObjectWrapper` case to the parameter of `9b97dfa`); this task squashes the two reference commits into one, and the tree after it equals the reference's after `d9c3059` for every file of this task (note 6).

```csharp
using System;
using System.Drawing;
using Grasshopper.Kernel;
using Grasshopper.Kernel.Types;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Goo;
using Rhino.Geometry;

namespace Lod.Grasshopper.Parameters;

/// <summary>A parameter carrying BEMGen objects; previews them when they support it. Hidden from the toolbar.</summary>
/// <typeparam name="TGoo">Goo type.</typeparam>
public abstract class BemParameter<TGoo> : GH_Param<TGoo>, IGH_PreviewObject
    where TGoo : class, IGH_Goo
{
    protected BemParameter(string name, string nickname, string description)
        : this(name, nickname, description, ComponentCategories.Info)
    {
    }

    protected BemParameter(string name, string nickname, string description, string subCategory)
        : base(name, nickname, description, ComponentCategories.Category, subCategory, GH_ParamAccess.item)
    {
    }

    public override GH_Exposure Exposure => GH_Exposure.hidden;

    protected override Bitmap? Icon => IconLibrary.For(this);

    public bool Hidden { get; set; }

    public bool IsPreviewCapable => true;

    public BoundingBox ClippingBox => Preview_ComputeClippingBox();

    public void DrawViewportMeshes(IGH_PreviewArgs args) => Preview_DrawMeshes(args);

    public void DrawViewportWires(IGH_PreviewArgs args) => Preview_DrawWires(args);
}

{
    public ScheduleParameter()
        : base("Schedule", "Sch", "An 8760-hour schedule.")
    {
    }

    public override Guid ComponentGuid => new("a4ae84ac-36d6-41b8-9b3d-c77cca52a2df");
}

public sealed class LoadParameter : BemParameter<LoadGoo>
{
    public LoadParameter()
        : base("Load", "L", "A load definition.")

    public override Guid ComponentGuid => new("9e9fbc12-9b81-42b7-8c5d-a4f8397a4487");
}

public sealed class ProgramPresetParameter : BemParameter<ProgramPresetGoo>
{
    public ProgramPresetParameter()
        : base("Program Preset", "P", "A program preset for one space type, or an any-program preset without one (D-108).")
    {
    }

    public override Guid ComponentGuid => new("56578999-825c-4926-afdd-ce897b53221a");

    /// <summary>Takes an any-program preset (D-108) as the preset it wraps, which has no space type.</summary>
    protected override ProgramPresetGoo PreferredCast(object data) => data is AnyProgramPresetGoo any ? new ProgramPresetGoo(any.Value) : null!;
}

/// <summary>
/// The data node of D-108, a floating parameter like Grasshopper's <i>Geometry</i>, visible in panel 1 Program: every preset wired into it, built-in
/// or general, comes out as an any-program preset (<see cref="ProgramPreset.AsAnyProgram"/>), which every preset input of every plan generator
/// accepts. It holds no data of its own: an unconnected one is empty.
/// </summary>
public sealed class AnyProgramPresetParameter : BemParameter<AnyProgramPresetGoo>
{
    public AnyProgramPresetParameter()
        : base(
            "Any Program Preset",
            "AnyP",
            "Casts a program preset of any space type to an any-program preset: the same name, loads, schedules, conditioning, setpoints, and WWR, without a space type. Wire it into any preset input of any plan generator; the zones keep that input's space type, and the plan records the use in its provenance (AnyProgramPreset.<Input>, D-108).",
            ComponentCategories.Program)
    {
    }

    public override Guid ComponentGuid => new("24bc6a12-ad06-4c25-bc9d-4e8318b8744c");

    public override GH_Exposure Exposure => GH_Exposure.primary;

    /// <summary>
    /// Casts a preset to an any-program preset: one in a <see cref="ProgramPresetGoo"/> (any preset component or parameter), or a plain preset, also
    /// wrapped by Grasshopper (a script output). Anything else is left to Grasshopper's own conversion, which fails for it.
    /// </summary>
    protected override AnyProgramPresetGoo PreferredCast(object data) => data switch
    {
        ProgramPresetGoo { Value: { } preset } => new AnyProgramPresetGoo(preset),
        ProgramPreset preset => new AnyProgramPresetGoo(preset),
        GH_ObjectWrapper { Value: ProgramPreset preset } => new AnyProgramPresetGoo(preset),
        _ => null!,
    };
}
```

- [x] **Step 3: Replace `src/Lod.Grasshopper/Goo/BemGoo.cs` (full file)**

`AnyProgramPresetGoo` is added after `ProgramPresetGoo`, whose `ToString` names a preset without a space type; nothing else changes.

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using Grasshopper.Kernel;
using Grasshopper.Kernel.Types;
using Lod.Core.Loads;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Core.Schedules;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Convert;
using Rhino.Display;
using Rhino.Geometry;
using Surface = Lod.Core.Model.Surface;

namespace Lod.Grasshopper.Goo;

/// <summary>Grasshopper wrapper of an immutable pipeline object. Values are never modified, so duplicates share the value.</summary>
/// <typeparam name="T">Wrapped type.</typeparam>
public abstract class BemGoo<T> : GH_Goo<T>
    where T : class
{
    protected BemGoo(T value)
        : base(value)
    {
    }

    public override bool IsValid => Value is not null;

    public override string TypeDescription => $"BEMGen {TypeName}";
}

public sealed class ScheduleGoo : BemGoo<Schedule>
{
    public ScheduleGoo(Schedule value)
        : base(value)
    {
    }

    public override string TypeName => "Schedule";

    public override IGH_Goo Duplicate() => new ScheduleGoo(Value);

    public override string ToString() => $"Schedule {Value.Name} ({Value.Kind})";
}

public sealed class LoadGoo : BemGoo<LoadDefinition>
{
    public LoadGoo(LoadDefinition value)
        : base(value)
    {
    }

    public override string TypeName => "Load";

    public override IGH_Goo Duplicate() => new LoadGoo(Value);

    public override string ToString() => $"Load {Value.Type} {Value.Value} {Value.Basis}";
}

public sealed class ProgramPresetGoo : BemGoo<ProgramPreset>
{
    public ProgramPresetGoo(ProgramPreset value)
        : base(value)
    {
    }

    public override string TypeName => "Program Preset";

    public override IGH_Goo Duplicate() => new ProgramPresetGoo(Value);

    public override string ToString() => $"Preset {Value.Name} ({(Value.SpaceType is { } spaceType ? spaceType.ToString() : AnyProgramPresetGoo.NoSpaceType)}, WWR {Value.WindowToWallRatio})";
}

/// <summary>
/// A preset cast to an any-program preset (D-108): <see cref="ProgramPreset.AsAnyProgram"/> of the wrapped preset, so it has no space type and fits
/// every preset input of every plan generator. Carried by <see cref="Parameters.AnyProgramPresetParameter"/>.
/// </summary>
public sealed class AnyProgramPresetGoo : BemGoo<ProgramPreset>
{
    /// <summary>How a preset without a space type is described.</summary>
    public const string NoSpaceType = "any program, no space type";

    /// <param name="value">A preset of any space type, or an any-program preset; it is cast to an any-program preset.</param>
    public AnyProgramPresetGoo(ProgramPreset value)
        : base(value.AsAnyProgram())
    {
    }

    public override string TypeName => "Any Program Preset";

    public override IGH_Goo Duplicate() => new AnyProgramPresetGoo(Value);

    public override string ToString() => $"Preset {Value.Name} ({NoSpaceType}, WWR {Value.WindowToWallRatio})";

    /// <summary>Gives the any-program preset as a <see cref="ProgramPresetGoo"/> (a plan generator's preset input) or as the plain preset.</summary>
    public override bool CastTo<TQ>(ref TQ target)
    {
        if (typeof(TQ).IsAssignableFrom(typeof(ProgramPresetGoo)))
        {
            target = (TQ)(object)new ProgramPresetGoo(Value);
            return true;
        }

        if (typeof(TQ).IsAssignableFrom(typeof(ProgramPreset)))
        {
            target = (TQ)(object)Value;
            return true;
        }

        return false;
    }
}

/// <summary>
/// Wrapper with viewport preview: zones coloured by space type (a zone with several parts as one merged volume, D-071), storeys that are represented
/// but not modelled in transparent grey (D-071, D-075), windows in blue. The preview is raised to <see cref="StoreyElevation"/> and moved by
/// <see cref="PreviewOffset"/> (D-062, D-108); the wrapped value keeps its true coordinates.
/// </summary>
/// <typeparam name="T">Wrapped type.</typeparam>
public abstract class PreviewGoo<T> : BemGoo<T>, IGH_PreviewData
    where T : class
{
    private static readonly Color WindowColour = Color.FromArgb(70, 140, 220);
    private static readonly Color UnmodelledColour = Color.FromArgb(150, 150, 150);
    private List<(Brep Brep, DisplayMaterial Material)>? _preview;

    protected PreviewGoo(T value, Vector3d previewOffset)
        : base(value)
    {
        PreviewOffset = previewOffset;
    }

    /// <summary>Translation of the viewport preview only, in model units (D-062).</summary>
    public Vector3d PreviewOffset { get; }

    public BoundingBox ClippingBox
    {
        get
        {
            BoundingBox box = BoundingBox.Empty;
            foreach ((Brep brep, DisplayMaterial _) in Preview)
            {
                box.Union(brep.GetBoundingBox(false));
            }

            return box;
        }
    }

    private List<(Brep Brep, DisplayMaterial Material)> Preview => _preview ??= BuildPreview();

    public void DrawViewportMeshes(GH_PreviewMeshArgs args)
    {
        foreach ((Brep brep, DisplayMaterial material) in Preview)
        {
            args.Pipeline.DrawBrepShaded(brep, material);
        }
    }

    public void DrawViewportWires(GH_PreviewWireArgs args)
    {
        foreach ((Brep brep, DisplayMaterial _) in Preview)
        {
            args.Pipeline.DrawBrepWires(brep, args.Color);
        }
    }

    protected abstract IEnumerable<Zone> Zones { get; }

    /// <summary>
    /// The base elevation, m, of the storey the value is or comes from (<see cref="StoreyProvenance"/>, ADR-015), at which its preview is drawn below
    /// the Preview Location; 0 unless a plan or floor says so.
    /// </summary>
    protected virtual double StoreyElevation => 0.0;

    protected abstract IEnumerable<Surface> Surfaces { get; }

    /// <summary>Parts of storeys that are represented but not modelled, drawn transparent grey; none unless a building says so.</summary>
    protected virtual IEnumerable<ZonePart> UnmodelledParts => Enumerable.Empty<ZonePart>();

    private List<(Brep, DisplayMaterial)> BuildPreview()
    {
        var items = new List<(Brep, DisplayMaterial)>();
        foreach (Zone zone in Zones)
        {
            var material = new DisplayMaterial(SpaceTypeColour(zone.SpaceType), 0.6);
            IEnumerable<Brep> volumes = zone.Parts.Count > 1 ? RhinoGeometry.MergedZoneBreps(zone) : RhinoGeometry.ZoneBreps(zone);
            items.AddRange(volumes.Select(b => (b, material)));
        }

        var unmodelled = new DisplayMaterial(UnmodelledColour, 0.85);
        items.AddRange(RhinoGeometry.PartBreps(UnmodelledParts).Select(b => (b, unmodelled)));

        var glass = new DisplayMaterial(WindowColour, 0.2);
        foreach (WallSurface wall in Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>())
        {
            items.AddRange(RhinoGeometry.WindowBreps(wall).Select(b => (b, glass)));
        }

        Vector3d offset = PreviewOffset + new Vector3d(0.0, 0.0, StoreyElevation * RhinoGeometry.UnitScale);
        if (!offset.IsZero)
        {
            Transform move = Transform.Translation(offset);
            foreach ((Brep brep, DisplayMaterial _) in items)
            {
                brep.Transform(move);
            }
        }

        return items;
    }

    private static Color SpaceTypeColour(SpaceType type) => type switch
    {
        SpaceType.DwellingUnit => Color.FromArgb(240, 200, 120),
        SpaceType.Corridor => Color.FromArgb(180, 180, 180),
        SpaceType.Stair => Color.FromArgb(200, 120, 120),
        SpaceType.Core => Color.FromArgb(160, 120, 200),
        SpaceType.Mixed => Color.FromArgb(120, 190, 160),
        SpaceType.Office => Color.FromArgb(130, 170, 220),
        SpaceType.Retail => Color.FromArgb(230, 150, 170),
        SpaceType.Mall => Color.FromArgb(210, 210, 160),
        SpaceType.Kitchen => Color.FromArgb(220, 130, 90),
        SpaceType.Dining => Color.FromArgb(240, 220, 150),
        SpaceType.OperatingTheatre => Color.FromArgb(110, 190, 200),
        SpaceType.CleanCorridor => Color.FromArgb(200, 230, 230),
        SpaceType.DirtyCorridor => Color.FromArgb(160, 140, 120),
        SpaceType.ClinicalSupport => Color.FromArgb(150, 200, 180),
        SpaceType.CareBedroom => Color.FromArgb(230, 180, 200),
        SpaceType.CareCommunal => Color.FromArgb(200, 160, 220),
        SpaceType.ActivityHall => Color.FromArgb(170, 210, 120),
        _ => Color.FromArgb(150, 170, 200),
    };
}

public sealed class PlanGoo : PreviewGoo<IGeneratedPlan>
{
    public PlanGoo(IGeneratedPlan value, Vector3d previewOffset = default)

    protected override IEnumerable<Zone> Zones => Value.Zones;

    public override string ToString() => $"Building ({Value.Zones.Count} zones, {Value.Provenance.Operation})";
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Grasshopper.Kernel;
using Grasshopper.Kernel.Parameters;
using Grasshopper.Kernel.Types;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Rhino.Geometry;

namespace Lod.Grasshopper.Components;

/// <summary>Adapters between core diagnostics and Grasshopper runtime messages, text-to-enum input parsing, and the shared Preview Location input.</summary>
internal static class ComponentSupport
{
    private const string PreviewLocationName = "Preview Location";
    private const string PreviewLocationNickname = "PL";
    private const string PreviewLocationDescription =
        "Moves this component's viewport preview by this vector, in model units; the data and all outputs keep their true coordinates (D-062).";

    private const string AnyProgramPresetNote =
        " An Any Program Preset (D-108) is accepted too: its zones keep this space type and take the preset's program, and the plan's provenance records it.";

    /// <summary>Adds each diagnostic as a runtime message: errors as errors, warnings as warnings, info as remarks.</summary>
    public static void Report(GH_Component component, IEnumerable<Diagnostic> diagnostics)
    {
        foreach (Diagnostic diagnostic in diagnostics)
        {
            GH_RuntimeMessageLevel level = diagnostic.Severity switch
            {
                DiagnosticSeverity.Error => GH_RuntimeMessageLevel.Error,
                DiagnosticSeverity.Warning => GH_RuntimeMessageLevel.Warning,
                _ => GH_RuntimeMessageLevel.Remark,
            };
            component.AddRuntimeMessage(level, diagnostic.ToString());
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Reports the diagnostics and returns the value, or <c>null</c> on failure.</summary>
    public static T? Unwrap<T>(GH_Component component, Result<T> result)
        where T : class
    {
        Report(component, result.Diagnostics);
        return result.IsSuccess ? result.Value : null;
    }

    /// <summary>Parses an enum name case-insensitively; on failure adds an error listing the valid names.</summary>
    public static bool TryParse<TEnum>(GH_Component component, string text, out TEnum value)
        where TEnum : struct
    {
        if (Enum.TryParse(text?.Trim(), ignoreCase: true, out value) && Enum.IsDefined(typeof(TEnum), value))
        {
            return true;
        }

        component.AddRuntimeMessage(
            GH_RuntimeMessageLevel.Error,
            $"'{text}' is not a valid {typeof(TEnum).Name}. Use one of: {string.Join(", ", Enum.GetNames(typeof(TEnum)))}.");
        return false;
    }

    /// <summary>The valid names of an enum, for input descriptions.</summary>
    public static string Names<TEnum>() => string.Join(", ", Enum.GetNames(typeof(TEnum)).Select(n => n));

    /// <summary>
    /// The description of a plan generator's preset input (D-061), the same for every generator: its role and space type, and that an any-program
    /// preset is accepted too (D-108).
    /// </summary>
    public static string PresetInputDescription(string role, SpaceType spaceType) => $"Program preset of {role}; space type {spaceType}." + AnyProgramPresetNote;

    /// <summary>
    /// The optional Preview Location input (D-062), default zero. Register it last, <c>pManager.AddParameter(ComponentSupport.PreviewLocationParameter())</c>,
    /// so <see cref="PreviewLocation"/> finds it as the component's last input.
    /// </summary>
    public static IGH_Param PreviewLocationParameter()
    {
        var parameter = new Param_Vector
        {
            Name = PreviewLocationName,
            NickName = PreviewLocationNickname,
            Description = PreviewLocationDescription,
            Access = GH_ParamAccess.item,
            Optional = true,
        };
        parameter.SetPersistentData(new GH_Vector(Vector3d.Zero));
        return parameter;
    }

    /// <summary>Reads the Preview Location input, the component's last input; zero when it holds no vector.</summary>
    public static Vector3d PreviewLocation(GH_Component component, IGH_DataAccess DA)
    {
        Vector3d offset = Vector3d.Zero;
        return DA.GetData(component.Params.Input.Count - 1, ref offset) ? offset : Vector3d.Zero;
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using Grasshopper.Kernel;
using Grasshopper.Kernel.Types;
using Lod.Core.Buildings;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Core.Simplification;
using Lod.Generators.Linear;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Goo;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Parameters;

namespace Lod.Grasshopper.Components;

public sealed class LinearPlanGeneratorComponent : GH_Component
{
    public LinearPlanGeneratorComponent()
        : base(
            "Linear Plan Generator",
            "Linear",
            "Detailed linear block plan, the double-sided internal corridor bar (HDHM-TYP-009, ADR-006): west stair, double-loaded corridor, one zone per dwelling unit. All lengths in metres regardless of model units.",
            ComponentCategories.Category,
            ComponentCategories.Generate)
    {
    }

    public override Guid ComponentGuid => new("a4a9131e-0a0f-49e0-99a4-f26760f6810f");

    protected override Bitmap? Icon => IconLibrary.For(this);

    protected override void RegisterInputParams(GH_InputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Length", "L", "Plan length along X, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, 24.0);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Unit Depth", "UD", "Depth of each unit row, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, 8.0);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Corridor Width", "CW", "Corridor width, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, 2.0);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Unit Width", "UW", "Target unit width, m; the row is divided evenly.", GH_ParamAccess.item, 10.0);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Stair Length", "SL", "Length of the west stair, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, 4.0);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Floor Height", "FH", "Floor-to-floor height, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, 3.0);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Orientation", "O", "Clockwise rotation of plan north from true north, degrees.", GH_ParamAccess.item, 0.0);
        pManager.AddParameter(new ProgramPresetParameter(), "Dwelling Unit", "DU", ComponentSupport.PresetInputDescription("the dwelling units", SpaceType.DwellingUnit), GH_ParamAccess.item);
        pManager.AddParameter(new ProgramPresetParameter(), "Corridor", "Co", ComponentSupport.PresetInputDescription("the corridor", SpaceType.Corridor), GH_ParamAccess.item);
        pManager.AddParameter(new ProgramPresetParameter(), "Stair", "St", ComponentSupport.PresetInputDescription("the stair", SpaceType.Stair), GH_ParamAccess.item);
        pManager.AddParameter(ComponentSupport.PreviewLocationParameter());
    }

    protected override void RegisterOutputParams(GH_OutputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddParameter(new PlanParameter(), "Plan", "Plan", "The detailed plan.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
    }

    protected override void SolveInstance(IGH_DataAccess DA)
    {
        double[] values = new double[7];
        for (int i = 0; i < values.Length; i++)
        {
            if (!DA.GetData(i, ref values[i]))
            {
                return;
            }
        }

        ProgramPresetGoo? dwellingUnit = null;
        ProgramPresetGoo? corridor = null;
        ProgramPresetGoo? stair = null;
        if (!DA.GetData(7, ref dwellingUnit) || !DA.GetData(8, ref corridor) || !DA.GetData(9, ref stair)
            || dwellingUnit is null || corridor is null || stair is null)
        {
            return;
        }

        var parameters = new LinearPlanParameters(values[0], values[1], values[2], values[3], values[4], values[5], values[6]);
        var presets = new LinearPlanPresets(dwellingUnit.Value, corridor.Value, stair.Value);
        IGeneratedPlan? plan = ComponentSupport.Unwrap(this, new LinearPlanGenerator(ToleranceSettings.Default).Generate(parameters, presets));
        if (plan is not null)
        {
            DA.SetData(0, new PlanGoo(plan, ComponentSupport.PreviewLocation(this, DA)));
        }
    }
}

public sealed class NoSimplificationComponent : GH_Component
{
    public NoSimplificationComponent()
        : base("No Simplification", "Z0", "Keeps the detailed plan's zones and surfaces (Z0).", ComponentCategories.Category, ComponentCategories.Simplify)
    {
    }

    public override Guid ComponentGuid => new("83270abe-a13f-4f4c-a893-79739ad6e006");

    protected override Bitmap? Icon => IconLibrary.For(this);

    protected override void RegisterInputParams(GH_InputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddParameter(new PlanParameter(), "Plan", "Plan", "Detailed plan.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
        pManager.AddParameter(ComponentSupport.PreviewLocationParameter());
    }

    protected override void RegisterOutputParams(GH_OutputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddParameter(new FloorParameter(), "Floor", "Floor", "The floor.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
    }

    protected override void SolveInstance(IGH_DataAccess DA)
    {
        PlanGoo? plan = null;
        if (!DA.GetData(0, ref plan) || plan is null)
        {
            return;
        }

        IFloor? floor = ComponentSupport.Unwrap(this, new NoSimplification().Simplify(plan.Value));
        if (floor is not null)
        {
            DA.SetData(0, new FloorGoo(floor, ComponentSupport.PreviewLocation(this, DA)));
        }
    }
}

/// <summary>Shared inputs and solving of the floor aggregator components: floors bottom to top and their multipliers.</summary>
public abstract class FloorAggregatorComponent : GH_Component
{
    protected FloorAggregatorComponent(string name, string nickname, string description)
        : base(name, nickname, description, ComponentCategories.Category, ComponentCategories.Aggregate)
    {
    }

    protected override Bitmap? Icon => IconLibrary.For(this);

    protected abstract string MultiplierDescription { get; }

    protected override void RegisterInputParams(GH_InputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddParameter(new FloorParameter(), "Floors", "F", "Floors, bottom to top.", GH_ParamAccess.list);
        pManager.AddIntegerParameter("Multipliers", "N", MultiplierDescription, GH_ParamAccess.list);
        pManager[1].Optional = true;
        pManager.AddParameter(ComponentSupport.PreviewLocationParameter());
    }

    protected override void RegisterOutputParams(GH_OutputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddParameter(new BuildingParameter(), "Building", "Bldg", "The building.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
    }

    protected override void SolveInstance(IGH_DataAccess DA)
    {
        var floors = new List<FloorGoo>();
        var multipliers = new List<int>();
        if (!DA.GetDataList(0, floors) || floors.Any(f => f is null))
        {
            return;
        }

        DA.GetDataList(1, multipliers);
        if (multipliers.Count != 0 && multipliers.Count != floors.Count)
        {
            AddRuntimeMessage(GH_RuntimeMessageLevel.Error, $"Give one multiplier per floor ({floors.Count}), or none for all 1.");
            return;
        }

        FloorEntry[] entries = floors.Select((f, i) => new FloorEntry(f.Value, multipliers.Count == 0 ? 1 : multipliers[i])).ToArray();
        IGeneratedBuilding? building = ComponentSupport.Unwrap(this, CreateAggregator().Aggregate(entries));
        if (building is not null)
        {
            DA.SetData(0, new BuildingGoo(building, ComponentSupport.PreviewLocation(this, DA), UnmodelledParts(entries)));
        }
    }

    protected abstract IFloorAggregator CreateAggregator();

    /// <summary>Parts of the storeys the building represents but does not model, for its preview; none unless the method leaves storeys out.</summary>
    protected virtual IReadOnlyList<ZonePart> UnmodelledParts(IReadOnlyList<FloorEntry> entries) => new ZonePart[0];
}

public sealed class StackFloorsComponent : FloorAggregatorComponent
{
    public StackFloorsComponent()
        : base("Stack Floors", "Stack", "All storeys explicit: each floor is repeated by its multiplier and stacked; inter-storey surfaces are interzone.")
    {
    }

    public override Guid ComponentGuid => new("6ce6524d-09b0-49fc-9b88-4c15bdcc8797");

    protected override string MultiplierDescription => "How many storeys each floor stands for (repeated explicitly). Default 1.";

    protected override IFloorAggregator CreateAggregator() => new Stack(ToleranceSettings.Default);
}

public sealed class InspectComponent : GH_Component
{
    public InspectComponent()
        : base("Inspect", "Inspect", "Text report and provenance of a plan, floor, or building.", ComponentCategories.Category, ComponentCategories.Inspect)
    {
    }

    public override Guid ComponentGuid => new("1d56a553-7a66-451f-90e2-81e744b43bdd");

    protected override Bitmap? Icon => IconLibrary.For(this);

    protected override void RegisterInputParams(GH_InputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddGenericParameter("Object", "O", "A plan, floor, or building.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
    }

    protected override void RegisterOutputParams(GH_OutputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddTextParameter("Report", "R", "Zones, loads, and surfaces.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
        pManager.AddTextParameter("Provenance", "P", "How the object was produced, with code version.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
    }

    protected override void SolveInstance(IGH_DataAccess DA)
    {
        IGH_Goo? goo = null;
        if (!DA.GetData(0, ref goo) || goo is null)
        {
            return;
        }

        (string Report, Provenance Provenance)? described = goo switch
        {
            PlanGoo plan => (TextReport.Describe(plan.Value), plan.Value.Provenance),
            FloorGoo floor => (TextReport.Describe(floor.Value), floor.Value.Provenance),
            BuildingGoo building => (TextReport.Describe(building.Value), building.Value.Provenance),
            _ => null,
        };
        if (described is null)
        {
            AddRuntimeMessage(GH_RuntimeMessageLevel.Error, $"Expected a plan, floor, or building, got {goo.TypeName}.");
            return;
        }

        DA.SetData(0, described.Value.Report);
        DA.SetData(1, described.Value.Provenance.Describe());
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using Grasshopper.Kernel;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.BranchingMall;
using Lod.Generators.Cafeteria;
using Lod.Generators.CareHub;
using Lod.Generators.CourtCluster;
using Lod.Generators.EnclosedCourt;
using Lod.Generators.FoyerHalls;
using Lod.Generators.GalleryBar;
using Lod.Generators.OfficePlate;
using Lod.Generators.OpenCourt;
using Lod.Generators.OperatingSuite;
using Lod.Generators.PointPlate;
using Lod.Generators.RadialLobes;
using Lod.Generators.StairBayBar;
using Lod.Generators.StairPair;
using Lod.Generators.SteppedBand;
using Lod.Generators.TerraceRow;
using Lod.Generators.WingedBand;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Goo;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Parameters;
using Rhino.Geometry;

namespace Lod.Grasshopper.Components;

/// <summary>
/// Shared inputs and output of the typology plan generator components (ADR-006): number inputs with the family's defaults, one required preset input
/// per space type (D-061, D-065), the Preview Location last (D-062), and one Plan output: one plan, or, for a family whose plan differs per storey,
/// the list of storey plans bottom-up (ADR-015).
/// </summary>
public abstract class TypologyGeneratorComponent : GH_Component
{
    protected const string FloorHeightDescription = "Floor-to-floor height, m.";

    protected const string OrientationDescription = "Clockwise rotation of plan north from true north, degrees.";

    protected const string Metres = " All lengths in metres regardless of model units.";

    protected TypologyGeneratorComponent(string name, string nickname, string description)
        : base(name, nickname, description + Metres, ComponentCategories.Category, ComponentCategories.Generate)
    {
    }

    protected override Bitmap? Icon => IconLibrary.For(this);

    /// <summary>Whether the Plan output is the list of storey plans of a multi-storey family (ADR-015) rather than one plan.</summary>
    protected virtual bool PlanPerStorey => false;

    protected override void RegisterOutputParams(GH_OutputParamManager pManager)
    {
        if (PlanPerStorey)
        {
            pManager.AddParameter(
                new PlanParameter(),
                "Plan",
                "Plan",
                "The detailed plan of every storey, bottom-up (ADR-015). Wire it through a plan simplifier into a floor aggregator's Floors, with Multipliers unconnected (one storey each).",
                GH_ParamAccess.list);
        }
        else
        {
            pManager.AddParameter(new PlanParameter(), "Plan", "Plan", "The detailed plan.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Adds the required preset input of one space type; it also takes an any-program preset (D-108).</summary>
    protected static void AddPreset(GH_InputParamManager pManager, string name, string nickname, SpaceType spaceType, string role) =>
        pManager.AddParameter(new ProgramPresetParameter(), name, nickname, ComponentSupport.PresetInputDescription(role, spaceType), GH_ParamAccess.item);

    /// <summary>Reads <paramref name="count"/> number inputs from <paramref name="first"/>; <c>false</c> when one is missing.</summary>
    protected static bool TryNumbers(IGH_DataAccess DA, int first, int count, out double[] values)
    {
        values = new double[count];
        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
        {
            if (!DA.GetData(first + i, ref values[i]))
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    /// <summary>Reads a preset input; <c>null</c> when it is unconnected, which Grasshopper reports as a missing input (D-065).</summary>
    protected static ProgramPreset? Preset(IGH_DataAccess DA, int index)
    {
        ProgramPresetGoo? goo = null;
        return DA.GetData(index, ref goo) && goo is not null ? goo.Value : null;
    }

    /// <summary>Reports the generator's diagnostics and outputs the plan with this component's preview location.</summary>
    protected void Output(IGH_DataAccess DA, Result<IGeneratedPlan> result)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan? plan = ComponentSupport.Unwrap(this, result);
        if (plan is not null)
        {
            DA.SetData(0, new PlanGoo(plan, ComponentSupport.PreviewLocation(this, DA)));
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Reports the generator's diagnostics and outputs the storey plans, bottom-up, with this component's preview location; each storey's preview is
    /// drawn at the elevation recorded in its plan (<see cref="Lod.Core.Plans.StoreyProvenance"/>), while the plans keep elevation 0 (ADR-015, D-108).
    /// </summary>
    protected void Output(IGH_DataAccess DA, Result<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>> result)
    {
        IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>? plans = ComponentSupport.Unwrap(this, result);
        if (plans is not null)
        {
            Vector3d location = ComponentSupport.PreviewLocation(this, DA);
            DA.SetDataList(0, plans.Select(p => new PlanGoo(p, location)));
        }
    }
}
```
