- [x] **Step 6: Build, check Python, and see the icon script fail**

```bash
dotnet build BEMGen.sln -c Release
python -c "import sys, PIL; print(sys.version_info[:2] >= (3, 10), PIL.__version__)"
python scripts/make_icons.py
git status --short
```

Expected: `Build succeeded.`, `0 Warning(s)`, `0 Error(s)`; `True 12.3.0`; the script stops with exit code 1 and the message `Classes without an icon: ['AnyProgramPresetParameter']; icons without a class: []`, and writes nothing (`git status --short` lists only the five ` M` files of Steps 2 to 5). With another Pillow version the script still runs, but the PNG bytes may differ from Step 8's hashes; then Step 9 decides.

- [x] **Step 7: Replace `scripts/make_icons.py` (full file)**

The motif is added after `small_preset`, and its entry after `ProgramPresetParameter`; every other motif and entry is unchanged.

```python
"""Draws the BEMGen Grasshopper icons (D-063).

Every icon is drawn at 96 x 96 pixels on a transparent background and downsampled to 24 x 24 with LANCZOS, then written to
src/Lod.Grasshopper/Icons/<Name>.png, where <Name> is the class name of the component, parameter, or assembly info that shows it.
Each icon is a dark glyph plus the accent colour of its toolbar panel; a parameter uses the panel of the component that makes its object.
Shapes only, no fonts, so the output does not depend on installed fonts.

Run from the repository root:  python scripts/make_icons.py
Requires Python 3.10+ and Pillow. Generated with Pillow 12.3.0; the same Pillow version gives the same pixels.
The script stops with an error when an icon has no class in src/Lod.Grasshopper or a class has no icon.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
import sys
from collections.abc import Callable
from pathlib import Path

from PIL import Image, ImageDraw

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
SOURCE = ROOT / "src" / "Lod.Grasshopper"
OUTPUT = SOURCE / "Icons"

CANVAS = 96
SIZE = 24
LINE = 8  # 2 px after downsampling

DARK = (38, 38, 38, 255)
WHITE = (255, 255, 255, 255)

# One accent per toolbar panel (ComponentCategories).
INFO = (96, 125, 139, 255)
PROGRAM = (230, 140, 30, 255)
GENERATE = (46, 158, 79, 255)
SIMPLIFY = (47, 123, 216, 255)
AGGREGATE = (138, 79, 200, 255)
CONVERT = (208, 69, 58, 255)
INSPECT = (24, 150, 150, 255)

Colour = tuple[int, int, int, int]
Draw = ImageDraw.ImageDraw
Motif = Callable[[Draw, Colour], None]
Painter = Callable[[Image.Image, Colour], None]


def box(d: Draw, xy: tuple[float, float, float, float], fill=None, outline=DARK, width: int = LINE) -> None:
    d.rectangle(xy, fill=fill, outline=outline, width=width)


def line(d: Draw, points: list[tuple[float, float]], width: int = LINE, fill=DARK) -> None:
    d.line(points, fill=fill, width=width, joint="curve")


def arrow_head(d: Draw, tip: tuple[float, float], direction: str, size: float, fill=DARK) -> None:
    x, y = tip
    if direction == "right":
        d.polygon([(x, y), (x - size, y - size), (x - size, y + size)], fill=fill)
    elif direction == "down":
        d.polygon([(x, y), (x - size, y - size), (x + size, y - size)], fill=fill)


# --- motifs: each draws inside the 96 x 96 canvas -------------------------------------------------------------


def info(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.ellipse((6, 6, 90, 90), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.rectangle((42, 40, 54, 74), fill=WHITE)
    d.rectangle((42, 20, 54, 32), fill=WHITE)


def assembly(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A stepped block: the building at three levels of detail.
    box(d, (8, 52, 88, 88), fill=accent)
    box(d, (8, 30, 60, 56), fill=accent)
    box(d, (8, 8, 36, 34), fill=accent)


def schedule(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A 24-hour profile as bars on a time axis.
    heights = [16, 48, 72, 36, 60, 76, 24]
    for i, h in enumerate(heights):
        x = 12 + i * 12
        d.rectangle((x, 84 - h, x + 7, 83), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((4, 84, 92, 91), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((4, 4, 11, 91), fill=DARK)


def load(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    bolt = [(58, 4), (18, 54), (44, 54), (34, 92), (78, 38), (52, 38), (64, 4)]
    d.polygon(bolt, fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)


def preset_tag(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    tag = [(8, 30), (30, 8), (88, 8), (88, 88), (30, 88), (8, 66)]
    d.polygon(tag, fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.ellipse((24, 40, 40, 56), fill=WHITE, outline=DARK, width=4)
    line(d, [(52, 34), (76, 34)], width=6)
    line(d, [(52, 48), (76, 48)], width=6)
    line(d, [(52, 62), (76, 62)], width=6)


def house(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.polygon([(48, 6), (90, 44), (6, 44)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    box(d, (16, 42, 80, 90), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((40, 60, 56, 90), fill=DARK)


def corridor(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (4, 30, 92, 66), fill=accent)
    line(d, [(16, 48), (60, 48)], width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (78, 48), "right", 12)


def stair(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    steps = [(6, 90), (6, 70), (28, 70), (28, 50), (50, 50), (50, 30), (72, 30), (72, 10), (90, 10), (90, 90)]
    d.polygon(steps, fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)


def office(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # An office block in elevation with a grid of windows.
    box(d, (16, 6, 80, 90), fill=accent)
    for y in (18, 38, 58):
        for x in (26, 52):
            d.rectangle((x, y, x + 16, y + 12), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((40, 76, 56, 90), fill=DARK)


def core(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A service core: a box with two lift doors and an up and down arrow.
    box(d, (8, 8, 88, 88), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((20, 34, 42, 80), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((54, 34, 76, 80), fill=DARK)
    d.polygon([(48, 12), (38, 26), (58, 26)], fill=DARK)


def retail(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A shopping bag with its handle.
    d.arc((30, 6, 66, 46), start=180, end=360, fill=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.polygon([(14, 30), (82, 30), (90, 90), (6, 90)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)


def mall(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A branching mall in plan: shop blocks (accent) along a T of public routes (white).
    box(d, (4, 4, 92, 92), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((40, 8, 56, 88), fill=WHITE)
    d.rectangle((8, 40, 40, 56), fill=WHITE)
    for a, b in [((40, 4), (40, 40)), ((56, 4), (56, 92)), ((40, 56), (40, 92)), ((4, 40), (40, 40)), ((4, 56), (40, 56))]:
        line(d, [a, b], width=4)


def kitchen(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A cooking pot with a lid and two handles.
    box(d, (16, 40, 80, 86), fill=accent)
    line(d, [(4, 50), (16, 50)])
    line(d, [(80, 50), (92, 50)])
    d.rectangle((12, 28, 84, 38), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((42, 16, 54, 28), fill=DARK)


def dining(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A plate between a fork and a knife.
    d.ellipse((26, 22, 74, 70), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.ellipse((38, 34, 62, 58), outline=DARK, width=4)
    line(d, [(12, 14), (12, 86)], width=6)
    line(d, [(84, 14), (84, 86)], width=6)
    d.rectangle((80, 14, 88, 46), fill=DARK)


def operating_theatre(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A medical cross.
    d.polygon(
        [(34, 6), (62, 6), (62, 34), (90, 34), (90, 62), (62, 62), (62, 90), (34, 90), (34, 62), (6, 62), (6, 34), (34, 34)],
        fill=accent,
        outline=DARK,
        width=LINE,
    )


def clean_corridor(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A corridor with a white (clean) route and an arrow.
    box(d, (4, 30, 92, 66), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((10, 42, 70, 54), fill=WHITE)
    arrow_head(d, (86, 48), "right", 12)


def dirty_corridor(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A corridor with a hatched (dirty) route and an arrow.
    box(d, (4, 30, 92, 66), fill=accent)
    for x in range(12, 66, 14):
        line(d, [(x, 60), (x + 10, 36)], width=5)
    arrow_head(d, (86, 48), "right", 12)


def clinical_support(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Shelves of supplies beside a small medical cross.
    box(d, (6, 10, 58, 90), fill=accent)
    for y in (34, 58):
        line(d, [(6, y), (58, y)], width=6)
    d.rectangle((70, 28, 82, 64), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((58, 40, 94, 52), fill=DARK)


def care_bedroom(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A bed in elevation: headboard, mattress, and pillow.
    d.rectangle((6, 24, 16, 86), fill=DARK)
    box(d, (16, 54, 90, 72), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((20, 42, 42, 54), fill=DARK)
    line(d, [(86, 72), (86, 86)])


def care_communal(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A round table with four people around it.
    d.ellipse((28, 28, 68, 68), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    for x, y in [(48, 12), (84, 48), (48, 84), (12, 48)]:
        d.ellipse((x - 9, y - 9, x + 9, y + 9), fill=DARK)


def lobby(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A glazed entrance front with an open door and an arrow leading in.
    box(d, (6, 10, 90, 88), fill=accent)
    line(d, [(34, 10), (34, 88)], width=4)
    line(d, [(62, 10), (62, 88)], width=4)
    d.rectangle((38, 44, 58, 88), fill=WHITE, outline=DARK, width=4)
    arrow_head(d, (48, 84), "down", 8)


def activity_hall(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A sports court in plan: the hall, its centre line, and the centre circle.
    box(d, (4, 16, 92, 80), fill=accent)
    line(d, [(48, 16), (48, 80)], width=5)
    d.ellipse((34, 34, 62, 62), outline=DARK, width=5)


def service_rooms(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A row of three lockers with their handles.
    for left in (6, 36, 66):
        box(d, (left, 8, left + 24, 88), fill=accent, width=6)
        d.rectangle((left + 15, 40, left + 19, 56), fill=DARK)


def linear_generator(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Footprint with a west stair, a corridor band, and dwelling-unit divisions on both sides.
    box(d, (4, 12, 91, 83))
    d.rectangle((12, 20, 23, 75), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((28, 40, 87, 55), fill=accent)
    for x in (48, 68):
        line(d, [(x, 12), (x, 40)])
        line(d, [(x, 55), (x, 83)])


def stair_bay_bar(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A long bar of two stair bays: dwelling (accent) | stair (dark) | dwelling, repeated, with a party wall between the bays.
    box(d, (2, 30, 93, 66), fill=accent)
    for x in (20, 66):
        d.rectangle((x, 30, x + 10, 66), fill=DARK)
    line(d, [(48, 30), (48, 66)], width=4)


def stair_pair(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # One compact plate: two dwellings (accent) around one full-depth stair (dark).
    box(d, (14, 22, 82, 74), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((42, 26, 54, 70), fill=DARK)


def gallery_bar(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A bar of dwellings (accent) between two end stairs (dark); the gallery along it is dashed, as it is not modelled.
    box(d, (4, 34, 91, 76), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((8, 38, 18, 72), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((77, 38, 87, 72), fill=DARK)
    for x in (36, 54):
        line(d, [(x, 34), (x, 76)], width=6)
    for x in range(6, 90, 16):
        line(d, [(x, 20), (x + 9, 20)], width=6)


def terrace_row(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Attached houses in elevation: three gables sharing their side walls.
    houses = [(6, 34), (34, 62), (62, 90)]
    for left, right in houses:
        middle = (left + right) / 2
        d.polygon([(left, 46), (middle, 22), (right, 46), (right, 88), (left, 88)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)
    for left, right in houses:
        d.rectangle((left + 10, 68, right - 10, 88), fill=DARK)


def point_plate(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A square plate: four dwellings (accent) in a pinwheel around one central core (dark) without façades.
    box(d, (6, 6, 90, 90), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((36, 36, 60, 60), fill=DARK)
    for a, b in [((6, 36), (60, 36)), ((60, 6), (60, 60)), ((36, 60), (90, 60)), ((36, 36), (36, 90))]:
        line(d, [a, b], width=6)


def winged_band(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A comb in plan: a band (bottom) with three wings projecting north (up), every wing joined to the band along its root; stairs dark.
    d.polygon(
        [(4, 90), (92, 90), (92, 8), (70, 8), (70, 62), (59, 62), (59, 8), (37, 8), (37, 62), (26, 62), (26, 8), (4, 8)],
        fill=accent,
        outline=DARK,
        width=6,
    )
    for left, right in ((4, 26), (37, 59), (70, 92)):
        d.rectangle((left + 3, 30, right - 3, 38), fill=DARK)
    for x in (20, 48, 76):
        d.rectangle((x, 66, x + 6, 87), fill=DARK)


def open_court(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A U in plan: a north wing (top) across the full width and two side wings, around a court open to the south (bottom); stairs dark.
    d.polygon([(4, 92), (28, 92), (28, 34), (68, 34), (68, 92), (92, 92), (92, 6), (4, 6)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)
    for x in (24, 45, 66):
        d.rectangle((x, 9, x + 6, 31), fill=DARK)
    for left in (4, 68):
        d.rectangle((left + 3, 58, left + 21, 66), fill=DARK)


def enclosed_court(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A closed ring in plan: south and north wings across the full width and west and east wings between them, around a court (the hole,
    # transparent) closed on all four sides; stairs dark.
    box(d, (4, 4, 92, 92), fill=accent, width=6)
    box(d, (30, 30, 66, 66), fill=(0, 0, 0, 0), width=6)
    for y in (7, 69):
        for x in (24, 66):
            d.rectangle((x, y, x + 6, y + 20), fill=DARK)
    for left in (7, 69):
        d.rectangle((left, 45, left + 20, 51), fill=DARK)


def office_plate(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A rectangular plate: one office work area (accent) all around a central service core (dark).
    box(d, (4, 16, 92, 80), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((32, 40, 64, 56), fill=DARK)


def cafeteria(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A rectangle: the kitchen and servery along the north edge (dark) and the dining field (accent) with tables.
    box(d, (4, 10, 92, 86), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((8, 14, 88, 34), fill=DARK)
    for y in (48, 68):
        for x in (20, 44, 68):
            d.ellipse((x, y, x + 10, y + 10), fill=DARK)


def transform_plan(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A footprint outline and the same footprint rotated and moved, joined by a turning arrow.
    box(d, (6, 52, 50, 90), width=6)
    d.polygon([(66, 12), (92, 28), (76, 56), (50, 40)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)
    d.arc((18, 20, 70, 72), start=180, end=270, fill=DARK, width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (58, 24), "right", 10)


def z0(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Many cells: every unit its own zone.
    box(d, (4, 4, 91, 91), fill=accent)
    for k in (32, 60):
        line(d, [(k, 4), (k, 91)])
        line(d, [(4, k), (91, k)])


def z1(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Cells of one space type merged: two unit rows and a corridor.
    box(d, (4, 10, 92, 40), fill=accent)
    box(d, (4, 56, 92, 86), fill=accent)
    box(d, (4, 36, 92, 60))


def z2(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Perimeter ring split on the corner bisectors, plus a core.
    box(d, (4, 4, 92, 92))
    d.rectangle((30, 30, 66, 66), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    for a, b in [((4, 4), (30, 30)), ((92, 4), (66, 30)), ((4, 92), (30, 66)), ((92, 92), (66, 66))]:
        line(d, [a, b], width=6)


def z3(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (6, 16, 90, 80), fill=accent)


def slab(d: Draw, accent, y: float, height: float = 16, left: float = 6, right: float = 90) -> None:
    box(d, (left, y, right, y + height), fill=accent, width=6)


def stack(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    for y in (8, 36, 64):
        slab(d, accent, y, 24)


def multiplier(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    slab(d, accent, 6, 24, 6, 90)
    line(d, [(18, 44), (50, 84)], width=10)
    line(d, [(50, 44), (18, 84)], width=10)
    for y in (44, 58, 72):
        d.rectangle((62, y, 90, y + 8), fill=DARK)


def single_zone_per_floor_type(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # One zone per floor entry: a one-storey zone below a three-storey zone.
    box(d, (24, 66, 71, 91), fill=accent)
    box(d, (24, 4, 71, 58), fill=accent)


def single_zone_building(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (24, 4, 71, 91), fill=accent)


def convert(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.polygon([(40, 44), (90, 44), (90, 92), (40, 92)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    line(d, [(6, 14), (44, 14), (64, 34), (64, 56)], width=10)
    arrow_head(d, (64, 74), "down", 16)


def inspect(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.ellipse((6, 6, 64, 64), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.ellipse((18, 18, 38, 38), fill=WHITE)
    line(d, [(56, 56), (88, 88)], width=16)


def mini_grid(d: Draw, origin: tuple[float, float], size: float, cells: int, accent) -> None:
    x0, y0 = origin
    box(d, (x0, y0, x0 + size, y0 + size), fill=accent, width=6)
    step = size / cells
    for k in range(1, cells):
        line(d, [(x0 + k * step, y0), (x0 + k * step, y0 + size)], width=4)
        line(d, [(x0, y0 + k * step), (x0 + size, y0 + k * step)], width=4)


def map_source_target(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    mini_grid(d, (4, 4), 40, 2, accent)
    box(d, (52, 52, 92, 92), fill=accent, width=6)
    line(d, [(32, 56), (32, 72), (40, 72)], width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (52, 72), "right", 12)
    line(d, [(56, 26), (72, 26), (72, 36)], width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (72, 50), "down", 12)


def validate(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.ellipse((4, 4, 92, 92), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    line(d, [(24, 50), (42, 68), (74, 30)], width=14, fill=WHITE)


# Parameter motifs: small versions of their object's motif, drawn inside the hexagon (x and y in 20..76).


def small_schedule(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    for x, h in ((28, 16), (40, 36), (52, 24), (64, 40)):
        d.rectangle((x, 72 - h, x + 7, 71), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((24, 72, 75, 75), fill=DARK)


def small_load(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.polygon([(54, 20), (30, 52), (46, 52), (40, 76), (68, 44), (52, 44), (60, 20)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=4)


def small_preset(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.polygon([(20, 36), (32, 24), (76, 24), (76, 72), (32, 72), (20, 60)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=4)
    d.ellipse((30, 42, 42, 54), fill=WHITE, outline=DARK, width=4)


def small_any_preset(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # The small preset tag with a wildcard asterisk where its hole was: a preset of any program, without a space type (D-108).
    d.polygon([(20, 36), (32, 24), (76, 24), (76, 72), (32, 72), (20, 60)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=4)
    for (x0, y0), (x1, y1) in (((52, 34), (52, 62)), ((40, 41), (64, 55)), ((40, 55), (64, 41))):
        line(d, [(x0, y0), (x1, y1)], width=6)


def small_plan(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (20, 24, 75, 71), width=4)
    d.rectangle((24, 44, 71, 51), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((24, 28, 31, 67), fill=DARK)
    for x in (48,):
        line(d, [(x, 24), (x, 44)], width=4)
        line(d, [(x, 52), (x, 71)], width=4)


def small_floor(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (16, 36, 79, 59), fill=accent, width=4)


def small_building(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (32, 16, 63, 79), fill=accent, width=4)
    for y in (36, 56):
        line(d, [(32, y), (63, y)], width=4)


def radial_lobes(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A cross in plan: four lobes (accent) on the sides of a square core (dark), each lobe split along its axis into two dwellings.
    d.polygon(
        [(32, 4), (64, 4), (64, 32), (92, 32), (92, 64), (64, 64), (64, 92), (32, 92), (32, 64), (4, 64), (4, 32), (32, 32)],
        fill=accent,
        outline=DARK,
        width=6,
    )
    d.rectangle((32, 32, 64, 64), fill=DARK)
    for a, b in [((64, 48), (92, 48)), ((48, 4), (48, 32)), ((4, 48), (32, 48)), ((48, 64), (48, 92))]:
        line(d, [a, b], width=4)


def branching_mall(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A T in plan: shop wings (accent) along a mall (dark) that branches from a central court into three branches, anchors at the branch ends.
    d.polygon([(4, 50), (34, 50), (34, 6), (62, 6), (62, 50), (92, 50), (92, 82), (4, 82)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)
    d.rectangle((18, 62, 78, 70), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((44, 20, 52, 70), fill=DARK)
    for a, b in [((18, 50), (18, 82)), ((78, 50), (78, 82)), ((34, 20), (62, 20))]:
        line(d, [a, b], width=6)


def care_hub(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A communal hub (accent, a table in its middle) with three narrower bedroom wings (accent), each with a corridor (dark) along its axis.
    for xy in [(66, 49, 92, 75), (4, 49, 30, 75), (35, 6, 61, 42)]:
        box(d, xy, fill=accent, width=6)
    box(d, (30, 42, 66, 82), fill=accent, width=6)
    for a, b in [((66, 62), (92, 62)), ((4, 62), (30, 62)), ((48, 6), (48, 42))]:
        line(d, [a, b], width=4)
    d.ellipse((40, 54, 56, 70), fill=DARK)


def foyer_halls(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A foyer (white) with two large halls (accent) east and west, the support wing (dark) north, and its entrance from the south.
    box(d, (4, 40, 34, 74), fill=accent, width=6)
    box(d, (62, 40, 92, 74), fill=accent, width=6)
    box(d, (34, 18, 62, 40), fill=DARK, width=6)
    box(d, (34, 40, 62, 74), fill=WHITE, width=6)
    d.polygon([(48, 78), (40, 90), (56, 90)], fill=DARK)


def operating_suite(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Support rooms (accent) across the west end and bands to their east, south to north: a dirty corridor (dark), a theatre bank (accent), a
    # clean corridor (white), a theatre bank, and a dirty corridor.
    box(d, (4, 6, 92, 90), fill=accent, width=6)
    line(d, [(30, 6), (30, 90)], width=6)
    d.rectangle((30, 6, 92, 20), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((30, 76, 92, 90), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((30, 40, 92, 56), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((33, 44, 89, 52), fill=WHITE)


def court_cluster(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Wings around a row of two courts (the holes, transparent): south and north wings across the full width, and a west, middle, and east wing
    # between them; stairs dark.
    box(d, (4, 16, 92, 80), fill=accent, width=6)
    for left in (18, 56):
        box(d, (left, 30, left + 22, 66), fill=(0, 0, 0, 0), width=6)
    for y in (19, 69):
        for x in (24, 46, 68):
            d.rectangle((x, y, x + 5, y + 8), fill=DARK)
    for left in (7, 43, 81):
        d.rectangle((left, 45, left + 8, 51), fill=DARK)


def stepped_band(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A section through the band: three storeys stepping back from the south (left), so each lower roof is a terrace; the rear corridor dark.
    for left, top in ((6, 64), (32, 38), (58, 12)):
        box(d, (left, top, 90, top + 26), fill=accent, width=6)
    d.rectangle((76, 12, 90, 90), fill=DARK)


# --- icons ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


def component(motif: Motif) -> Painter:
    """A component: its motif at full size."""

    def paint(image: Image.Image, accent: Colour) -> None:
        motif(ImageDraw.Draw(image), accent)

    return paint


def parameter(motif: Motif) -> Painter:
    """A parameter: a small version of its object's motif inside a hexagon (the Grasshopper parameter shape)."""

    def paint(image: Image.Image, accent: Colour) -> None:
        d = ImageDraw.Draw(image)
        d.polygon([(24, 4), (72, 4), (94, 48), (72, 92), (24, 92), (2, 48)], fill=WHITE, outline=DARK, width=4)
        motif(d, accent)

    return paint


ICONS: dict[str, tuple[Colour, Painter]] = {
    "BemGenAssemblyInfo": (INFO, component(assembly)),
    "BemGenInfoComponent": (INFO, component(info)),
    "ScheduleComponent": (PROGRAM, component(schedule)),
    "LoadComponent": (PROGRAM, component(load)),
    "ProgramPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(preset_tag)),
    "DwellingUnitPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(house)),
    "CorridorPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(corridor)),
    "StairPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(stair)),
    "OfficePresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(office)),
    "CorePresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(core)),
    "RetailPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(retail)),
    "MallPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(mall)),
    "KitchenPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(kitchen)),
    "DiningPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(dining)),
    "OperatingTheatrePresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(operating_theatre)),
    "CleanCorridorPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(clean_corridor)),
    "DirtyCorridorPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(dirty_corridor)),
    "ClinicalSupportPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(clinical_support)),
    "CareBedroomPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(care_bedroom)),
    "CareCommunalPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(care_communal)),
    "LobbyPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(lobby)),
    "ActivityHallPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(activity_hall)),
    "ServicePresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(service_rooms)),
    "LinearPlanGeneratorComponent": (GENERATE, component(linear_generator)),
    "StairBayBarComponent": (GENERATE, component(stair_bay_bar)),
    "StairPairComponent": (GENERATE, component(stair_pair)),
    "GalleryBarComponent": (GENERATE, component(gallery_bar)),
    "TerraceRowComponent": (GENERATE, component(terrace_row)),
    "PointPlateComponent": (GENERATE, component(point_plate)),
    "WingedBandComponent": (GENERATE, component(winged_band)),
    "OpenCourtComponent": (GENERATE, component(open_court)),
    "EnclosedCourtComponent": (GENERATE, component(enclosed_court)),
    "OfficePlateComponent": (GENERATE, component(office_plate)),
    "CafeteriaComponent": (GENERATE, component(cafeteria)),
    "RadialLobesComponent": (GENERATE, component(radial_lobes)),
    "BranchingMallComponent": (GENERATE, component(branching_mall)),
    "CareHubComponent": (GENERATE, component(care_hub)),
    "FoyerHallsComponent": (GENERATE, component(foyer_halls)),
    "OperatingSuiteComponent": (GENERATE, component(operating_suite)),
    "CourtClusterComponent": (GENERATE, component(court_cluster)),
    "SteppedBandComponent": (GENERATE, component(stepped_band)),
    "TransformPlanComponent": (GENERATE, component(transform_plan)),
    "NoSimplificationComponent": (SIMPLIFY, component(z0)),
    "SemanticMergeComponent": (SIMPLIFY, component(z1)),
    "PerimeterCoreComponent": (SIMPLIFY, component(z2)),
    "SingleZonePerFloorComponent": (SIMPLIFY, component(z3)),
    "StackFloorsComponent": (AGGREGATE, component(stack)),
    "StackedFloorZoneMultiplierComponent": (AGGREGATE, component(multiplier)),
    "SingleZonePerFloorTypeComponent": (AGGREGATE, component(single_zone_per_floor_type)),
    "SingleZoneBuildingComponent": (AGGREGATE, component(single_zone_building)),
    "Convert2BemComponent": (CONVERT, component(convert)),
    "InspectComponent": (INSPECT, component(inspect)),
    "MapSourceToTargetComponent": (INSPECT, component(map_source_target)),
    "ValidateComponent": (INSPECT, component(validate)),
    "ScheduleParameter": (PROGRAM, parameter(small_schedule)),
    "LoadParameter": (PROGRAM, parameter(small_load)),
    "ProgramPresetParameter": (PROGRAM, parameter(small_preset)),
    "AnyProgramPresetParameter": (PROGRAM, parameter(small_any_preset)),
    "PlanParameter": (GENERATE, parameter(small_plan)),
    "FloorParameter": (SIMPLIFY, parameter(small_floor)),
    "BuildingParameter": (AGGREGATE, parameter(small_building)),
}


def render(name: str) -> Image.Image:
    accent, paint = ICONS[name]
    image = Image.new("RGBA", (CANVAS, CANVAS), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    paint(image, accent)
    return image.resize((SIZE, SIZE), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)


def classes_with_icons() -> set[str]:
    """Concrete classes that show an icon: components, parameters, and the assembly info."""
    pattern = re.compile(r"public sealed class (\w+) : (\w+)")
    names = set()
    for path in SOURCE.rglob("*.cs"):
        if "obj" in path.parts or "bin" in path.parts:
            continue
        for name, _ in pattern.findall(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")):
            if name.endswith(("Component", "Parameter")) or name == "BemGenAssemblyInfo":
                names.add(name)
    return names


def main() -> int:
    expected = classes_with_icons()
    missing = sorted(expected - ICONS.keys())
    unknown = sorted(ICONS.keys() - expected)
    if missing or unknown:
        print(f"Classes without an icon: {missing}; icons without a class: {unknown}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1

    OUTPUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    stale = sorted(p.name for p in OUTPUT.glob("*.png") if p.stem not in ICONS)
    if stale:
        print(f"Icons without a class in {OUTPUT}: {stale}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1

    for name in sorted(ICONS):
        render(name).save(OUTPUT / f"{name}.png", optimize=False)
    print(f"Wrote {len(ICONS)} icons to {OUTPUT.relative_to(ROOT)}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

- [x] **Step 9: Look at the icons**

Draw a contact sheet of all icons at 8× (nearest neighbour) into the build folder, which git ignores:

```bash
python - <<'EOF'
from pathlib import Path
from PIL import Image
icons = sorted(Path("src/Lod.Grasshopper/Icons").glob("*.png"))
cell = 24 * 8 + 16
sheet = Image.new("RGBA", (6 * cell, -(-len(icons) // 6) * cell), (235, 235, 235, 255))
for i, path in enumerate(icons):
    big = Image.open(path).convert("RGBA").resize((24 * 8, 24 * 8), Image.Resampling.NEAREST)
    sheet.alpha_composite(big, ((i % 6) * cell + 8, (i // 6) * cell + 8))
Path("src/Lod.Grasshopper/bin").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
sheet.save("src/Lod.Grasshopper/bin/icon-sheet.png")
print(len(icons), "icons drawn on src/Lod.Grasshopper/bin/icon-sheet.png")
EOF
```

Expected: `61 icons drawn on src/Lod.Grasshopper/bin/icon-sheet.png`.

Open the image. In the order of `sorted()` on Windows (case-insensitive), six per row: *Any Program Preset* is the second icon of the first row, after *Activity Hall Preset* (a white parameter hexagon holding an orange preset tag with a dark asterisk where the hole of the *Program Preset* parameter's tag is), and before *BEMGen Assembly Info*. Every icon after it moves one place on, the 60 icons of S8.7 keep their motifs (the *Program Preset* parameter, a hexagon with a tag and its hole, is the last icon of the seventh row), and the 61 icons fill ten rows of six and one icon of an eleventh row.

If the hashes of Step 8 differed (another Pillow version), this check decides: when every icon matches the description above, continue and record the Pillow version in the commit message body; otherwise stop and report the difference. The script is never edited to match hashes.

### Task 7: The built-in preset components in their own panel

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/Lod.Grasshopper/ComponentCategories.cs` (S0 file), `src/Lod.Grasshopper/Components/PresetComponents.cs` (S4.1 file, last changed in S8.4)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ComponentCategories` (S0: `Category`, `Info`, `Program`, `Generate`, …), `DefaultPresetComponent` (S4.1), the base of the eighteen default preset components (three of S4.1, fifteen of S8.4).
- Produces: `public const string ProgramPresets = "1 Program Presets"`, after `Program`; `DefaultPresetComponent` registers its components in it, so the eighteen default preset components move to the panel *1 Program Presets* with their GUIDs, icons (the Program accent), and inputs unchanged. *Schedule*, *Load*, *Program Preset*, and *Any Program Preset* stay in *1 Program*.

- [x] **Step 1: Replace `src/Lod.Grasshopper/ComponentCategories.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
namespace Lod.Grasshopper;

/// <summary>Toolbar tab and panels, ordered along the pipeline.</summary>
internal static class ComponentCategories
{
    public const string Category = "BEMGen";

    public const string Info = "0 Info";

    public const string Program = "1 Program";

    /// <summary>The built-in default preset components, one per space type (D-061, ADR-014, D-108).</summary>
    public const string ProgramPresets = "1 Program Presets";

    public const string Generate = "2 Generate";

    public const string Simplify = "3 Simplify";

    public const string Aggregate = "4 Aggregate";

    public const string Convert = "5 Convert";

    public const string Inspect = "6 Inspect";
}
```

- [x] **Step 2: Replace `src/Lod.Grasshopper/Components/PresetComponents.cs` (full file)**

Only the summary and the panel of `DefaultPresetComponent`'s constructor change.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using Grasshopper.Kernel;
using Lod.Core.Loads;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Goo;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Parameters;

namespace Lod.Grasshopper.Components;

/// <summary>
/// Shared inputs and solving of the default preset components (D-061), in their own panel 1 Program Presets (D-108): one component per space type
/// whose inputs all default to its <see cref="PresetValues"/>, so it works with nothing connected. Inputs: Name, WWR, Conditioned, Heating, Cooling,
/// then a value and an optional schedule per load of the defaults, in their order.
/// </summary>
public abstract class DefaultPresetComponent : GH_Component
{
    private const string IllustrativeRemark = "Illustrative values; not sourced from DOE prototypes or standards.";
    private const int FirstLoadInput = 5;

    protected DefaultPresetComponent(string name, string nickname, string description)
        : base(name, nickname, description, ComponentCategories.Category, ComponentCategories.ProgramPresets)
    {
    }

    protected override Bitmap? Icon => IconLibrary.For(this);

    /// <summary>The default values; read while inputs are registered, so they must not depend on instance state.</summary>
    protected abstract PresetValues Defaults { get; }

    protected override void RegisterInputParams(GH_InputParamManager pManager)
    {
        PresetValues defaults = Defaults;
        pManager.AddTextParameter("Name", "N", "Preset name; descriptive only (reports, provenance, messages), never used for matching.", GH_ParamAccess.item, defaults.Name);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("WWR", "WWR", "Window-to-wall ratio in [0, 1) used by the plan generator for every exterior wall.", GH_ParamAccess.item, defaults.WindowToWallRatio);
        pManager.AddBooleanParameter("Conditioned", "Cond", "Whether the space is conditioned (D-038); the setpoints are ignored when it is not.", GH_ParamAccess.item, defaults.Conditioned);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Heating", "H", "Constant heating setpoint, °C.", GH_ParamAccess.item, defaults.HeatingSetpoint);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Cooling", "C", "Constant cooling setpoint, °C.", GH_ParamAccess.item, defaults.CoolingSetpoint);
        foreach (PresetLoadValue load in defaults.Loads)
        {
            (string label, string nickname) = LoadLabel(defaults.Loads, load);
            pManager.AddNumberParameter(label, nickname, $"{label} design value, {Unit(load.Type, load.Basis)}.", GH_ParamAccess.item, load.Value);
            int schedule = pManager.AddParameter(
                new ScheduleParameter(),
                $"{label} Schedule",
                nickname + "S",
                $"Optional fraction schedule of {label}; replaces the built-in schedule '{load.Schedule.Name}' when connected.",
                GH_ParamAccess.item);
            pManager[schedule].Optional = true;
        }
    }

    protected override void RegisterOutputParams(GH_OutputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddParameter(new ProgramPresetParameter(), "Preset", "P", "The program preset.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
    }

    protected override void SolveInstance(IGH_DataAccess DA)
    {
        AddRuntimeMessage(GH_RuntimeMessageLevel.Remark, IllustrativeRemark);
        PresetValues defaults = Defaults;
        string name = string.Empty;
        double wwr = 0.0;
        bool conditioned = false;
        double heating = 0.0;
        double cooling = 0.0;
        if (!DA.GetData(0, ref name) || !DA.GetData(1, ref wwr) || !DA.GetData(2, ref conditioned) || !DA.GetData(3, ref heating) || !DA.GetData(4, ref cooling))
        {
            return;
        }

        var loads = new PresetLoadValue[defaults.Loads.Count];
        for (int k = 0; k < loads.Length; k++)
        {
            double value = 0.0;
            ScheduleGoo? schedule = null;
            if (!DA.GetData(FirstLoadInput + (2 * k), ref value))
            {
                return;
            }

            DA.GetData(FirstLoadInput + (2 * k) + 1, ref schedule);
            loads[k] = defaults.Loads[k] with { Value = value, Schedule = schedule?.Value ?? defaults.Loads[k].Schedule };
        }

        PresetValues values = defaults with
        {
            Name = name,
            WindowToWallRatio = wwr,
            Conditioned = conditioned,
            HeatingSetpoint = heating,
            CoolingSetpoint = cooling,
            Loads = loads,
        };
        ProgramPreset? preset = ComponentSupport.Unwrap(this, values.ToPreset());
        if (preset is not null)
        {
            DA.SetData(0, new ProgramPresetGoo(preset));
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Input name and nickname of a load; the basis is added when the defaults hold the load type in more than one basis (D-047).</summary>
    private static (string Label, string Nickname) LoadLabel(IReadOnlyList<PresetLoadValue> loads, PresetLoadValue load)
    {
        (string label, string nickname) = load.Type switch
        {
            LoadType.Occupancy => ("Occupancy", "Occ"),
            LoadType.Lighting => ("Lighting", "Lt"),
            LoadType.ElectricEquipment => ("Equipment", "Eq"),
            LoadType.GasEquipment => ("Gas Equipment", "Gas"),
            LoadType.DomesticHotWater => ("Hot Water", "DHW"),
            LoadType.Ventilation => ("Ventilation", "Vent"),
            _ => ("Infiltration", "Inf"),
        };
        return loads.Count(l => l.Type == load.Type) > 1
            ? ($"{label} {load.Basis}", nickname + string.Concat(load.Basis.ToString().Where(char.IsUpper)))
            : (label, nickname);
    }

    /// <summary>Unit of a load value: the magnitude unit of its type (people, W, m³/h) per its basis (ADR-007).</summary>
    private static string Unit(LoadType type, LoadBasis basis)
    {
        string magnitude = type switch
        {
            LoadType.Occupancy => "people",
            LoadType.Lighting or LoadType.ElectricEquipment or LoadType.GasEquipment => "W",
            _ => "m³/h",
        };
        return basis switch
        {
            LoadBasis.PerFloorArea => $"{magnitude}/m²",
            LoadBasis.PerPerson => $"{magnitude}/person",
            LoadBasis.PerExteriorWallArea => $"{magnitude}/m² of exterior wall",
            LoadBasis.AirChangesPerHour => "1/h (air changes per hour)",
            _ => magnitude,
        };
    }
}

   with

Rooms or tenancies as zones would need a room layout the precedents do not give and would break the parity with housing, where a dwelling is never subdivided (D-009). One zone per department is the most detailed level; the simplifiers and aggregators coarsen it as for housing.

### A preset without a space type (S8.8, D-108)

D-108 asks for a preset that every program input of every plan generator accepts, made from any preset, built-in or general, while a preset that keeps its space type must still match its input.

1. **A `SpaceType` value `Any`.** One enum value, but `SpaceType` is the semantic category of a zone, and no zone would ever have this one; every switch over space types (preview colours, *Semantic Merge* grouping, *Convert2BEM*) would have to handle a value that never occurs on a zone, and `Other` and `Mixed` already mean something else.
2. **A flag beside a kept space type.** The preset would keep, say, `Kitchen` and say that it fits every input: two sources of truth, and a preset that reads `Kitchen` while it programs a dwelling.
3. **No space type (chosen).** `ProgramPreset.SpaceType` becomes `SpaceType?`, `null` meaning that the preset has no space type; `AsAnyProgram()` returns the preset without it. The absence is explicit data, nothing is inferred from a name (D-061), and the slot check needs one condition more.
4. **Matching by name or by a list of compatible types.** Names are descriptive only (D-061), and a compatibility table would be a research rule that no source supports.
```

3. Replace

   with

The existing label and unit mapping of `DefaultPresetComponent` already covers every load above (*Gas Equipment* in W/m², *Ventilation* and *Infiltration* in 1/h); no component is special-cased.
```

```

   with

```markdown
`SYN-TYP-013` has no generator (D-096). The `001`–`009` families are unchanged (ADR-006).

### Any-program presets (S8.8, D-108)

- **Model.** `ProgramPreset.SpaceType` is `SpaceType?`; `IsAnyProgram` is `true` when it is `null`. A preset is always created with a space type (`ProgramPreset.Create`, `PresetValues.ToPreset`, every default preset component, *Program Preset*); `AsAnyProgram()` returns a copy without it that keeps everything else: the name, the same `ZoneProgram` (loads, their schedules, conditioning, setpoints), and the WWR. Casting an any-program preset again returns it unchanged.
- **Plan generators.** The slot check of every generator, single-plan and multi-storey (`PlanBuilder`), accepts an any-program preset on any slot; a preset with a space type must still have its slot's (`PresetSpaceTypeMismatch`, unchanged). A zone keeps the space type the generator's layout gives it (`PlannedZone.SpaceType`) and takes its program and the WWR of its windows from its slot's preset, as for a typed preset.
- **Provenance (GLOBAL.md scientific rule 3).** The plan's `Presets` parameter lists `{slot space type}:{preset name}` per slot, which for a typed preset is its own space type, as before. For each slot that received an any-program preset the plan also records `AnyProgramPreset.{Input}` = the preset's name (`PlanPresets.AnyProgramPresetKeyPrefix`), for example `AnyProgramPreset.Corridor=Example Office`; every floor and building made from the plan carries it in its provenance inputs. A plan of typed presets records no such key, so its output is unchanged.
- **Downstream.** Zones carry their space type and program, never their preset, so simplifiers, aggregators, validation, the mapping, *Inspect*, and *Convert2BEM* are unchanged: a dwelling zone with an office program is a `DwellingUnit` zone with that program, it is coloured and grouped by *Semantic Merge* as a dwelling, and *Convert2BEM* reports its space type `DwellingUnit`. Integration tests run a linear plan and a stepped band with any-program presets through every simplifier and aggregator, and every result validates.
- **Grasshopper.** The parameter *Any Program Preset* (AnyP, `AnyProgramPresetParameter`, `24bc6a12-ad06-4c25-bc9d-4e8318b8744c`) in panel *1 Program* is a floating data node like Grasshopper's *Geometry* parameter: wire any preset into it (a default preset component, *Program Preset*, or a *Program Preset* parameter), and it outputs the any-program cast (`AnyProgramPresetGoo`, shown as `Preset <name> (any program, no space type, WWR <ratio>)`). It holds no data of its own. Every preset input of every generator, a *Program Preset* parameter, takes it; the input's description says so.

## Consequences
```

```

- **Allow it (chosen).** A multiplier repeats its storey, as for any floor: entries `storey 0 × 2, storey 1 × 1, storey 2 × 2` are a band that steps back above its second and third storeys. The building differs from the generator's (it has more storeys, and its terraces lie higher), so the generator's building is the one with every multiplier 1, which is what the Grasshopper wiring gives by default. Exposure is never multiplied (D-046): with `StackedFloorZoneMultiplier` every storey with a terrace is split off as × 1. `TerraceTests` covers this case with every aggregator.

   with

```markdown
- **Allow it (chosen).** A multiplier repeats its storey, as for any floor: entries `storey 0 × 2, storey 1 × 1, storey 2 × 2` are a band that steps back above its second and third storeys. The building differs from the generator's (it has more storeys, and its terraces lie higher), so the generator's building is the one with every multiplier 1, which is what the Grasshopper wiring gives by default. Exposure is never multiplied (D-046): with `StackedFloorZoneMultiplier` every storey with a terrace is split off as × 1. `TerraceTests` covers this case with every aggregator.

### Where the preview finds a storey's height (S8.8, D-108)

Through S8.7 the generator component raised each storey's preview by the floor heights below it, but a simplifier's floor of that storey is previewed at height 0, so the plans and the floors of one band were drawn at different heights.

- **Raise the floors in the simplifier components.** A simplifier component does not know which storey a plan is; the information has to travel with the data.
- **A dedicated field on `IGeneratedPlan` and `IFloor`.** Explicit, but a pipeline type change for a value that only the previews use: `GeneratedPlan`, `PlanTransform`, and every simplifier would carry it, and the data must stay at elevation 0.
- **The storey index times the floor height.** `Storey` is already recorded, and every storey has the parameters' floor height, but it would tie the previews to equal floor heights.
- **The base elevation in provenance (chosen).** The storey's plan records its base elevation, which provenance already carries through *Transform Plan* and every simplifier as their one input.

## Decision
```

```

   with

```markdown
- **Provenance:** the operation is the generator's `Name`; the parameters are the family's (`Describe`), `FloorHeight`, `OrientationDegrees`, `Presets` as for every plan, non-default limits (ADR-011), `Storey`, the storey index from 0 at the bottom, and `StoreyElevation` (S8.8, D-108), the storey's base elevation in metres, the sum of the floor heights below it (`StoreyProvenance.ElevationKey`).

Simplifier components preview their floors at elevation 0, so the floors of a band overlap there until they are aggregated.

   with

```markdown
- **Preview:** each storey is previewed at its storey's elevation (the sum of the floor heights below it, in model units) plus the *Preview Location*, so the generator's preview shows the stepped band; the plans' data stay at elevation 0. Since S8.8 (D-108) the height comes from the data: `StoreyProvenance.ElevationOf` reads `StoreyElevation` from an object's provenance, following an operation of exactly one input (*Transform Plan*, a plan simplifier) to its input, and is 0 for anything else, a building included. The plan and floor previews (`PlanGoo`, `FloorGoo`) are raised by it, so the generator, *Transform Plan*, and every simplifier draw a storey at the same height, each plus its own *Preview Location*. Plans and floors that are not storeys stay at 0, and buildings, which stack their storeys in their data, are unchanged. Data, Breps, and *Convert2BEM* outputs are unchanged.
```

At Checkpoint 1 (stages S0–S4, D-030) the pipeline is complete for the linear plan; the revision stage S4.1 (D-061 to D-064) gave the plan generator one preset input per space type and added default preset components, a *Preview Location* input, and component icons; the revision stage S4.2 (D-068 to D-075, [ADR-013](../decisions/ADR-013-vertical-aggregation-methods.md)) added *Transform Plan*, made the floor aggregators designate floors and ceilings on the assembled stack, turned them into four methods, and made heat transfer between zones a conversion option; stage S5 (D-079, [ADR-012](../decisions/ADR-012-centred-windows.md)) gives every rebuilt outdoor wall one centred window with the glazed area it covers; stage S8.1 (D-082 to D-084, [ADR-006](../decisions/ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md), [ADR-011](../decisions/ADR-011-minimum-wall-and-window-geometry.md)) adds a plan generator for each bar family of the *High-Density Housing* precedent study and the minimum wall and window geometry of every generator; stage S8.2 (D-084) adds the non-rectangular families without holes, the point plate, the winged band, and the open court, and makes the united zones and the edge tests of the surface builder exact on rotated plans; stage S8.3 (D-084) adds the enclosed court, the first footprint with a hole, which every simplifier and aggregator supports; stage S8.4 (D-093 to D-098, [ADR-014](../decisions/ADR-014-program-types-and-department-zoning.md)) adds the non-residential space types with their presets and default preset components, and the office plate and the cafeteria of the four-source precedent set; stage S8.5 (D-098, D-100) adds the four families that branch from a hub on the shared hub-and-arms layout, the radial lobes, the branching mall, the care hub, and the foyer with halls, and makes the perimeter zones of *Perimeter Core* keep the footprint's own vertices; stage S8.6 (D-098) adds the operating suite with separated clean and dirty routes and the court cluster, the first footprint with several holes; stage S8.7 (D-095, D-098, [ADR-015](../decisions/ADR-015-plans-per-storey.md)) adds generators that return one plan per storey and the stepped band, whose lower roofs become terraces. This document describes each step, the rules it applies, where results are validated, and the Grasshopper components that expose it. The domain types are described in [domain-model.md](domain-model.md), the validation checks in [validation.md](validation.md), and the `Convert2BEM` output layout in [convert2bem.md](convert2bem.md).

stage S8.3 (D-084) adds the enclosed court, the first footprint with a hole, which every simplifier and aggregator supports; stage S8.4 (D-093 to D-098, [ADR-014](../decisions/ADR-014-program-types-and-department-zoning.md)) adds the non-residential space types with their presets and default preset components, and the office plate and the cafeteria of the four-source precedent set; stage S8.5 (D-098, D-100) adds the four families that branch from a hub on the shared hub-and-arms layout, the radial lobes, the branching mall, the care hub, and the foyer with halls, and makes the perimeter zones of *Perimeter Core* keep the footprint's own vertices; stage S8.6 (D-098) adds the operating suite with separated clean and dirty routes and the court cluster, the first footprint with several holes; stage S8.7 (D-095, D-098, [ADR-015](../decisions/ADR-015-plans-per-storey.md)) adds generators that return one plan per storey and the stepped band, whose lower roofs become terraces; the revision stage S8.8 (D-108) previews a storey's floors at the storey's height, as its plan, adds the *Any Program Preset* parameter, which casts any preset to one without a space type that every preset input takes, and moves the built-in preset components to a panel of their own. This document describes each step, the rules it applies, where results are validated, and the Grasshopper components that expose it. The domain types are described in [domain-model.md](domain-model.md), the validation checks in [validation.md](validation.md), and the `Convert2BEM` output layout in [convert2bem.md](convert2bem.md).
```

2. Replace

```markdown
- All logic lives in `Lod.Core` and `Lod.Generators`, which reference neither Rhino nor Grasshopper; their tests run with `dotnet test`. `Lod.Grasshopper` wraps each pipeline type in a Goo with a viewport preview and exposes one component per step (D-017, D-018). Every component that previews a plan, floor, or building has a last input *Preview Location* that moves only its preview (D-062, [below](#grasshopper-components-through-s87)).
```

Every component that previews a plan, floor, or building has a last input *Preview Location* that moves only its preview (D-062, [below](#grasshopper-components-through-s88)).

4. Replace

```markdown
- A program preset holds the zone program of one space type and the window-to-wall ratio in [0, 1) that the plan generator uses (D-024, D-026). Its name is descriptive only: it appears in reports, provenance, and messages and is never used for matching (D-061).
```

   with

```markdown
- A program preset holds the zone program of one space type and the window-to-wall ratio in [0, 1) that the plan generator uses (D-024, D-026). Its name is descriptive only: it appears in reports, provenance, and messages and is never used for matching (D-061).
- An any-program preset (S8.8, D-108, [ADR-014](../decisions/ADR-014-program-types-and-department-zoning.md)) has no space type: `ProgramPreset.SpaceType` is `null` and `IsAnyProgram` is `true`. Every preset is created with a space type; `AsAnyProgram()` returns it without one and keeps its name, program (loads, schedules, conditioning, setpoints), and WWR.
```

5. Replace

```markdown
- Presets (D-061): a generator takes a typed preset record with one preset per space type it places, a `PlanPresets` whose `Slots` list its inputs as `PresetSlot(Input, SpaceType, Preset)`. For the linear block this is `LinearPlanPresets(DwellingUnit, Corridor, Stair)`. Before the layout is built every slot is checked: a preset of another space type is the error `PresetSpaceTypeMismatch` (one per wrong slot, naming the input, both space types, and the preset), a missing preset the error `MissingPreset`. Each zone gets the preset of the slot of its space type; provenance records `Presets=DwellingUnit:<name>;Corridor:<name>;Stair:<name>` in slot order.
```

   with

```markdown
- Presets (D-061): a generator takes a typed preset record with one preset per space type it places, a `PlanPresets` whose `Slots` list its inputs as `PresetSlot(Input, SpaceType, Preset)`. For the linear block this is `LinearPlanPresets(DwellingUnit, Corridor, Stair)`. Before the layout is built every slot is checked: a preset of another space type is the error `PresetSpaceTypeMismatch` (one per wrong slot, naming the input, both space types, and the preset), a missing preset the error `MissingPreset`. Each zone gets the preset of the slot of its space type; provenance records `Presets=DwellingUnit:<name>;Corridor:<name>;Stair:<name>` in slot order, with each slot's space type. An any-program preset (S8.8, D-108) is accepted on every slot: the zones keep the space type of the layout and take the preset's program and WWR, and the plan records `AnyProgramPreset.<Input>=<name>` for each such slot (for example `AnyProgramPreset.Corridor=Example Office`), so the change of program is never silent (GLOBAL.md scientific rule 3). A plan of typed presets records no such parameter and is unchanged.
```

6. Replace

```markdown
- Plans per storey (S8.7, D-095, [ADR-015](../decisions/ADR-015-plans-per-storey.md)): a `MultiStoreyPlanGenerator` returns one ordinary plan per storey, bottom-up, for a family whose plan differs by storey. It checks the parameters and presets once and builds every storey as `PlanGenerator` builds its plan; each plan lies at elevation 0, has the parameters' floor height and orientation, and records its storey index as the provenance parameter `Storey` (from 0). A diagnostic of one storey keeps its code and subject, and its message starts with `Storey k: `.
```

   with

```markdown
- Plans per storey (S8.7, D-095, [ADR-015](../decisions/ADR-015-plans-per-storey.md)): a `MultiStoreyPlanGenerator` returns one ordinary plan per storey, bottom-up, for a family whose plan differs by storey. It checks the parameters and presets once and builds every storey as `PlanGenerator` builds its plan; each plan lies at elevation 0, has the parameters' floor height and orientation, and records its storey index as the provenance parameter `Storey` (from 0) and its base elevation, the sum of the floor heights below it, as `StoreyElevation` (m, S8.8, D-108), which only the previews read (`StoreyProvenance`). A diagnostic of one storey keeps its code and subject, and its message starts with `Storey k: `.
```

A wall or window below the ADR-011 minimum gets no window (`WindowOmitted`) | Error; no plan (`WindowOmitted` is a remark; the plan is produced) |

9. Replace

```markdown
- **Preview Location (D-062).** The eighteen plan generators, *Transform Plan*, the four plan simplifiers, and the four floor aggregators have an optional last input *Preview Location* (PL): a vector in model units, default `0,0,0` (a point converts to a vector). It moves only that component's viewport preview: the plan, floor, or building data, *Inspect*, *Validate*, and *Convert2BEM* keep the true coordinates, and downstream components do not inherit the move (each wraps its output with its own input). *Convert2BEM* has no such input.
- **Default preset components (D-061).** *Dwelling Unit Preset*, *Corridor Preset*, and *Stair Preset* share their inputs: Name (default the example name), WWR, Conditioned, Heating (°C), Cooling (°C), then for each load of the space type's example preset, in its order, a value input (its unit in the description: people/m², W/m², or 1/h) and an optional schedule input that replaces the built-in schedule when connected. Every input except the schedules has a default; the output is one *Preset*.
```

   with

```markdown
- **Preview Location (D-062).** The eighteen plan generators, *Transform Plan*, the four plan simplifiers, and the four floor aggregators have an optional last input *Preview Location* (PL): a vector in model units, default `0,0,0` (a point converts to a vector). It moves only that component's viewport preview: the plan, floor, or building data, *Inspect*, *Validate*, and *Convert2BEM* keep the true coordinates, and downstream components do not inherit the move (each wraps its output with its own input). *Convert2BEM* has no such input. A plan or floor that is a storey of a multi-storey generator, or is made from one alone through *Transform Plan* or a simplifier, is previewed at the storey's elevation (`StoreyElevation`, S8.8, D-108) plus the *Preview Location*; every other plan and floor, and every building, is previewed at its own coordinates plus the *Preview Location*.
- **Default preset components (D-061).** *Dwelling Unit Preset*, *Corridor Preset*, and *Stair Preset*, in panel *1 Program Presets* since S8.8 (D-108) like every built-in preset component, share their inputs: Name (default the example name), WWR, Conditioned, Heating (°C), Cooling (°C), then for each load of the space type's example preset, in its order, a value input (its unit in the description: people/m², W/m², or 1/h) and an optional schedule input that replaces the built-in schedule when connected. Every input except the schedules has a default; the output is one *Preset*.
```

10. Replace

```markdown
- **Wiring storeys that differ (S8.7, ADR-015).** Connect the *Stepped Band*'s *Plan* list to a plan simplifier (optionally through *Transform Plan*); Grasshopper runs it once per storey, and its *Floor* output is the list of floors in the same order. Connect that list to a floor aggregator's *Floors* and leave *Multipliers* unconnected: every storey counts once, bottom to top. The simplifiers preview their floors at elevation 0, so the storeys overlap there until they are aggregated. A *Single Zone Building* or *Single Zone per Floor Type* zone whose storey outlines differ is previewed by a boolean union of its storeys, as for setbacks (`RhinoGeometry.MergedZoneBreps`).
- **Default preset components of the non-residential types (S8.4, ADR-014).** *Office Preset* to *Service Preset* share the inputs of the residential ones (above), one value and one optional schedule input per load of their example preset; *Gas Equipment* is in W/m², *Ventilation* and *Infiltration* in 1/h. Zones of the new types have their own preview colours.
```

    with

```markdown
- **Wiring storeys that differ (S8.7, ADR-015).** Connect the *Stepped Band*'s *Plan* list to a plan simplifier (optionally through *Transform Plan*); Grasshopper runs it once per storey, and its *Floor* output is the list of floors in the same order. Connect that list to a floor aggregator's *Floors* and leave *Multipliers* unconnected: every storey counts once, bottom to top. *Transform Plan* and every simplifier preview each storey's plan or floor at the storey's elevation, as the generator does (S8.8, D-108; through S8.7 they previewed it at 0), each plus its own *Preview Location*; the data stay at elevation 0, and the aggregator places the storeys. A *Single Zone Building* or *Single Zone per Floor Type* zone whose storey outlines differ is previewed by a boolean union of its storeys, as for setbacks (`RhinoGeometry.MergedZoneBreps`).
- **Default preset components of the non-residential types (S8.4, ADR-014).** *Office Preset* to *Service Preset* share the inputs of the residential ones (above), one value and one optional schedule input per load of their example preset; *Gas Equipment* is in W/m², *Ventilation* and *Infiltration* in 1/h. Zones of the new types have their own preview colours.
- **Any Program Preset (S8.8, D-108).** A floating parameter in panel *1 Program*, like Grasshopper's *Geometry* parameter: place it on the canvas, wire a preset into its left grip (any default preset component, *Program Preset*, or another preset parameter), and wire its right grip into any preset input of any plan generator. It outputs each preset as an any-program preset (`AnyProgramPresetGoo`, shown as `Preset <name> (any program, no space type, WWR <ratio>)`), with the same name, program, and WWR; it holds no data of its own, so an unconnected one is empty. The generator's preset inputs take it (`ProgramPresetParameter` converts it), and their descriptions say so in one shared text. The zones keep the input's space type (preview colour, *Semantic Merge* groups, *Convert2BEM* *Space Types*) and take the preset's program; *Inspect*'s provenance shows `AnyProgramPreset.<Input>`. A preset wired directly, without the parameter, must still have the input's space type.
- **Panels (S8.8, D-108).** *1 Program* holds *Schedule*, *Load*, *Program Preset*, and *Any Program Preset*; the eighteen built-in preset components are in *1 Program Presets*, next to it. Their icons keep the *Program* accent. No GUID changed.
```

   with

```markdown
A plan generator takes one preset per space type it places, as a typed record (D-061): the linear plan generator takes `LinearPlanPresets(DwellingUnit, Corridor, Stair)`, and in Grasshopper the *Linear Plan Generator* has the three required inputs *Dwelling Unit*, *Corridor*, and *Stair*. A preset whose space type differs from its input's is an error (`PresetSpaceTypeMismatch`); each zone gets the preset of its space type. An any-program preset (S8.8, D-108) has no space type (`SpaceType` is `null`): `preset.AsAnyProgram()` returns any preset without its space type and with every other value unchanged, and every input of every generator accepts it; the zones keep the input's space type, take the preset's program and WWR, and the plan's provenance records `AnyProgramPreset.<Input>=<name>`. (Until S4.1 presets were passed as a `ProgramPresetSet`, a list with at most one preset per space type.)
```

   with

```markdown
S4.1 adds one default preset component per space type of the linear plan, *Dwelling Unit Preset*, *Corridor Preset*, and *Stair Preset*, and deletes *Example Residential Presets* (D-061). Every input of a default preset component has a default, the values of §2, so the component works with nothing connected: Name, WWR, Conditioned, Heating (°C), Cooling (°C), and per load a value input (people/m², W/m², or 1/h, as in its description) and an optional schedule input that replaces the built-in schedule when connected. Each shows the remark "Illustrative values; not sourced from DOE prototypes or standards." S8.4 adds one default preset component per non-residential space type of §2.2, *Office Preset*, *Core Preset*, *Retail Preset*, *Mall Preset*, *Kitchen Preset*, *Dining Preset*, *Operating Theatre Preset*, *Clean Corridor Preset*, *Dirty Corridor Preset*, *Clinical Support Preset*, *Care Bedroom Preset*, *Care Communal Preset*, *Lobby Preset*, *Activity Hall Preset*, and *Service Preset*, with the same inputs; *Gas Equipment* is in W/m², and *Ventilation* and *Infiltration* are in 1/h. Their inputs and outputs are listed in [pipeline.md](../architecture/pipeline.md#grasshopper-components-through-s88).

S8.8 moves the eighteen default preset components to their own panel, *1 Program Presets*; *Schedule*, *Load*, and *Program Preset* stay in *1 Program*, with the new parameter *Any Program Preset* (AnyP, D-108). Wire any preset into *Any Program Preset* and its output into any preset input of any generator to apply that program to the input's space type: for example an *Office Preset* through *Any Program Preset* into the *Corridor* input of the *Linear Plan Generator* gives the corridor the office program while it stays a `Corridor` zone. The preset keeps its name, loads, schedules, conditioning, setpoints, and WWR, and the plan's provenance records the use.

- [x] **Step 5: Check the code fences of the edited documents**

Check the code fences of the documents of Tasks 8 and 9: every opening fence line has a language, every closing one is bare, and no file ends inside a block.

```bash
python - <<'EOF'
from pathlib import Path

fence = "`" * 3
names = [
    "docs/architecture/domain-model.md",
    "docs/architecture/pipeline.md",
    "docs/decisions/ADR-014-program-types-and-department-zoning.md",
    "docs/decisions/ADR-015-plans-per-storey.md",
    "docs/development/grasshopper-smoke-test.md",
    "docs/research/program-presets.md",
]
for name in names:
    lines = Path(name).read_text(encoding="utf-8").split("\n")
    inside, blocks, problems = False, 0, []
    for number, line in enumerate(lines, 1):
        mark = line.strip()
        if not mark.startswith(fence):
            continue
        if not inside and mark == fence:
            problems.append(f"line {number} opens a block without a language")
        if inside and mark != fence:
            problems.append(f"line {number} opens a block inside a block")
        blocks += inside
        inside = not inside
    if inside:
        problems.append("the file ends inside a block")
    print(f"{name}: {blocks} code blocks, " + ("; ".join(problems) or "fences balanced"))
EOF
```

docs/architecture/pipeline.md: 1 code blocks, fences balanced
docs/decisions/ADR-014-program-types-and-department-zoning.md: 0 code blocks, fences balanced
docs/decisions/ADR-015-plans-per-storey.md: 0 code blocks, fences balanced
docs/development/grasshopper-smoke-test.md: 2 code blocks, fences balanced
docs/research/program-presets.md: 1 code blocks, fences balanced
```

If a line reports anything else, stop: find the stray or missing fence line, fix the file, and run the check again.

```markdown
### D-109 — How the storey previews, the any-program preset, and the preset panel are built

- **Date:** <date> · **Decided by:** controller (D-108) · **Status:** Provisional
- Why: D-108 fixed the three requests and left their realisation to the controller; [ADR-014](ADR-014-program-types-and-department-zoning.md) and [ADR-015](ADR-015-plans-per-storey.md) record the options.
- **Any-program preset.** `ProgramPreset.SpaceType` is nullable; `AsAnyProgram()` returns the preset without a space type and with its name, program, and WWR unchanged; presets are still created typed. The slot check of every generator accepts an any-program preset on every slot, a typed preset must still match (`PresetSpaceTypeMismatch`); the zones keep the layout's space type. `Presets` records each slot's space type with the preset name, and each slot given an any-program preset adds `AnyProgramPreset.<Input>=<name>`; plans of typed presets are unchanged. Rejected: a `SpaceType.Any` value, a flag beside a kept type, matching by name.
- **Grasshopper.** The parameter *Any Program Preset* (AnyP, `AnyProgramPresetParameter`, `24bc6a12-ad06-4c25-bc9d-4e8318b8744c`) in *1 Program* casts what is wired into it through its `PreferredCast` (a preset goo, a plain preset, or a plain preset wrapped by Grasshopper, as a script gives it); the generators' preset inputs (`ProgramPresetParameter`) take its goo through theirs; one shared sentence in every preset input's description. The eighteen built-in preset components move to `1 Program Presets` and keep the Program accent.
- **Storey previews.** Storey plans record `StoreyElevation` (the floor heights below, m), one more provenance key on every plan of a `MultiStoreyPlanGenerator` such as the stepped band; `StoreyProvenance.ElevationOf` follows operations of one input, so plans, moved plans, and floors of a storey preview at its height; buildings and other plans and floors at 0. Rejected: a field on the pipeline types, `Storey × FloorHeight`.
```

```markdown
### D-110 — S8.8 complete

- **Date:** <date> · **Decided by:** controller (D-108) · **Status:** Provisional
- Revision stage S8.8 (D-108) is complete and tagged `v0.8.7` locally; pushing waits for the owner (D-108). Implemented as D-109 decides: the any-program preset ([ADR-014](ADR-014-program-types-and-department-zoning.md)), the parameter *Any Program Preset* (AnyP, `24bc6a12-ad06-4c25-bc9d-4e8318b8744c`, with icon) in *1 Program*, the panel *1 Program Presets* with the eighteen built-in preset components, and storey previews at `StoreyElevation` for plans, moved plans, and floors ([ADR-015](ADR-015-plans-per-storey.md)). No GUID changed; data, Breps, and *Convert2BEM* outputs unchanged.
- The twenty-five existing snapshots and sixty existing icons are byte-identical. 243 core, 518 generator, and 1518 integration tests pass on `net8.0`, including any-program presets on every input of all 18 generators and the linear plan and stepped band with any-program presets through every simplifier and aggregator, each validating.
- Smoke test (D-056): the controller's scripted headless Grasshopper session on the verified reference (`d9c3059`, whose `src/`, `tests/`, and `scripts/` equal the executed branch) in Rhino 8.25.25314.11001 (.NET 8.0.31) passed every performed item: *Any Program Preset* (AnyP, primary, 24 × 24 icon) in *1 Program* with *Program Preset*, *Load*, and *Schedule*, and the eighteen built-in presets in *1 Program Presets* with unchanged GUIDs; the cast of *Office Preset* shown as `Preset Example Office (any program, no space type, WWR 0.4)`; AnyP(Office) on the *Linear Plan Generator*'s *Corridor* input with no runtime message, `AnyProgramPreset.Corridor=Example Office` in the provenance, and *Validate* passing, while *Office Preset* wired directly still gives `PresetSpaceTypeMismatch`; AnyP(Kitchen) on both inputs of *Office Plate*; AnyP(Care Bedroom) on all three inputs of *Stepped Band* with all 16 simplifier × aggregator combinations validating; and the storeys of the default stepped band drawn at 0–3, 3–6, and 6–9 m by the generator and every simplifier, also through *Transform Plan* and with a simplifier's *Preview Location*, while the linear plan and its floors stay at 0–3 m. It is recorded in `docs/development/grasshopper-smoke-test.md` and the close-out commit; the viewport look, the toolbar panels and icons, a non-preset value wired into AnyP, and example definitions stay open for a person (D-058).

- [x] **Step 10: Check the code fences of every document of the stage**

Check the code fences of every Markdown file this stage touches, this plan included, and the entries and sections appended to the decision log and the smoke-test guide: every opening fence line has a language, every closing one is bare, and no file ends inside a block.

```bash
python - <<'EOF'
import re
from pathlib import Path

fence = "`" * 3
names = [
    "AGENTS.md",
    "README.md",
    "docs/architecture/domain-model.md",
    "docs/architecture/pipeline.md",
    "docs/decisions/ADR-014-program-types-and-department-zoning.md",
    "docs/decisions/ADR-015-plans-per-storey.md",
    "docs/decisions/decision-log.md",
    "docs/development/grasshopper-smoke-test.md",
    "docs/plans/2026-09-30-implementation-roadmap.md",
    "docs/plans/2026-10-02-s8.8-previews-any-preset-panel.md",
    "docs/research/program-presets.md",
]
for name in names:
    lines = Path(name).read_text(encoding="utf-8").split("\n")
    inside, blocks, problems = False, 0, []
    for number, line in enumerate(lines, 1):
        mark = line.strip()
        if not mark.startswith(fence):
            continue
        if not inside and mark == fence:
            problems.append(f"line {number} opens a block without a language")
        if inside and mark != fence:
            problems.append(f"line {number} opens a block inside a block")
        blocks += inside
        inside = not inside
    if inside:
        problems.append("the file ends inside a block")
    print(f"{name}: {blocks} code blocks, " + ("; ".join(problems) or "fences balanced"))
log = Path("docs/decisions/decision-log.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
guide = Path("docs/development/grasshopper-smoke-test.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
entries = [part for part in log.split("\n### ")[1:] if part.startswith(("D-108", "D-109", "D-110"))]
sections = [part for part in re.split(r"\n(?=#{3,4} )", guide) if re.match(r"#{3,4} .*S8\.[78]", part)]
for kind, parts in (("decision-log entries D-108 to D-110", entries), ("smoke-test sections of S8.7 and S8.8", sections)):
    ending = [part.split("\n")[0] for part in parts if part.rstrip().split("\n")[-1].strip().startswith(fence)]
    print(f"{len(parts)} {kind}, ending with a fence line: " + (", ".join(ending) or "none"))
EOF
```

docs/decisions/ADR-014-program-types-and-department-zoning.md: 0 code blocks, fences balanced
docs/decisions/ADR-015-plans-per-storey.md: 0 code blocks, fences balanced
docs/decisions/decision-log.md: 1 code blocks, fences balanced
