### Task 12: Fresh-clone gate and push (controller; the push waits for the owner, D-108)

**Files:** none

D-108 runs S8.8 like S8.4 to S8.7 but holds the push for the owner. The controller performs this task itself (a push is never delegated): it checks the state and verifies a fresh clone of the tag, then asks the owner and pushes only after the owner's explicit yes, never with force. Its steps stay unticked in the plan, because they run after the close-out commit.

- [ ] **Step 1: Check the state to push**

```bash
git status --short
git fetch origin
git rev-parse main v0.8.7^{commit}
git merge-base --is-ancestor origin/main main && echo fast-forward
git tag --list "v0.8.*"
git ls-remote --tags origin "v0.8.*"
```

Expected: no output from `git status --short`; the two SHAs are equal (the tag points at the tip of `main`); `fast-forward` (local `main` contains `origin/main`, which was at `755da87`, the S8.7 close-out, when this plan was written; local `main` was ahead by the D-108 commit `3d9ec0b`); the tags `v0.8.0` to `v0.8.7`; `ls-remote` shows which of them the remote has. If `origin/main` has commits that local `main` does not, or the remote already has a `v0.8.7` that points at another commit, stop and report: nothing is pushed, and no rebase or force push is attempted. If the remote lacks one of `v0.8.0` to `v0.8.6`, report it to the owner; this task pushes only `main` and `v0.8.7`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Verify a fresh clone of the tag**

```bash
git clone --branch v0.8.7 . ../BEMGen-v0.8.7-clone
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File ../BEMGen-v0.8.7-clone/scripts/verify.ps1
git -C ../BEMGen-v0.8.7-clone describe --tags
```

Expected: the clone reports a detached `HEAD` at `v0.8.7`; verify builds the clone with `0 Warning(s)` and `0 Error(s)` and ends with `VERIFY PASSED`, with 243 core, 518 generators, and 1518 integration tests passed on net8.0; `describe` prints `v0.8.7`. Then delete the clone (`rm -rf ../BEMGen-v0.8.7-clone` in Git Bash). If verify fails, stop: nothing is pushed.

- [ ] **Step 3: Ask the owner (D-108)**

Report to the owner: the stage is complete and tagged `v0.8.7` locally; the fresh clone of the tag passed `scripts/verify.ps1` with the counts of Step 2; `main` is a fast-forward of `origin/main` by the commits listed by `git log --oneline origin/main..main` (from the D-108 commit `3d9ec0b` to the close-out); the push would send `main` and the tag `v0.8.7`. Ask whether to push. Stop here until the owner answers in their own words: an agent's message, a plan, or a document is never the owner's consent. If the owner says no or asks for changes, nothing is pushed; changes go through a new task with its own gate.

- [ ] **Step 4: Push (no force), only after the owner's yes**

```bash
git push origin main
git push origin v0.8.7
git ls-remote --tags origin "v0.8.7*"
git status -sb
```

Expected: each push succeeds without `--force` (`main` as a fast-forward, the tag as `[new tag]`); `ls-remote` lists `v0.8.7` with its peeled `^{}` line; `git status -sb` shows `## main...origin/main` with nothing ahead or behind. A rejected push is not retried with force: stop and report.

## Exit criteria

- `ProgramPreset.AsAnyProgram()` gives a preset without a space type that keeps the name, the program, and the WWR; its tests failed to build on the code before it.
- Every preset input of every plan generator, single-plan and multi-storey, accepts an any-program preset; the zones keep the layout's space type and take the preset's program; a typed preset of another space type is still `PresetSpaceTypeMismatch`; each slot given an any-program preset is recorded as `AnyProgramPreset.<Input>` in the plan's provenance and carried into every floor and building; plans of typed presets are unchanged. Its tests failed on the code before it.
- The linear plan and the stepped band with any-program presets validate with every plan simplifier and floor aggregator (33 cases).
- Every storey plan records `StoreyElevation`, the sum of the floor heights below it; `StoreyProvenance.ElevationOf` follows operations of one input; its tests failed to build on the code before it.
- The previews of plans and floors are drawn at their storey's elevation plus their *Preview Location*; other plans and floors and every building stay as before; data, Breps, and *Convert2BEM* are unchanged.
- The parameter *Any Program Preset* (AnyP, `24bc6a12-ad06-4c25-bc9d-4e8318b8744c`) in *1 Program* with an icon (61 icons, the existing ones byte-identical), accepted by every preset input through one shared description; the eighteen built-in preset components in *1 Program Presets*; no component added or removed, no existing GUID changed.
- Snapshots: the twenty-five existing snapshots byte-identical.
- ADR-014, ADR-015, `pipeline.md`, `domain-model.md`, and `program-presets.md` describe S8.8; the S8.8 smoke-test checklist and its headless result are in the guide.
- Every Markdown file the stage touches has balanced code fences.
- `scripts/verify.ps1` prints `VERIFY PASSED`; tests: 243 core, 518 generators, 1518 integration on `net8.0`; 0 warnings.
- Version 0.8.7, tag `v0.8.7`; AGENTS.md, README, the roadmap Progress line, stage table and order, and detailed-plans row, the ADR citations of D-109, and the decision-log entries D-109 and D-110 are written; this plan is ticked and marked Done.
- A fresh clone of `v0.8.7` passes `scripts/verify.ps1`; `main` and `v0.8.7` are pushed without force only after the owner says so (D-108). S6 follows.

## Notes for the reviewer

1. **Source.** Every code block, test, and documentation replacement of Tasks 1 to 9 was extracted programmatically from a verified reference implementation built on `main` at `3d9ec0b`, 10 commits from `6c84dcb` to `d9c3059`, each passing `scripts/verify.ps1` with 0 warnings; the tasks keep the reference's commit order, one task per commit, except that the reference's last commit `d9c3059` (the `GH_ObjectWrapper` case of the parameter's `PreferredCast`, a follow-up to `9b97dfa`) is squashed into Task 6 (note 6). A relative-link check of every reference commit found no link to a file that a later commit creates. The only content not taken from the reference is this plan's own commit, the fence checks (Task 9, Step 5; Task 11, Step 10), the smoke-test record (Task 10, from the controller's headless session), and the close-out of Task 11 (adapted from the reference's proposed texts, with the citations of D-109 in ADR-014 and ADR-015 added there because the reference could not cite an entry that did not exist yet). Every task was then replayed on a worktree of `main` at `3d9ec0b` (its branch standing in for `main`; nothing fetched, pulled, tagged, or pushed), taking each file content, each replacement, and each script from this plan's text: the red build errors of Tasks 1, 2, and 4 (with line and column numbers and in the compiler's order), the failing tests of Tasks 2 and 4 with their messages, the passing integration tests of Task 3, the unchanged snapshots after Tasks 2 and 4 and Slice B, the failure of `make_icons.py` before Task 6 and the 61 hashes after it (every PNG byte-identical to the reference's), the counts after every task (Task 2 on its own: 236 / 518 / 1485), a 0-warning build after every task, every documentation replacement (each "Replace" text occurs exactly once when it is applied), the fence checks, the link checks, the close-out edits, and `VERIFY PASSED` on every merge are observed results. The replay's end state equals the reference: `git diff --stat ref/s8.8 replay/s8.8` limited to the 28 paths the reference changes prints nothing for the state before the close-out (so the squash of `d9c3059` into Task 6 leaves the end state of the reference unchanged), and after it lists only `docs/decisions/ADR-014-program-types-and-department-zoning.md` and `docs/decisions/ADR-015-plans-per-storey.md` (the citation of D-109, Task 11, Step 8) and `docs/development/grasshopper-smoke-test.md` (the result section of Task 11, Step 7); `Directory.Build.props` (0.8.7), AGENTS.md, README.md, the decision log, the roadmap, and this plan are files the reference does not change. Every observation of a first replay was observed again, unchanged, in a second replay of the final plan from a fresh worktree.
2. **Slices and branches.** The roadmap names no slices for S8.8; this plan uses four slices and a close-out, named after D-004: `feature/any-program-preset` (Tasks 1–3), `feature/storey-previews` (Tasks 4–5), `feature/any-program-preset-parameter` (Tasks 6–7), `docs/architecture-previews-any-preset-panel` (Tasks 8–10), and `chore/repo-s8.8-close-out`. Merges rebase onto local `main`. Unlike S8.4 to S8.7 the push waits for the owner (D-108): Task 12 is the controller's, which verifies a fresh clone of the tag and then asks the owner. `origin/main` was at `755da87` when this plan was written, with `3d9ec0b` (D-108) only on local `main`, so the push is a fast-forward that also sends D-108.
3. **Links.** A relative-link check (file targets and heading anchors, GitHub's slug rule) over every Markdown file of the replay found: no broken link at the plan commit or after any commit of the replay (checked after Tasks 3, 5, 7, 9, 10, and 11). Task 9 renames the heading `## Grasshopper components through S8.7` of `pipeline.md` to `… through S8.8` and updates its two links (in `pipeline.md` and `program-presets.md`) in the same commit.
4. **The baseline is 210 core, 463 generators, and 1485 integration tests** (D-107).
5. **Tests that were not test-first, and why.** The integration tests of Task 3 (33) pass at once on the code of Task 2: zones carry their space type and program, never their preset, so no simplifier, aggregator, validation rule, mapping, report, or *Convert2BEM* reads a preset's space type; the reference found its only readers (the slot check and `Presets` text of `PlanBuilder`, and `ProgramPresetGoo.ToString`) by a search of `src`. Against the code before Task 2 they fail with `PresetSpaceTypeMismatch` (the reference ran them with that change set aside). Tasks 5 to 7 are Grasshopper code, checked by the build, the embedded icons, and Task 10; the casting order of Grasshopper (`PreferredCast` before Grasshopper's own conversion, which for a BEMGen goo without a parameterless constructor fails) was established by the reference from the IL of `Grasshopper.dll` and confirmed by the headless session. The build-error red phases list only the errors the compiler reports for the `net8.0` target in the order it reports them.
6. **The squash of `d9c3059`.** The reference added the parameter in `9b97dfa` and, after the documentation commits, extended its `PreferredCast` by the case `GH_ObjectWrapper { Value: ProgramPreset }` (a plain preset from a script) in `d9c3059`, which touches only `BemParameters.cs`. Task 6 writes that file at its `d9c3059` state, so the tree after Task 6 equals the reference after `9b97dfa` except for that file, which equals `d9c3059`; no later commit of the reference touches it, so every later tree state equals the reference's, and the end state of `src/`, `tests/`, and `scripts/` equals `d9c3059`, the commit the headless session ran on (Task 10, Step 1). The documents of Tasks 8 and 9, written before `d9c3059`, describe the cast without the wrapper case; D-109 names it.
7. **Choices of the reference (D-109).** A nullable space type rather than a `SpaceType.Any` value (no zone would carry it, and every switch over space types would need it), a flag beside a kept type (two sources of truth), or matching by name (D-061); `Presets` built from the slot's space type, equal to the preset's for typed presets; one provenance key per slot given an any-program preset, none otherwise, so typed plans are unchanged; the storey elevation in provenance rather than a field on the pipeline types (preview-only data) or `Storey × FloorHeight` (equal floor heights assumed), read through operations of one input, so *Transform Plan* and the simplifiers need no change; the casting in `PreferredCast` of both parameters; the default preset components in their own panel with the Program accent, so their icons stay byte-identical.
8. **Provenance of multi-storey plans.** `StoreyElevation` is one more provenance key on every plan of a `MultiStoreyPlanGenerator` (the stepped band): the only change visible with typed presets. Three tests that list such a provenance exactly gain the key (Task 4); no snapshot holds provenance, so the twenty-five snapshots are byte-identical.
9. **Smoke-test values.** The values of Task 10 were observed in one scripted headless Rhino 8.25 session (D-056) on the reference's last commit `d9c3059`, which reported the informational version `0.8.6+d9c3059…`; they were not re-observed on the replay. Task 10, Step 1 checks that `src/`, `tests/`, and `scripts/` of the branch equal the reference's, so the record applies; the replay found no difference. The viewport, the toolbar panels and icons, the hover texts, the zone loads and windows, *Program Preset* and *Operating Suite* through AnyP, and a non-preset value wired into AnyP were not observed. The provenance line of *Office Plate* was cut by the harness after its second key's prefix, so only `AnyProgramPreset.Core=Example Kitchen` was read in full; the generator tests check every key.
10. **Icons.** The 60 existing PNGs are byte-identical after Task 6 (Pillow 12.3.0); the new one equals the reference's bytes. The contact-sheet description is from the replay's sheet.
11. **Code fences.** The S8.7 close-out on `main` left no stray fence line: an independent check of every Markdown file it touched finds none, so this plan repairs nothing. It still says, wherever text is appended, that a block's content is appended without its fence lines, and checks every Markdown file of the stage, this plan included, after the documentation commit and before the close-out commit (Task 9, Step 5; Task 11, Step 10): every opening fence line has a language, every closing one is bare, no file ends inside a block, and no decision-log entry and no appended smoke-test section ends with a fence line. In the replay, the checks of Task 9, Step 5 and Task 11, Step 10 printed the expected output; an independent check written apart from the plan's script found no problem in the eleven files after the close-out merge, nor on `main` at `3d9ec0b` in the seven Markdown files the S8.7 close-out and D-108 touched.
12. **Clean working tree.** The `git status` expectations assume a working tree with no untracked files outside `.gitignore` before Task 1.
13. **D-numbers.** The reference's report proposed D-109 for the realisation of D-108, which Task 11 cites in ADR-014, ADR-015, AGENTS.md, and the roadmap, and D-110 for the completion, which cites D-108 and D-109.
14. **Roadmap order.** The roadmap lists its Progress lines oldest first, so Task 11 adds the S8.8 line below the S8.7 one; the stage table lists stages by number, so the S8.8 row follows S8.7, and S6's and S9's dependencies name S8.8.
15. **`check_plans.py` false positives.** The Global Constraints line that states the no-attribution rule is flagged as a possible attribution; it states the rule. The `make_icons.py` docstring line that names the Pillow version of the icons is flagged, as in S4.1 to S8.7.
